nakkhattaṃ paripucchitvā         brahmadattassa pāhiṇi .

Dadāhidāni me bhariyaṃ         nāriṃ sabbaṅgasobhaniṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 230 -->
<a id="page-230"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 230]*

Suvaṇṇena paṭicchannaṃ          dāsīgaṇapurakkhitaṃ .

[627] dadāmidāni te bhariyaṃ         nāriṃ sabbaṅgasobhaniṃ    
suvaṇṇena paṭicchannaṃ          dāsīgaṇapurakkhitaṃ .

[628] hatthī assā rathā patti       senā tiṭṭhanti cammikā    
ukkā padittā jhāyanti       kinnu maññanti paṇḍitā .

[629] hatthī assā rathā patti       senā tiṭṭhanti cammikā    
ukkā padittā jhāyanti       kinnu kāhanti paṇḍita .

[630] rakkhati taṃ mahārāja          cūḷaneyyo mahabbalo    
paduṭṭho te brahmadatto [^230-1]   pāto taṃ ghātayissati .

[631] ubbedhati me hadayaṃ          mukhañca parisussati    
nibbutiṃ nādhigacchāmi          aggidaḍhḍhova ātape .

Kammārānaṃ yathā ukkā       anto jhāyati no bahi    
evampi hadayaṃ mayhaṃ          anto jhāyati no bahi .

[632] pamatto mantanātīto         bhinnamantosi khattiya    
idāni kho taṃ tāyantu        paṇḍitā mantino janā .

Akatvāmaccassa vacanaṃ         atthakāmahitesino    
attapītirato rāja           migo kūṭeva ohito .

Yathāpi maccho balisaṃ          vaṅkaṃ maṃsena chāditaṃ    
āmagiddho na jānāti        maccho maraṇamattano .

Evameva tuvaṃ rāja          cūḷaneyyassa dhītaraṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 231 -->
<a id="page-231"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 231]*

Kāmagiddho na jānāsi        macchova maraṇamattano .

Sace gacchasi pañcālaṃ         khippamattaṃ jahissasi    
migaṃ panthānupannaṃva           mahantaṃ bhayamessasi .

Anariyarūpo puriso janinda    
ahīva uccaṅkagato ḍaseyya    
na tena mittiṃ kayirātha dhīro    
dukkho have kāpurisena saṅgamo .

Yaṃ tveva [^231-1] jaññā puriso [^231-2] janinda    
sīlavāyaṃ bahussuto    
teneva mittiṃ kayirātha dhīro    
sukho have sappurisehi saṅgamo .

[633] bālo tuvaṃ elamūgosi rāja    
yo uttamatthāni mayī lapittho    
kimevahaṃ naṅgalakoṭivaḍḍho    
atthāni jānissaṃ [^231-3] yathāpi aññe .

Imaṃ gale gahetvāna         nāsetha vijitā mama    
yo me ratanalābhassa         antarāyāya bhāsati .

[634] mahosadha atītena            nānuvijjhanti paṇḍitā    
kiṃ maṃ assaṃva sambandhaṃ         patodeneva vijjhasi .

Sace passasi mokkhaṃ [^231-4] me    khemaṃ vā pana passasi  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 232 -->
<a id="page-232"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 232]*

Teneva maṃ anusāsa          kiṃ atītena vijjhasi .

[635] atītaṃ mānusaṃ kammaṃ           dukkaraṃ durabhisambhavaṃ    
na taṃ sakkomi mocetuṃ        tvaṃ pajānassu [^232-1] khattiya .

Santi vehāyasā nāgā       iddhimanto yasassino    
tepi ādāya gaccheyyuṃ       yassa honti tathāvidhā .

Santi vehāyasā assā       iddhimanto yasassino    
tepi ādāya gaccheyyuṃ       yassa honti tathāvidhā .

Santi vehāyasā pakkhī        iddhimanto yasassino    
tepi ādāya gaccheyyuṃ       yassa honti tathāvidhā .

Santi vehāyasā yakkhā       iddhimanto yasassino    
tepi ādāya gaccheyyuṃ       yassa honti tathāvidhā .

Atītaṃ mānusaṃ kammaṃ           dukkaraṃ durabhisambhavaṃ    
na taṃ sakkomi mocetuṃ        antalikkhena khattiya .

[636] atīradassī puriso            mahante udakaṇṇave    
yattha so labhate gādhaṃ        tattha so vindate sukhaṃ .

Evaṃ amhañca rañño ca       tvaṃ patiṭṭhā mahosadha    
tvaṃ nosi mantīnaṃ seṭṭho      amhe dukkhā pamocaya .

[637] atītaṃ mānusaṃ kammaṃ           dukkaraṃ durabhisambhavaṃ    
na taṃ sakkomi mocetuṃ        tvaṃ pajānassu senaka .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 233 -->
<a id="page-233"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 233]*

[638] suṇohi metaṃ vacanaṃ           passasenaṃ mahabbhayaṃ    
senakadāni pucchāmi          kiṃ kiccaṃ idha maññasi .

[639] aggiṃ vā dvārato dema      gaṇhāmase vikantanaṃ [^233-1]    
aññamaññaṃ vadhitvāna          khippaṃ hissāma jīvitaṃ    
mā no rājā brahmadatto    ciraṃ dukkhena mārayi .

[640] suṇohi metaṃ vacanaṃ           passasenaṃ mahabbhayaṃ    
pukkusadāni pucchāmi          kiṃ kiccaṃ idha maññasi .

[641] visaṃ khāditvā miyyāma        khippaṃ hissāma jīvitaṃ    
mā no rājā brahmadatto    ciraṃ dukkhena mārayi .

[642] suṇohi metaṃ vacanaṃ           passasenaṃ mahabbhayaṃ    
kāmindadāni pucchāmi         kiṃ kiccaṃ idha maññasi .

[643] rajjuyā bajjha miyyāma        papātā papatemase [^233-2]    
mā no rājā brahmadatto    ciraṃ dukkhena mārayi .

[644] suṇohi metaṃ vacanaṃ           passasenaṃ mahabbhayaṃ    
devindadāni pucchāmi         kiṃ kiccaṃ idha maññasi .

[645] aggiṃ vā dvārato dema      gaṇhāmase vikantanaṃ    
aññamaññaṃ vadhitvāna          khippaṃ hissāma jīvitaṃ    
na no sakkoti mocetuṃ       sukheneva mahosadho .

[646] yathā kadalino sāraṃ          anvesaṃ nādhigacchati    
evaṃ anvesamānā naṃ        pañhaṃ nājjhagamāmase .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 234 -->
<a id="page-234"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 234]*

yathā simbalino sāraṃ         anvesaṃ nādhigacchati    
evaṃ anvesamānā naṃ        pañhaṃ nājjhagamāmase .

Adese vata no vuṭṭhaṃ        kuñjarānaṃvanūdake    
sakāse dummanussānaṃ         bālānaṃ avijānataṃ    
ubbedhati me hadayaṃ          mukhañca parisussati    
nibbutiṃ nādhigacchāmi          aggidaḍḍhova ātape .

Kammārānaṃ yathā ukkā       anto jhāyati no bahi    
evampi hadayaṃ mayhaṃ          anto jhāyati no bahi .

[647] tato so paṇḍito dhīro       atthadassī mahosadho    
vedehaṃ dukkhitaṃ disvā        idaṃ vacanamabravi .

Mā tvaṃ bhāyi mahārāja       mā tvaṃ bhāyi rathesabha    
ahantaṃ mocayissāmi          rāhuggahitaṃva candimaṃ .

Mā tvaṃ bhāyi mahārāja       mā tvaṃ bhāyi rathesabha    
ahantaṃ mocayissāmi          rāhuggahitaṃva suriyaṃ .

Mā tvaṃ bhāyi mahārāja       mā tvaṃ bhāyi rathesabha    
ahantaṃ mocayissāmi          paṅke sannaṃva kuñjaraṃ .

Mā tvaṃ bhāyi mahārāja       mā tvaṃ bhāyi rathesabha    
ahantaṃ mocayissāmi          peḷabandhaṃva pannagaṃ .

Mā tvaṃ bhāyi mahārāja       mā tvaṃ bhāyi rathesabha    
ahantaṃ mocayissāmi          pakkhiṃ bandhaṃva pañjare .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 235 -->
<a id="page-235"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 235]*

Mā tvaṃ bhāyi mahārāja       mā tvaṃ bhāyi rathesabha    
ahantaṃ mocayissāmi          macche jālagateriva .

Mā tvaṃ bhāyi mahārāja       mā tvaṃ bhāyi rathesabha    
ahantaṃ mocayissāmi          sayoggabalavāhanaṃ .

Mā tvaṃ bhāyi mahārāja       mā tvaṃ bhāyi rathesabha    
pañcālaṃ bāhayissāmi         kākasenaṃva leḍḍunā .

Ādū paññā kimatthiyā        amacco vāpi tādiso    
yo taṃ sambādhapakkhantaṃ [^235-1]     dukkhā na parimocaye .

[648] etha māṇavā uṭṭhetha        mukhaṃ sodhetha sandhino    
vedeho sahamaccehi         ummaṅgena gamissati .

[649] tassa taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā         paṇḍitassānusārino    
ummaṅgadvāraṃ vivariṃsu         yantayutte ca aggaḷe .

[650] purato senako yāti         pacchato ca mahosadho    
majjhe ca rājā vedeho     amaccaparivārito .

[651] ummaṅgā nikkhamitvāna        vedeho nāvamāruhi    
abhiruyhañca ñatvāna          anusāsi mahosadho .

Ayaṃ te sassuro deva        ayaṃ sassu janādhipa    
yathā mātu paṭipatti          evaṃ te hotu sassuyā .

Yathāpi niyako bhātā         sodariyo ekamātuko    
evaṃ pañcālacando te       dayitabbo rathesabha .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 236 -->
<a id="page-236"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 236]*

Ayaṃ pañcālacandī te         rājaputtī abhijjhitā [^236-1]    
kāmaṃ karohi te tāya        bhariyā te rathesabha .

[652] āruyha nāvaṃ taramāno       kiṃ nu tīramhi tiṭṭhasi    
kicchā muttamha dukkhato       yāmadāni mahosadha .

[653] nesa dhammo mahārāja        yohaṃ senāya nāyako    
senāṅgaṃ parihāpetvā       attānaṃ parimocaye .

Nivesanamhi te deva         senāṅgaṃ parihāpitaṃ    
taṃ dinnaṃ brahmadattena        ānayissaṃ rathesabha .

[654] appaseno mahāsenaṃ         kathaṃ niggayha [^236-2] ṭhassasi    
dubbalo balavantena          vihaññissasi paṇḍita .

[655] appasenopi ce mantī        mahāsenaṃ amantinaṃ    
jānāti rājā rājāno      ādiccovudayantamaṃ .

[656] susukhaṃ vata saṃvāso           paṇḍitehīti senaka    
pakkhīva pañjare bandhe        macche jālagateriva    
amittahatthatthagate [^236-3]        mocayi no mahosadho .

[657] evameva [^236-4] mahārāja       paṇḍitā hi sukhāvahā    
pakkhīva pañjare bandhe        macche jālagateriva    
amittahatthatthagate [^236-3]        mocayi no mahosadho .

[658] rakkhitvā kasiṇaṃ rattiṃ         cūḷaneyyo mahabbalo    
udentaṃ aruṇuggasmiṃ          upakāraṃ upāgami .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 237 -->
<a id="page-237"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 237]*

Āruyha pavaraṃ nāgaṃ          balavantaṃ saṭṭhihāyanaṃ    
rājā avoca pañcālo       cūḷaneyyo mahabbalo .

Sannaddho maṇicammena         saramādāya pāṇinā    
pesiye ajjhabhāsittha         puthugumbe samāgate .

Hatthārohe anīkaṭṭhe        rathike pattikārake    
upāsanamhi katahatthe         vālavedhe samāgate .

[659] pesetha kuñjare dantī        balavante saṭṭhihāyane    
maddantu kuñjarā nagaraṃ        vedehena sumāpitaṃ .

Vacchadantamukhā setā         tikkhaggā aṭṭhivedhino    
panuṇṇā dhanuvegena          sampatantutarītarā .

Māṇavā cammino sūrā        cittadaṇḍayutāvudhā    
pakkhandino mahānāgā        hatthīnaṃ hontu sammukhā .

Sattiyo teladhotāyo        acchimanto [^237-1] pabhassarā    
vijjotamānā tiṭṭhantu        sataraṃsīva tārakā .

Āvudhabalavantānaṃ            guṇikāyuradhārinaṃ    
etādisānaṃ yodhānaṃ         saṅgāme apalāyinaṃ    
vedeho kuto muccissati      sace pakkhīva kāhiti .

Tiṃsa me purisanāvutyo        sabbe ekekaniccitā [^237-2]    
yesaṃ samaṃ na passāmi         kevalaṃ mahimaṃ caraṃ .

Nāgā ca kappitā dantī       balavantā saṭṭhihāyanā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 238 -->
<a id="page-238"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 238]*

Yesaṃ khandhesu sobhanti        kumārā cārudassanā .

Pītālaṅkārā pītavasanā       pītuttaranivāsanā    
nāgakkhandhesu sobhanti        devaputtāva nandane .

Pāṭhīnavaṇṇā nettiṃsā        teladhotā pabhassarā    
niṭṭhitā naravīrehi [^238-1]        samadhārā sunissitā .

Vellāḷino vītamalā         sikkāyasamayā daḷhā    
gahitā balavantehi           suppahārappahāribhi .

Suvaṇṇatharusampannā           lohitakañcūpadhāritā    
vivattamānā sobhanti         vijjuvabbhaghanantare .

Pathakā cammino [^238-2] sūrā      asicammassa kovidā    
dhanuggahā sikkhitarā [^238-3]       nāgakkhandhanipātino [^238-4] .

Etādisehi parikkhitto       natthi mokkho ito tava    
pabhāvante na passāmi        yena tvaṃ mithilaṃ vaje .

[660] kinnu santaramānova          nāgaṃ pesesi kuñjaraṃ    
pahaṭṭharūpo āgamasi [^238-5]       laddhatthosmīti [^238-6] maññasi .

Oharetaṃ dhanucāpaṃ           khurappaṃ paṭisaṃhara    
oharetaṃ subhaṃ cammaṃ          veḷuriyamaṇisannibhaṃ [^238-7] .

[661] pasannamukhavaṇṇosi            mitapubbañca [^238-8] bhāsasi    
hoti kho maraṇakāle         edisī vaṇṇasampadā .

[662] moghante gajjitaṃ rāja        bhinnamantosi khattiya    
#[^238-1] ma. Naradhīrehi .  [^238-2] sī. Yu. Patākā . Ma. Paṭākā cammino .   [^238-3] sī. Yu.

#tharuggāhā sikkhitāro .  [^238-4] sī. Yu. Nāgakhandhātipātino . Ma. Nāgakhandhe nipātino .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 239 -->
<a id="page-239"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 239]*

Duggaṇho hi [^239-1] tayā rājā   khaluṅkeneva sindhavo .

Tiṇṇo hiyyo rājā gaṅgaṃ     sāmacco saparijjano    
haṃsarājaṃ yathā dhaṅko         anujjavaṃ patissati .

[663] sigālā rattibhāgena         phullaṃ disvāna kiṃsukaṃ    
maṃsapesīti maññantā          paribyūḷhā migādhamā .

Vītivattāsu rattīsu           uggatasmiṃ divākare [^239-2]    
kiṃsukaṃ phullitaṃ disvā          āsāchinnā migādhamā .

Evameva tuvaṃ rāja          vedehaṃ parivāraya [^239-3]    
āsacchinno gamissasi         sigālā kiṃsukaṃ yathā .

[664] imassa hatthapāde ca         kaṇṇanāsañca chindatha    
yo me amittaṃ hatthagataṃ       vedehaṃ parimocayi .

Imaṃ maṃsaṃva pātabyaṃ [^239-4]        sūle katvā pacantu naṃ    
yo me amittaṃ hatthagataṃ       vedehaṃ parimocayi .

Yathāpi usabhacammaṃ            paṭhabyā vihanīyati [^239-5]    
sīhassa atho byagghassa        hoti saṅkusamāhataṃ .

Evantaṃ vihanitvāna          vedhayissāmi sattiyā    
yo me amittaṃ hatthagataṃ       vedehaṃ parimocayi .

[665] sace me hatthapāde ca       kaṇṇanāsañca chedasi [^239-6]    
evaṃ pañcālacandassa         vedeho chedayissati .

Sace me hatthapāde ca       kaṇṇanāsañca chedasi    
#[^239-1] ma. Duggaṇhosi .    [^239-2] ma. Divaṅkare .   [^239-3] ma. Parivāriya .  [^239-4] sī. Yu. Pātabbaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 240 -->
<a id="page-240"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 240]*

Evaṃ pañcālacandiyā         vedeho chedayissati .

Sace me hatthapāde ca       kaṇṇanāsañca chedasi    
evaṃ nandāya deviyā        vedeho chedayissati .

Sace me hatthapāde ca       kaṇṇanāsañca chedasi    
evante puttadārassa        vedeho chedayissati .

Sace maṃsañca pātabyaṃ         sūle katvā pacissasi    
evaṃ pañcālacandassa         vedeho pācayissati .

Sace maṃsañca pātabyaṃ         sūle katvā pacissasi    
evaṃ pañcālacandiyā         vedeho pācayissati .

Sace maṃsañca pātabyaṃ         sūle katvā pacissasi    
evaṃ nandāya deviyā        vedeho pācayissati .

Sace maṃsañca pātabyaṃ         sūle katvā pacissasi    
evante puttadārassa        vedeho pācayissati .

Sace maṃ vihanitvāna [^240-1]       vedhayissasi sattiyā    
evaṃ pañcālacandassa         vedeho vedhayissati .

Sace maṃ vihanitvāna          vedhayissasi sattiyā    
evaṃ pañcālacandiyā         vedeho vedhayissati .

Sace maṃ vihanitvāna          vedhayissasi sattiyā    
evaṃ nandāya deviyā        vedeho vedhayissati .

Sace maṃ vihanitvāna          vedhayissasi sattiyā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 241 -->
<a id="page-241"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 241]*

Evante puttadārassa        vedeho vedhayissati .

Evaṃ no mantitaṃ raho        vedehena mayā saha    
yathā [^241-1] palasataṃ cammaṃ        kontimantāsuniṭṭhitaṃ [^241-2]    
upeti tanutāṇāya           sarānaṃ paṭihantave .

Sukhāvaho dukkhanūdo          vedehassa yasassino    
matinte paṭihaññāmi          usuṃ palasatena vā .

[666] iṅgha passa mahārāja         suññaṃ antepuraṃ tava    
orodhā ca kumārā ca       tava mātā ca khattiya    
ummaṅgā nīharitvāna         vedehassupanāmitā .

[667] iṅgha antepuraṃ mayhaṃ         gantvāna vicinātha naṃ    
yathā imassa vacanaṃ           saccaṃ vā yadi vā musā .

[668] evametaṃ mahārāja          yathā āha mahosadho    
suññaṃ antepuraṃ sabbaṃ         kākapatanakaṃ yathā .

[669] ito gatā mahārāja         nārī sabbaṅgasobhanā    
kosumbhaphalakasussoṇī [^241-3]       haṃsagaggarabhāṇinī .Tha    
ito nītā mahārāja         nārī sabbaṅgasobhanā    
koseyyavasanā sāmā        jātarūpasumekhalā .

Surattapādā kalyāṇī         suvaṇṇamaṇimekhalā    
pārevatakkhī sutanū           bimboṭṭhā tanumajjhimā .

Sujātā bhujalaṭṭhīva           vedīva [^241-4] tanumajjhimā    
#[^241-1] ma. Yathāpi .    [^241-2] sī. Yu. Kontīmantī suniṭṭhitaṃ .   [^241-3] ma. Kosambaphalaka... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 242 -->
<a id="page-242"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 242]*

Dīghassā kesā asitā        īsakaggapavellitā .

Sujātā migacchāpīva          hemantaggisikhāriva    
nadīva giriduggesu            sañchannā khuddaveḷubhi .

Nāganāsūrukalyāṇī           paṭhamā [^242-1] timbarutthanī    
nātidīghā nātirassā         nālomā nātilomakā [^242-2] .

Nandāya nūna maraṇena         nandasi sirivāhana    
ahañca nūna nandā ca         gacchāma yamasādhanaṃ .

[670] dibbaṃ adhiyase māyaṃ          akāsi cakkhumohanaṃ    
yo me amittaṃ hatthagataṃ       vedehaṃ parimocayi .

[671] adhiyanti ve [^242-3] mahārāja     dibbamāyīdha paṇḍitā    
te mocayanti attānaṃ        paṇḍitā mantino janā .

Santi māṇavaputtā me        kusalā sandhichedakā    
yesaṃ katena maggena         vedeho mithilaṃ gato .

[672] iṅgha passa mahārāja         ummaṅgaṃ sādhumāpitaṃ    
hatthīnaṃ atha assānaṃ          rathānaṃ atha pattinaṃ    
ālokabhūtaṃ tiṭṭhantaṃ          ummaṅgaṃ sādhuniṭṭhitaṃ [^242-4] .

[673] lābhā vata videhānaṃ         yassime edisā paṇḍitā    
ghare vasanti vijite          yathā tvamasi mahosadha .

[674] vuttiñca parihārañca          diguṇaṃ bhattavettanaṃ  
dadāmi vipule bhoge         bhuñja kāme ramassu ca  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 243 -->
<a id="page-243"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 243]*

Mā videhaṃ paccagamā         kiṃ videho karissati .

[675] yo cajetha mahārāja         bhattāraṃ dhanakāraṇā    
ubhinnaṃ hoti gārayho        attano ca parassa ca    
yāva jīveyya vedeho       nāññassa puriso siyaṃ [^243-1] .

Yo cajetha mahārāja         bhattāraṃ dhanakāraṇā    
ubhinnaṃ hoti gārayho        attano ca parassa ca    
yāva tiṭṭheyya vedeho      nāññassa vijite vase .

[676] dammi nikkhasahassante         gāmāsītiñca kāsisu    
dāsīsatāni cattāri          dammi bhariyāsatañca te    
sabbaṃ senāṅgamādāya        sotthiṃ gaccha mahosadha .

[677] yāva dadantu hatthīnaṃ          assānaṃ diguṇaṃ vidhaṃ    
tappentu annapānena        rathike pattikārake .

[678] hatthī asse rathe pattī       gacchevādāya paṇḍita    
passatu taṃ mahārājā         vedeho mithilaṃ gataṃ .

[679] hatthī assā rathā pattī       senā sādissate mahā    
caturaṅginī bhiṃsarūpā [^243-2]        kinnu maññanti [^243-3] paṇḍitā .

[680] ānando te mahārāja       uttamo paṭidissati    
sabbaṃ senāṅgamādāya        sotthippatto mahosadho .

[681] yathā petaṃ susānasmiṃ         chaḍḍetvā caturo janā    
evaṃ kappiliye tyamhā       chaḍḍayitvā idhāgatā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 244 -->
<a id="page-244"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 244]*

Atha tvaṃ kena vaṇṇena        kena vā pana hetunā    
kena vā atthajātena        attānaṃ parimocayi .

[682] atthaṃ atthena vedeha        mantaṃ mantena khattiya    
parivārayissaṃ [^244-1] rājānaṃ      jambūdīpaṃva sāgaro .

Dinnaṃ nikkhasahassaṃ me         gāmāsītiñca [^244-2] kāsisu    
dāsīsatāni cattāri          dinnaṃ bhariyāsatañca me    
sabbaṃ senāṅgamādāya        sotthināmhi idhāgato .

[683] susukhaṃ vata saṃvāso           paṇḍitehīti senaka    
pakkhīva pañjare bandhe        macche jālagateriva    
amittahatthatthagate [^244-3]        mocayi no mahosadho .

[684] evameva mahārāja          paṇḍitā hi sukhāvahā    
pakkhīva pañjare bandhe        macche jālagateriva    
amittahatthatthagate [^244-3]        mocayi no mahosadho .

[685] āhaññantu sabbavīṇā         bheriyo dendimāni ca    
dhamantu māgadhā saṅkhā        vaggū nadantu dundubhī .

[686] orodhā ca kumārā ca       vesiyānā ca brāhmaṇā    
bahuṃ annañca pānañca         paṇḍitassābhihārayuṃ .

Hatthārohā anīkaṭṭhā        rathikā pattikārakā    
bahuṃ annañca pānañca         paṇḍitassābhihārayuṃ .

Samāgatā jānapadā          negamā ca samāgatā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 245 -->
<a id="page-245"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 245]*

Bahuṃ annañca pānañca         paṇḍitassābhihārayuṃ .

Bahujjano pasannosi          disvā paṇḍitamāgataṃ    
paṇḍitamhi anuppatte         celukkhepo pavattathāti [^245-1] .T    
mahosadhajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ .

[^245-6] bhūridattajātakaṃ    
[687] yaṅkiñci ratanamatthi           dhataraṭṭhanivesane    
sabbāni te upayantu         dhītaraṃ dehi rājino .

[688] na no vivāho nāgehi       katapubbo kudācanaṃ    
taṃ vivāhaṃ asaṃyuttaṃ           kathaṃ amhe karomase .

[689] jīvitaṃ nūna te cattaṃ          raṭṭhaṃ vā manujādhipa    
na hi nāge kuppitamhi        ciraṃ jīvanti tādisā .

Yo tvaṃ deva manussosi       iddhimantaṃ aniddhimā    
varuṇassa niyaṃ puttaṃ           yāmunaṃ atimaññasi .

[690] nātimaññāmi rājānaṃ         dhataraṭṭhaṃ yasassinaṃ    
dhataraṭṭho hi nāgānaṃ         bahūnamapi issaro .

Ahi mahānubhāvopi           na me dhītaramāraho    
khattiyo ca videhānaṃ         abhijātā samuddajā .

[691] kambalassatarā uṭṭhentu       sabbe nāge nivedaya    
bārāṇasiṃ pavajjantu          mā ca kañci [^245-2] viheṭhayuṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 246 -->
<a id="page-246"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 246]*

[692] nivesanesu sobbhesu         rathiyā caccaresu ca    
rukkhaggesu ca lambantu        vitatā toraṇesu ca .

Ahampi sabbasetena          mahatā sumahaṃ puraṃ    
parikkhipissaṃ bhogehi          kāsīnaṃ janayaṃ bhayaṃ .

[693] tassa taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā         uragānekavaṇṇino    
bārāṇasiṃ pavajjiṃsu           na ca kañci viheṭhayuṃ .

Nivesanesu sobbhesu         rathiyā caccaresu ca    
rukkhaggesu ca lambiṃsu         vitatā toraṇesu ca .

Ete disvāna lambante      pūthū kandiṃsu nāriyo    
nāge soṇḍikate disvā      passasante mahuṃ mahuṃ [^246-1] .

Bārāṇasī sabyādhitā [^246-2]      āturā samapajjatha    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       dhītaraṃ dehi rājino .

[694] pupphābhihārassa vanassa majjhena    
ko lohitakkho vihatantaraṃso [^246-3]    
kā kambukāyūradharā suvatthā    
tiṭṭhanti nāriyo dasa vandamānā .

Ko tvaṃ brahābāhu vanassa majjhe    
virocasi ghatasitto va aggi    
mahesakkho aññatarosi yakkho    
udāhu nāgosi mahānubhāvo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 247 -->
<a id="page-247"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 247]*

[695] nāgohamasmi iddhimā         tejasī [^247-1] duratikkamo    
ḍaṃseyyaṃ tejasā kuddho       phītaṃ janapadaṃ api .

Samuddajā hi me mātā       dhataraṭṭho ca me pitā    
sudassanakaniṭṭhosmi           bhūridattoti maṃ vidū .

[696] yaṃ gambhīraṃ sadāvaṭṭaṃ          rahadaṃ bhesmaṃ [^247-2] pekkhasi    
eso dibyo mamāvāso      anekasataporiso .

Mayūrakoñcābhirudaṃ            nīlodaṃ vanamajjhato    
yamunaṃ pavisa mā bhīto         khemaṃ vattavatiṃ [^247-3] sivaṃ .

[697] tattha patto sānucaro        saha puttena brāhmaṇa    
pūjito mayhaṃ kāmehi         sukhaṃ brāhmaṇa vacchasi .

[698] samā samantaparito           pahutatagarā [^247-4] mahī    
indagopakasañchannā          sobhati harituttamā .

Rammāni vanacetyāni         rammā haṃsupakūjitā    
opupphapadumā tiṭṭhanti        pokkharaññā [^247-5] sunimmitā .

Aṭṭhaṃsā sukatā thambhā        sabbe veḷuriyāmayā    
sahassathambhā pāsādā        pūrā kaññāhi jotare .

Vimānaṃ upapannosi           dibbaṃ puññehi attano    
asambādhaṃ sivaṃ rammaṃ          accantasukhasañhitaṃ .

Maññe sahassanettassa        vimānaṃ nābhikaṅkhasi    
iddhi hi tyāyaṃ vipulā        sakkasseva jutīmato .

#[^247-1] ma. Tejassī .    [^247-2] ma. Bhismaṃ .   [^247-3] ma. Vattavataṃ .   [^247-4] ma. Pahūtatagarā . Sī. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 248 -->
<a id="page-248"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 248]*

[699] manasāpi na pattabbo         ānubhāvo jutīmato    
paricāriyamānānaṃ            indānaṃ [^248-1] vasavattinaṃ .

[700] taṃ vimānaṃ abhijjhāya          amarānaṃ sukhesinaṃ    
uposathaṃ upavasanto          semi vammikamuddhani .

[701] ahañca migamesāno          saputto pāvisiṃ vanaṃ    
te [^248-2] maṃ mataṃ vā jīvaṃ vā    nābhivedenti ñātakā .

Āmantaye bhūridattaṃ          kāsiputtaṃ yasassinaṃ    
tayā no samanuññātā        api passemu ñātake .

[702] eso hi vata me chando      yaṃ vasesi mamantike    
na hi etādisā kāmā       sulabhā honti mānuse .

Sace tvaṃ nicchase vatthuṃ       mama kāmehi pūjito    
mayā tvaṃ samanuññāto        sotthiṃ passāhi ñātake .

[703] dhārayimaṃ maṇiṃ dibyaṃ           pasuṃ putte ca vindasi    
arogo sukhito hohi [^248-3]      gacchevādāya brāhmaṇa .

[704] kusalaṃ paṭinandāmi            bhūridatta vaco tava    
pabbajissāmi jiṇṇosmi        na kāme abhipatthaye .

[705] brahmacariyassa ce bhaṅgo      hoti bhogehi kāriyaṃ    
avikappamāno eyyāsi       bahuṃ dassāmi te dhanaṃ .

[706] kusalaṃ paṭinandāmi            bhūridatta vaco tava    
punapi āgamissāmi           sace attho bhavissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 249 -->
<a id="page-249"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 249]*

[707] idaṃ vatvā bhūridatto         pesesi caturo jane    
etha gacchatha uṭṭhetha         khippaṃ pāpetha brāhmaṇaṃ .

Tassa taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā         uṭṭhāya caturo janā    
pesitā bhūridattena          khippaṃ pāpesuṃ brāhmaṇaṃ .

[708] maṇiṃ paggayha maṅgalyaṃ         sādhu vittaṃ [^249-1] manoramaṃ    
selaṃ byañjanasampannaṃ         ko imaṃ maṇimajjhagā .

[709] lohitakkhisahassāhi           samantā parivāritaṃ    
ajja kālaṃ pathaṃ [^249-2] gacchaṃ      ajjhagāhaṃ maṇiṃ imaṃ .

[710] supaciṇṇo ayaṃ selo         accito mahito sadā    
sudhārito sunikkhitto         sabbatthamabhisādhaye .

Upacāravipannassa            nikkhepe dhāraṇāya vā    
ayaṃ selo vināsāya         pariciṇṇo ayoniso .

Nayimaṃ akusalo dibyaṃ          maṇiṃ dhāretumāraho    
paṭipajja sataṃ nikkhaṃ           dehimaṃ ratanaṃ mama .

[711] neva māyaṃ [^249-3] maṇi keyyo    gohi vā ratanehi vā    
selo byañjanasampanno       neva keyyo maṇi mama .

[712] no ce tayā maṇi keyyo     gohi vā ratanehi vā    
atha kena maṇi keyyo        taṃ me akkhāhi pucchito .

[713] yo me saṃse mahānāgaṃ       tejasiṃ duratikkamaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 250 -->
<a id="page-250"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 250]*

Tassa dajjaṃ imaṃ selaṃ         jalantariva [^250-1] tejasā .

[714] ko nu brāhmaṇavaṇṇena       supaṇṇo patataṃ varo    
nāgaṃ jigiṃsamanveti [^250-2]        anvesaṃ bhakkhamattano .

[715] nāhaṃ dijādhipo homi         na diṭṭho garuḷo mayā    
āsīvisena vittosmi [^250-3]      vejjo brāhmaṇa maṃ vidū .

[716] kiṃ nu tuyhaṃ balaṃ atthi         kiṃ sippaṃ vijjate tava    
kismiṃ vā tvaṃ paratthaddho      uragaṃ nāpacāyasi .

[717] āraññikassa isino          ciraṃ rattaṃ tapassino    
supaṇṇo kosiyassakkhā        visavijjaṃ anuttaraṃ .

Taṃ bhāvitattaññataraṃ           sammantaṃ pabbatantare    
sakkaccantaṃ upaṭṭhāsiṃ         rattindivamatandito .

So tadā pariciṇṇo me       vattaṃ vā brahmacāriyaṃ [^250-4]    
dibyaṃ pātukari mantaṃ          kāmasā bhagavā mamaṃ .

Tyāhaṃ mante paratthaddho      nāhaṃ bhāyāmi bhoginaṃ    
ācariyo visaghātānaṃ         ālambāyanoti [^250-5] maṃ vidū .

[718] gaṇhāmase maṇiṃ tāta         somadatta vijānāhi    
mā daṇḍena siriṃ pattaṃ        kāmasā pajahāmase [^250-6] .

[719] sakaṃ nivesanaṃ pattaṃ           yo taṃ brāhmaṇa pūjayi    
evaṃ kalyāṇakārissa         kiṃ mohā dubbhimicchasi .

#[^250-1] ma. Jalantamiva .   [^250-2] ma. Jigī - .   [^250-3] cittosmītipi pāṭho . Ma. Vittoti .

#[^250-4] ma. Vattavā brahmacariyavā .   [^250-5] ma. Alampānoti . Sī. Yu. Ālambānoti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 251 -->
<a id="page-251"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 251]*

Sace tvaṃ [^251-1] dhanakāmosi      bhūridatto padissati [^251-2]    
tameva gantvā yācassu       bahuṃ dassati te dhanaṃ .

[720] hatthagataṃ pattagataṃ            nikkiṇṇaṃ khādituṃ varaṃ    
mā no sandiṭṭhiko attho     somadatta upaccagā .

[721] paccati niraye ghore         mahimassa vindrīyati [^251-3]    
mittadubbhī hitaccāgī          jīvare vāpi sussati [^251-4] .

Sace tvaṃ [^251-1] dhanakāmosi      bhūridatto padissati    
maññe attakataṃ veraṃ         na ciraṃ vedayissasi .

[722] mahāyaññaṃ yajitvāna          evaṃ sujjhanti brāhmaṇā    
mahāyaññaṃ yajissāma          evaṃ mokkhāma pāpakā .

[723] handadāni apāyāmi          nāhaṃ ajja tayā saha    
padamekaṃ [^251-5] na gaccheyyaṃ      evaṃ kibbisakārinā .

[724] idaṃ vatvāna pitaraṃ           somadatto bahussuto    
ujjhāpetvāna bhūtāni        tamhā ṭhānā apakkami .

[725] gaṇhāhetaṃ mahānāgaṃ         āharetaṃ maṇiṃ mama    
indagopakavaṇṇābhā [^251-6]       yassa rohitako siro .

Kappāsapicurāsīva            eso kāyo padissati [^251-7]    
vammikaggagato seti          taṃ tvaṃ gaṇhāhi brāhmaṇa .

[726] athosadhehi dibbehi          jappaṃ mantapadāni ca    
evantaṃ asakkhi phuṭṭhuṃ [^251-8]      katvā parittamattano .

#[^251-1] sī. Yu. Sace hi .  [^251-2] ma. Padassati .  [^251-3] ma. Mahissamapi vivarati . Sī. Yu.

#mahissamava dīyati .  [^251-4] sī. Yu. Jīvare cāpi sussare .  [^251-5] ma. Padampekaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 252 -->
<a id="page-252"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 252]*

[727] mamaṃ disvāna āyantaṃ         sabbakāmasamiddhinaṃ    
indriyāni ahaṭṭhāni         sāvaṃ jātaṃ mukhaṃ tava .

Padumaṃ yathā hatthagataṃ          pāṇinā parimadditaṃ    
sāvaṃ jātaṃ mukhaṃ tuyhaṃ         mamaṃ disvāna edisaṃ .

[728] kacci nu te nābhissasi        kacci te atthi vedanā    
yena sāvaṃ mukhaṃ tuyhaṃ         mamaṃ disvāna āgataṃ .

[729] supinaṃ tāta addakkhiṃ          ito māsaṃ adhogataṃ    
dakkhiṇaṃ viya me bāhuṃ         chetvā ruhiramakkhitaṃ    
puriso ādāya pakkāmi       mama rodantiyā sati .

Yatohaṃ supinamaddakkhiṃ          sudassana vijānāhi    
tato divā vā rattiṃ vā      sukhaṃ me nopalabbhati .

[730] yaṃ pubbe parivāriṃsu [^252-1]       kaññā ruciraviggahā    
hemajālapaṭicchannā          bhūridatto na dissati .

Yaṃ pubbe parivāriṃsu [^252-1]       nettiṃsavaradhārino    
kaṇikārāva samphullā         bhūridatto na dissati .

Handadāni gamissāma          bhūridattanivesanaṃ    
dhammaṭṭhaṃ sīlasampannaṃ          passāma tava bhātaraṃ .

[731] tañca disvāna āyantaṃ        bhūridattassa mātaraṃ    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       bhūridattassa bhariyāyo .

Puttanteyye na jānāma      ito māsaṃ adhogataṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 253 -->
<a id="page-253"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 253]*

Mataṃ vā yadi vā jīvaṃ         bhūridattaṃ yasassinaṃ .

[732] sakuṇī hataputtāva            suññaṃ disvā kulāvakaṃ    
ciraṃ dukkhena jhāyissaṃ         bhūridattaṃ apassatī .

Kururī hatacchāpāva [^253-1]        suññaṃ disvā kulāvakaṃ    
ciraṃ dukkhena jhāyissaṃ         bhūridattaṃ apassatī .

Sā nūna cakkavakkīva          pallalasmiṃ anūdake    
ciraṃ dukkhena jhāyissaṃ         bhūridattaṃ apassatī .

Kammārānaṃ yathā ukkā       anto jhāyati no bahi    
evaṃ jhāyāmi sokena        bhūridattaṃ apassatī .

[733] sālāva sampamadditā [^253-2]      mālutena pamadditā    
senti puttā ca dārā ca     bhūridattanivesane .

[734] tesaṃ sutvāna nigghosaṃ        bhūridattanivesane    
ariṭṭho ca subhogo [^253-3] ca     padhāviṃsu [^253-4] anantarā    
amma assāsa mā soci       evaṃdhammā hi pāṇino    
cavanti upapajjanti           esāssa pariṇāmatā .

[735] ahaṃpi tāta jānāmi          evaṃdhammā hi pāṇino    
sokena ca paretasmiṃ         bhūridattaṃ apassatī    
ajja ce me imaṃ rattiṃ       sudassana vijānāhi    
bhūridattaṃ apassantī           maññe hissāmi jīvitaṃ .

[736] amma assāsa mā soci       ānayissāma bhātaraṃ    
#[^253-1] ma. Kurarī hatachāpāva .   [^253-2] ma. Sampamathitā .    [^253-3] sī. Yu. Subhago .   [^253-4] sī. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 254 -->
<a id="page-254"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 254]*

Disodisaṃ gamissāma           bhātupariyesanaṃ caraṃ    
pabbate giriduggesu          gāmesu nigamesu ca    
orena sattarattassa [^254-1]      bhātaraṃ passa āgataṃ .

[737] hatthā pamutto urago        pāde te nipati bhusaṃ    
kacci nu ḍaṃsito [^254-2] tāta      mā bhāyi sukhito bhava .

[738] neva mayhaṃ ayaṃ nāgo        alaṃ dukkhāya kāyaci    
yāvatatthi ahiggāho         mayā bhiyyo na vijjati .

[739] ko nu brāhmaṇavaṇṇena       datto [^254-3] parisamāgato    
avhāyatu [^254-4] suyuddhena       suṇātu [^254-5] parisā mama .

[740] tvaṃ maṃ nāgena ālamba       ahaṃ maṇḍūkachāpiyā    
hotu no abbhutaṃ tattha        ā sahassehi pañcahi .

[741] ahañhi vasumā aḍḍho         tvaṃ daliddosi māṇava    
ko nu te pāṭibhogatthi       upajūtañca kiṃ siyā .

Upajūtañca me assa          pāṭibhogo ca tādiso    
hotu no abbhutaṃ tattha        ā sahassehi pañcahi .

[742] suṇohi me mahārāja         vacanaṃ bhaddamatthu te    
pañcannaṃ me sahassānaṃ        pāṭibhogo hi kittima .

[743] pettikaṃ vā iṇaṃ hoti        yaṃ vā hoti sayaṃ kataṃ    
kiṃ tvaṃ evaṃ bahuṃ mayhaṃ        dhanaṃ yācasi brāhmaṇa .

[744] ālambāyano [^254-6] hi nāgena   mamaṃ abhijigiṃsati    
#[^254-1] sī. Yu. Dasarattassa .   [^254-2] sī. Kacci taṃ nu ḍaṃsī . Ma. Kacci nu taṃ ḍaṃsī .

#yu. Kaccitānuḍasī .   [^254-3] ma. Ditto . Ito paraṃ īdisameva .  [^254-4] ma. Avhāyantu .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 255 -->
<a id="page-255"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 255]*

Ahaṃ maṇḍūkachāpiyā           ḍaṃsayissāmi brāhmaṇaṃ .

Taṃ tvaṃ daṭṭhuṃ mahārāja        ajja raṭṭhābhivaḍḍhana    
khagga [^255-1] saṅghaparibyūḷho      nīyāhi ahidassanaṃ [^255-2] .

[745] neva taṃ atimaññāmi          sippavādena māṇava    
atimattosi sippena          uragaṃ nāpacāyasi .

[746] ahampi nātimaññāmi          sippavādena brāhmaṇa    
avisena ca nāgena          bhusaṃ vañcayase janaṃ .

Evañcetaṃ jano jaññā       yathā jānāmi taṃ ahaṃ    
na tvaṃ labhasi ālamba         thusamuṭṭhiṃ [^255-3] kuto dhanaṃ .

[747] kharājino jaṭī rummī [^255-4]       datto parisamāgato    
yo tvaṃ evaṃ gataṃ nāgaṃ       aviso atimaññasi    
āpajja kho naṃ jaññāsi       puṇṇaṃ uggassa tejasā [^255-5]    
maññe taṃ bhasmarāsiṃva         khippameso karissati .

[748] siyā visaṃ siluttassa          duḍḍubhassa [^255-6] silābhuno    
neva lohitasīsassa           visaṃ nāgassa vijjati .

[749] sutametaṃ arahataṃ             saññatānaṃ tapassinaṃ    
idha dānāni datvāna         saggaṃ gacchanti dāyakā    
jīvanto dehi dānāni        yadi te atthi dātave .

Ayaṃ nāgo mahiddhiko         tejasī duratikkamo    
tena taṃ ḍaṃsayissāmi          so taṃ bhasmaṃ karissati .

#[^255-1] ma. Khatta- .   [^255-2] sī. Yu. Abhidassanaṃ .  [^255-3] ma. Bhusamuṭṭhiṃ . Sī. Yu. Sattumuṭṭhiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 256 -->
<a id="page-256"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 256]*

[750] mayāpetaṃ sutaṃ samma          saññatānaṃ tapassinaṃ    
idha dānāni datvāna         saggaṃ gacchanti dāyakā    
tvameva dehi jīvanto        yadi te atthi dātave .

Ayaṃ accimukhī [^256-1] nāma        puṇṇā uggassa tejasā    
tāya taṃ ḍaṃsayissāmi          sā taṃ bhasmaṃ karissati .

Yā dhītā dhataraṭṭhassa         vemātā bhaginī mama    
sā ḍaṃsatu accimukhī [^256-2]        puṇṇā uggassa tejasā .

[751] chamāyañce nisiñcissaṃ         brahmadatta vijānāhi    
tiṇalatāni osadhyo          ussusseyyuṃ asaṃsayaṃ .

[752] uddhañce pātayissāmi        brahmadatta vijānāhi    
satta vassāni yaṃ devo       na vasse na himaṃ pate .

[753] udake ce nisiñcissaṃ         brahmadatta vijānāhi    
yāvantodakajā pāṇā        mareyyuṃ macchakacchapā .

[754] lokyaṃ sajantaṃ udakaṃ          payāgasmiṃ patiṭṭhitaṃ    
ko maṃ ajjhoharī bhūto        ogāḷhaṃ yamunaṃ nadiṃ .

[755] yadesa lokādhipatī yasassī    
bārāṇasiṃ pakīraharī [^256-3] samantato    
tassāha putto uragūsabhassa    
subhogoti maṃ brāhmaṇa vedayanti .

[756] sacesi putto uragūsabhassa    
#[^256-1] ma. Ajamukhī .     [^256-2] ma. Sā taṃ ḍaṃsatajamukhī .  Sī. Yu. Sā dissatu accimukhī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 257 -->
<a id="page-257"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 257]*

Kāsissa [^257-1] rañño amarādhipassa    
mahesakkho aññataro pitā te    
maccesu mātā pana te atulyā    
na tādiso arahati brāhmaṇassa    
dāsaṃpi ohārituṃ [^257-2] mahānubhāvo .

[757] rukkhaṃ nissāya vijjhittho       eṇeyyaṃ pātumāgataṃ    
so viddho dūramācari [^257-3]      saravegena pekkhavā [^257-4] .

Taṃ tvaṃ patitamaddakkhi          araññasmiṃ brahāvane    
sa maṃsakājamādāya           sāyaṃ nigrodhupāgami .

Suvasāliyasaṅghuṭṭhaṃ            piṅgiyaṃ [^257-5] saṇṭhatāyutaṃ    
kokilābhirudaṃ rammaṃ           dhuvaṃ haritasaddalaṃ .

Tattha te so pāturahu        iddhiyā yasasā jalaṃ    
mahānubhāvo bhātā me       kaññāhi parivārito .

So tena pariciṇṇo tvaṃ       sabbakāmehi tappito    
aduṭṭhassa tuvaṃ dubbhi          tante veraṃ idhāgataṃ .

Khippaṃ gīvaṃ pasārehi          na te dassāmi jīvitaṃ    
bhātu parisaraṃ veraṃ           chedayissāmi te siraṃ .

[758] ajjhāyako yācayogī         āhutaggi ca brāhmaṇo    
etehi tīhi ṭhānehi         avajjho bhavati [^257-6] brāhmaṇo .

[759] yaṃ puraṃ dhataraṭṭhassa           ogāḷhaṃ yamunaṃ nadiṃ    
#[^257-1] sī. Yu. Kaṃsassa .   [^257-2] sī. Yu. Ohātuṃ .   [^257-3] ma. Dūramacari . Sī. Yu. Dūramasarā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 258 -->
<a id="page-258"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 258]*

Jotate sabbasovaṇṇaṃ         girimāhacca yāmunaṃ .

Tattha te purisabyagghā        sodariyā mama bhātaro    
yathā te tattha vakkhanti       tathā hessasi brāhmaṇa .

[760] anittarā ittarasampayuttā    
yaññā ca vedā ca subhoga loke    
tadaggarayhaṃ hi vinindamāno    
jahāti vittañca satañca dhammaṃ .

[761] ajjhenamariyā paṭhaviṃ janindā    
vessā kasiṃ pāricariyañca suddā    
upāgū paccekaṃ yathāpadesaṃ    
katāhu ete vasināti āhu .

[762] dhātā vidhātā varuṇo kuvero    
somo yamo candimā vāyu suriyo    
etepi yaññaṃ puthuso yajitvā    
ajjhāyakānaṃ atha [^258-1] sabbakāme .

Vikāsitā cāpasatāni pañca    
yo ajjuno balavā bhīmaseno    
sahassabāhu asamo paṭhabyā    
sopi tadā ādahi [^258-2] jātavedaṃ .

[763] yo brāhmaṇe bhojayi dīgharattaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 259 -->
<a id="page-259"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 259]*

Annena pānena yathānubhāvaṃ    
pasannacitto anumodamāno    
subhoga devaññataro ahosi .

[764] mahāsanaṃ devamanomavaṇṇaṃ    
yo sappinā asakkhi bhojetumaggiṃ    
so yaññatantaṃ varato yajitvā    
dibbaṃ gatiṃ mucalindajjhagacchi .

[765] mahānubhāvo vassasahassakhīvī    
yo pabbaji dassaneyyo uḷāro    
hitvā apariyantaṃ raṭṭhaṃ [^259-1] sasenaṃ    
rājā dudīpopajjhagāmi [^259-2] saggaṃ .

[766] yo sāgaro sāgarantaṃ vijitvā    
yūpaṃ subhaṃ sovaṇṇamayaṃ [^259-3] uḷāraṃ    
ussesi vessānaramādahāno    
subhoga devaññataro ahosi .

Yassānubhāvena subhoga gaṅgā    
pavattati [^259-4] dadhisanninnaṃ samuddaṃ    
so lomapādo paricariyamaggiṃ    
aṅgo sahassakkhapuramajjhagacchi .

[767] mahiddhiko devavaro yasassī  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 260 -->
<a id="page-260"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 260]*

Senāpati tidive vāsavassa    
so somayāgena malaṃ vihantvā    
subhoga devaññataro ahosi .

[768] akārayi lokamimaṃ parañca    
bhāgīrathiṃ himavantañca vijjhaṃ [^260-1]    
yo iddhimā devavaro yasassī    
sopi tadā ādahi jātavedaṃ .

Mālāgiri himavā yo ca vijjhā [^260-2]    
sudassano nisabho kākaveru [^260-3]    
ete ca aññe ca nagā mahantā    
cityā katā yaññakarebhi māhu .

[769] ajjhāyakaṃ mantaguṇūpapannaṃ    
tapassinaṃ yācayogītidhāhu [^260-4]    
tīre samuddassudakaṃ sajantaṃ    
taṃ sāgarajjhohari tenapeyyo .

[770] āyāgavatthūni puthū paṭhabyā    
saṃvijjanti brāhmaṇā vāsavassa    
purimaṃ disaṃ pacchimaṃ dakkhiṇuttaraṃ    
saṃvijjamānā janayanti vedaṃ .

[771] kalīhi dhīrāna kataṃ magānaṃ    
#[^260-1] ma. Gijjhaṃ .   [^260-2] ma. Gijjho .   [^260-3] ma. Kuveru . Sī. Yu. Kākaneru .   [^260-4] ma. ...


<!-- Siam Rath Page 261 -->
<a id="page-261"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 261]*

Bhavanti vedajjhagatā ariṭṭha    
marīcidhammaṃ asamekkhitattā    
māyāguṇā nātivahanti paññaṃ .

Vedā na tāṇāya bhavantirassa [^261-1]    
mittadduno bhūnahuno narassa    
na tāyate pariciṇṇova [^261-2] aggi    
dosantaraṃ maccamanariyakammaṃ .

Sabbañca maccā sadhanā sabhogā [^261-3]    
ādīpitaṃ dāru tiṇena missaṃ    
dahaṃ na tappe asamatthatejo    
ko taṃ subhikkhaṃ dirasaññu kayirā .

Yathāpi khīraṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ    
dadhi bhavitvā navanītaṃpi hoti    
evaṃpi aggi vipariṇāmadhammo    
tejo samārūhati yogayutto .

Na dissati aggimanuppaviṭṭho    
sukkhesu kaṭṭhesu navesu cāpi    
nāmatthamāno [^261-4] araṇīnarena    
nākammunā jāyati jātavedo .

Sace hi aggi antarato vaseyya  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 262 -->
<a id="page-262"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 262]*

Sukkhesu kaṭṭhesu navesu cāpi    
sabbāni susseyyuṃ vanāni loke    
sukkhāni kaṭṭhāni ca pajjaleyyuṃ .

Karoti ce dārutiṇena puññaṃ    
bhojaṃ naro dhūmasikhiṃ patāpavaṃ    
aṅgārikā loṇakarā ca sūdā    
sarīradāhāpi kareyyu puññaṃ .

Atha ce hi etepi karonti puññaṃ    
ajjhenamaggiṃ idha tappayitvā    
na koci lokasmiṃ karoti puññaṃ    
bhojaṃ naro dhūmasikhiṃ patāpavaṃ .

Kathaṃ hi lokāpacito samāno    
amanuññagandhaṃ bahūnaṃ akantaṃ    
yadeva maccā parivajjayanti    
tadappasaṭṭhaṃ dirasaññu bhuñje .

Sikhiñhi [^262-1] devesu vadanti eke    
āpaṃ milakkhū [^262-2] pana devamāhu    
sabbeva ete vitathaṃ bhaṇanti    
aggi na devaññataro na āpo .

Anindriyaṃ santamasaññakāyaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 263 -->
<a id="page-263"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 263]*

Vessānaraṃ kammakaraṃ pajānaṃ    
paricariyamaggiṃ sugatiṃ kathaṃ vajje    
pāpāni kammāni pakrubbamāno [^263-1] .

Sabbābhibhūtāhudha jīvikatthā    
aggissa brahmā paricārikoti    
sabbānubhāvī ca vasī kimatthaṃ    
animmito nimmitaṃ vanditassa .

Hāsaṃ [^263-2] anijjhānakkhamaṃ atacchaṃ    
sakkārahetu parikariṃsu pubbe    
te lābhasakkāre apātubhūte [^263-3]    
sandhābhitā [^263-4] jantubhi santidhammaṃ .

Ajjhenamariyā paṭhaviṃ janindā    
vessā kasiṃ pāricariyañca suddā    
upāgū paccekaṃ yathāpadesaṃ    
katāhu ete vasināti āhu .

Etañca saccaṃ vacanaṃ bhaveyya    
yathā idaṃ bhāsitaṃ brāhmaṇehi    
nākhattiyo jātu labhetha rajjaṃ    
nābrāhmaṇo mantapadāni sikkhe    
nāññatra vessā hi kasiṃ kareyya    
#[^263-1] ma. Pakubbamāno .    [^263-2] ma. Hassaṃ .    [^263-3] ma. Apātubhonte .   [^263-4] ma. Sandhāpitā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 264 -->
<a id="page-264"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 264]*

Suddo na muñce parapesanāya [^264-1] .

Yasmā ca etaṃ vacanaṃ abhūtaṃ    
musāvime odariyā bhaṇanti    
tadappapaññā abhisaddahanti    
passanti taṃ paṇḍitā attanāva .

Khatyā hi vessānaṃ [^264-2] baliṃ haranti    
ādāya satthāni caranti brāhmaṇā    
taṃ tādisaṃ saṅkhubhitaṃ pabhinnaṃ    
kasmā brahmā na ujuṃ karoti lokaṃ .

Sace hi so issaro sabbaloke    
brahmāpahū bhūtapatī [^264-3] pajānaṃ    
kiṃ sabbalokaṃ vidahi alakkhiṃ    
kiṃ sabbalokaṃ na sukhiṃ akāsi .

Sace hi so issaro sabbaloke    
brahmāpahū bhūtapatī pajānaṃ    
māyā musāvajjamadena cāpi    
lokaṃ adhammena kimatthakāri [^264-4] .

Sace hi so issaro sabbaloke  
brahmāpahū bhūtapatī pajānaṃh  
#[^264-1] sī. Yu. Parapessitāya .   [^264-2] sī. Yu. Khattā na vessāna .   [^264-3] ma. Brāhmā็  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 265 -->
<a id="page-265"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 265]*

Adhammiko bhūtapatī ariṭṭha    
dhamme satī so vidahī adhammaṃ .

Kīṭā paṭaṅgā uragā ca bheṅgā [^265-1]    
hantvā kimiṃ sujjhati makkhikā ca    
ete hi dhammā anarīyarūpā    
kambojakānaṃ vitathā bahūnaṃ .

[772] sace hi so sujjhati yo hanāti    
hatopi so saggamupeti ṭhānaṃ    
bhovādi bhovādinamārayeyyuṃ    
ye vāpi tesaṃ abhisaddaheyyuṃ .

Neva migā na pasunopi gāvo    
āyācanti attavadhāya keci    
vipphandamānā idha jīvikatthā    
yaññesu pāṇe pasumārabhanti [^265-2] .

Yūpassa ne [^265-3] pasubandhe ca bālā    
citrehi vaṇṇehi mukhaṃ nayanti    
ayante yūpo kāmaduho parattha    
bhavissati sassato samparāye .

Sace ca yūpe maṇisaṅkhamuttaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 266 -->
<a id="page-266"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 266]*

Dhaññaṃ dhanaṃ rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ    
sukkhesu kaṭṭhesu navesu cāpi    
sace duhe tidive sabbakāme    
tevijjasaṅghāva puthū yajeyyuṃ    
na brāhmaṇā [^266-1] kiñci na yājayeyyuṃ .

Kuto ca yūpe maṇisaṅkhamuttaṃ    
dhaññaṃ dhanaṃ rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ    
sukkhesu kaṭṭhesu navesu cāpi    
kuto duhe tidive sabbakāme .

Saṭhā ca luddā ca paluddhabālā [^266-2]    
citrehi [^266-3] vaṇṇehi mukhaṃ nayanti    
ādāya aggiṃ mama dehi vittaṃ    
tato sukhī hohisi sabbakāme .

Tamaggihuttaṃ saraṇaṃ pavissa    
citrehi [^266-3] vaṇṇehi mukhaṃ nayanti    
oropayitvā kesamassuṃ nakhañca    
vedehi vittaṃ atigāḷhayanti .

Kākā ulūkaṃva raho labhitvā  
ekaṃ samānaṃ bahukā samecca  
annāni bhutvā kuhakā kuhitvā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 267 -->
<a id="page-267"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 267]*

Muṇḍaṃ karitvā yaññapathosajanti [^267-1] .

Evaṃ hi so vañcito brāhmaṇehi    
eko samāno bahukā samecca    
te yogayogena vilumpamānā    
diṭṭhaṃ adiṭṭhena dhanaṃ haranti .

Akāsiyā rājūhi vānusiṭṭhā    
tadassa ādāya dhanaṃ haranti    
te tādisā corasamā asantā    
vajjhā na haññanti ariṭṭha loke .

Indassa bāhārasi dakkhiṇāti    
yaññesu chindanti palāsayaṭṭhiṃ    
tañcepi saccaṃ maghavā chinnabāhu    
kenassa indo asure jināti .

Tañceva tucchaṃ maghavā samaṅgī    
hantvā avajjho paramo sudevo [^267-2]    
mantā ime brāhmaṇā tuccharūpā    
sandiṭṭhikā vañcanā esa loke .

Mālāgiri himavā yo ca vijjhā  
sudassano nisabho kākaveru [^267-3]  
ete ca aññe ca nagā mahantā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 268 -->
<a id="page-268"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 268]*

Cityā katā yaññakarebhimāhu .

Yathāpakārāni hi iṭṭhakāni    
cityā katā yaññakarebhimāhu    
na pabbatā honti tathāpakārā    
aññādisā acalā diṭṭhaselā .

Na iṭṭhakā honti silā cirena    
na tattha sañjāyati ayo na lohaṃ    
yaññañca etaṃ parivaṇṇayantā    
cityā katā yaññakarebhimāhu .

Ajjhāyakaṃ mantaguṇūpapannaṃ    
tapassinaṃ yācayogītidhāhu [^268-1]    
tīre samuddassudakaṃ sajantaṃ    
taṃ sāgarajjhohari [^268-2] tenapeyyo .

Parosahassaṃpi samantavede  
mantūpapanne nadiyo vahanti  
na tena byāpannarasūdakāni  
kasmā samuddo atulo apeyyo .ฮ  
yekeci kūpā idha jīvaloke  
loṇūdakā kūpakhaṇehi khātā  
na brāhmaṇajjhoharaṇena tesu  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 269 -->
<a id="page-269"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 269]*

Āpo apeyyo dirasaññurāhu [^269-1] .

Pure puratthā kā kassa bhariyā    
mano manussaṃ ajanesi pubbe    
tenāpi dhammena na koci hīno    
evampi vossaggavibhaṅgamāhu .

Caṇḍālaputtopi adhicca vede    
bhāseyya mante kusalo matīmā [^269-2]    
na tassa muddhā viphaleyya sattadhā    
mantā ime attavadhāya katā [^269-3] .

Vācā katā giddhi katā gahitā    
dummocayā kābyāpathānupannā    
bālāna cittaṃ visame niviṭṭhaṃ    
tadappapaññā abhisaddahanti .

Sīhassa byagghassa ca dīpino ca    
na vijjatī porisiyaṃbalena    
manussabhāvo ca gavaṃva pekkho    
jātī hi tesaṃ asamā samānaṃ [^269-4] .

Sace ca rājā paṭhaviṃ vijitvā  
sajīvavā assavā pārisajjo  
sayameva so sattusaṅghaṃ vijeyya  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 270 -->
<a id="page-270"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 270]*

Tassappajā nicca sukhī bhaveyya .

Khattiyamantā ca tayo ca vedā    
atthena ete samakā bhavanti    
tesañca atthaṃ avinicchinitvā    
na bujjhati oghapathaṃva channaṃ .

Khattiyamantā ca tayo ca vedā    
atthena ete samakā bhavanti    
lābho alābho yaso ayaso ca    
sabbeva tesaṃ catunnañca [^270-1] dhammā .

Yathāpi ibbhā dhanadhaññahetu    
kammāni kārenti [^270-2] puthū paṭhabyā    
tevijjasaṅghāpi tatheva ajja    
kammāni kārenti [^270-2] puthū paṭhabyā .

Ibbhehi ete samakā bhavanti    
niccussukā kāmaguṇesu yuttā    
kammāni kārenti [^270-2] puthū paṭhabyā    
tadappapaññā dirasaññurā te .

[773] kassa bherī mudiṅgā ca        saṅkhā paṇavadindimā  
purato paṭipannāni           hāsayantā rathesabhaṃ .[^270-5]  
kassa kañcanapaṭṭena          puthunā vijjuvaṇṇinā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 271 -->
<a id="page-271"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 271]*

Yuvā kalāpasannaddho         ko eti siriyā jalaṃ .

Ukkāmukhe pahaṭṭhaṃva          khadiraṅgārasannibhaṃ    
mukhaṃ cārurivābhāti           ko eti siriyā jalaṃ .

Kassa jambonadaṃ chattaṃ         sasalākaṃ manoramaṃ    
ādiccaraṃsāvaraṇaṃ            ko eti siriyā jalaṃ .

Kassa aṅgapariggayha [^271-1]       vālavījanimuttamaṃ    
ubhato varapaññassa [^271-2]        muddhani uparūpari .

Kassa pekkhuṇahatthāni         citrāni ca mudūni ca    
suvaṇṇamaṇidaṇḍāni [^271-3]         caranti ubhatomukhaṃ .

Khadiraṅgāravaṇṇābhā          ukkāmukhapahaṃsitā    
kassete kuṇḍalā vaggū       sobhanti ubhatomukhaṃ .

Kassa vātena chupitā         siniddhaggā dumu kāḷikā [^271-4]    
sobhayanti nalātantaṃ          nabhā vijjurivuggatā .

Kassa etāni akkhīni         āyatāni puthūni ca    
ko sobhati visālakkho        kassetaṃ uṇṇajaṃ mukhaṃ .

Kassete lapanajātā         suddhā saṅkhavarūpamā    
bhāsamānassa sobhanti         dantā kuppilasādisā .

Kassa lākhārasasamā          hatthapādā sukhe ṭhitā    
ko so bimboṭṭhasampanno     divā suriyova sobhati .

Himaccaye himavato [^271-5]        brahāsālova [^271-6] pupphito    
#[^271-1] ma. Aṅgaṃ pariggayha . Sī. Yu. Aṅkaṃ ... .   [^271-2] sī. Yu. Carate varapaññassa .   [^271-3] ma.

#kāñcanamaṇidaṇḍāni . Sī. Yu. Tapaññamaṇidaṇḍāni .    [^271-4] ma. Niddhantā mudukāḷakā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 272 -->
<a id="page-272"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 272]*

Ko so odātapāvāro      jayaṃ indova sobhati .

Suvaṇṇapīḷakākiṇṇaṃ            maṇidaṇḍavicittakaṃ    
ko so parisamoggayha        aṃse [^272-1] khaggaṃ pamuñcati .

Suvaṇṇavikatā citrā          sukatā citrasibbinī [^272-2]    
ko so omuñcate pādā     namo katvā mahesino .

[774] dhataraṭṭhassa te nāgā [^272-3]     iddhimanto yasassino    
samuddajāya uppannā         ete nāgā mahiddhikāti .

Bhūridattajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ .

[^272-7] candakumārajātakaṃ [^272-4]    
[775] rājāsi luddakakammo    
ekarājā pupphavatitāyaṃ [^272-5]    
so pucchi brahmabandhuṃ    
khaṇḍahālaṃ purohitaṃ muḷhaṃ    
saggānamaggamācikkha [^272-6]    
tvamasi brāhmaṇa dhammavinayakusalo    
yathā ito vajanti sugatiṃ    
narā puññāni katvāna .

[776] atidānaṃ dadatvāna [^272-7]    
#[^272-1] ma. Īsaṃ . Sī. Yu. Īso khaggaṃva .     [^272-2] ma. Cittasibbanā .   [^272-3] ma. Dhataraṭṭhā hi    
#te nāgā .   [^272-4] sī. Yu. Khaṇḍahālajātakaṃ .    [^272-5] ma. Pupaphavatīyā .   [^272-6] sī. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 273 -->
<a id="page-273"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 273]*

Avajjhe deva ghātetvā    
evaṃ vajanti sugatiṃ    
narā puññāni katvāna .

[777] kiṃ panetaṃ [^273-1] atidānaṃ    
ke ca avajjhā imasmiṃ lokasmiṃ    
etañca kho no akkhāhi    
yajissāmi dadāmi [^273-2] dānāni .

[778] puttehi deva yajitabbaṃ    
mahesīhi negamehi ca    
usabhehi ājāniyehi catūhi    
sabbacatukkena deva yajitabbaṃ .

[779] taṃ sutvā antepure    
kumārā mahesiyo ca haññantu    
eko ahosi nigghoso    
bhesmā [^273-3] accuggato saddo .

[780] gacchatha vadetha kumāre    
candañca suriyañca bhaddasenañca    
surañca vāmagottañca    
pacurā [^273-4] kira hotha yaññatthāya .

[781] kumāriyopi vadetha  
#[^273-1] ma. Pana taṃ .    [^273-2] sī. Yu. Yajissāma dadāma .   [^273-3] ma. Bhismā .   [^273-4] sī. Yu. Pasurā .้  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 274 -->
<a id="page-274"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 274]*

Upasenañca kokilañca    
muditañca nandañcāpi kumārikaṃ    
pacurā kira hotha yaññatthāya .

[782] vijayampi mayhaṃ mahesiṃ    
erāvatiṃ [^274-1] kesiniṃ sunandañca    
lakkhaṇavarūpapannā    
pacurā kira hotha yaññatthāya .

[783] gahapatayopi [^274-2] vadetha    
puṇṇamukhaṃ bhaddiyaṃ siṅgālañca    
vaḍḍhañcāpi gahapatiṃ    
pacurā kira hotha yaññatthāya .

[784] te tattha gahapatayo avocayiṃsu    
samāgatā puttadāraparikiṇṇā    
sabbe [^274-3] sikhino deva karohi    
athavā no dāse sāvehi .

[785] abhayaṅkarampi me hatthiṃ        nāḷāgiriṃ accuggataṃ (varuṇadantaṃ)    
ānetha kho te khippaṃ        yaññatthāya bhavissati .

Assaratanampi me [^274-4] kesiṃ     surāmukhaṃ [^274-5] puṇṇakaṃ vinatakañca    
ānetha kho te khippaṃ        yaññatthāya bhavissati .

#[^274-1] yu. Ekapatiṃ .     [^274-2] ma. Ca .   [^274-3] ma. Sabbeva .   [^274-4] sī. Ma. Yu. Ayaṃ pāṭho natthi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 275 -->
<a id="page-275"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 275]*

Usabhampi me yūthapatiṃ    
anojaṃ nisabhaṃ gavampatiṃ    
tepi mayhaṃ ānetha sammukhā [^275-1] karontu sabbaṃ    
yajissāmi dadāmi dānāni .

Sabbaṃpi paṭiyādetha    
yaññaṃ pana uggatamhi suriyamhi    
āṇāpetha candakumāre [^275-2]    
abhiramantu imaṃ rattiṃ .

Sabbaṃpi upaṭṭhapetha    
yaññaṃ pana uggatamhi suriyamhi    
vadethadāni kumāre    
ajja kho pacchimā ratti .

[786] taṃ taṃ mātā avoca          rodantī āgatā [^275-3] vimānato [^275-4]    
yañño kira te putta         bhavissati catūhi puttehi .

[787] sabbepi mayhaṃ puttā cattā    candasmiṃ haññamānasmiṃ    
puttehi yaññaṃ yajitvāna       sugatiṃ saggaṃ gamissāmi .

[788] mā taṃ putta saddahesi        sugati kira hoti puttayaññena    
nirayāneso maggo          neso maggo hi saggānaṃ .

Dānāni dehi koṇḍañña       ahiṃsā sabbabhūtabhabyānaṃ    
esa maggo sugatiyā         na ca maggo puttayaññena .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 276 -->
<a id="page-276"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 276]*

[789] ācariyānaṃ vacanā           ghātissaṃ [^276-1] candañca suriyañca    
puttehi yajitvā [^276-2] duccajehi  sugatiṃ saggaṃ gamissāmi .

[790] taṃ taṃ pitāpi avaca           vasavatti orasaṃ sakaṃ puttaṃ    
yañño kira te putta         bhavissati catūhi puttehi .

[791] sabbepi mayhaṃ puttā cattā    candasmiṃ haññamānasmiṃ    
puttehi yaññaṃ yajitvāna       sugatiṃ saggaṃ gamissāmi .

[792] mā taṃ putta saddahesi        sugati kira hoti puttayaññena    
nirayāneso maggo          neso maggo hi saggānaṃ .

Dānāni dehi koṇḍañña       ahiṃsā sabbabhūtabhabyānaṃ    
esa maggo sugatiyā         na ca maggo puttayaññena .

[793] ācariyānaṃ vacanā           ghātissaṃ [^276-1] candañca suriyañca    
puttehi yajitvā [^276-2] duccajehi  sugatiṃ saggaṃ gamissāmi .

[794] dānāni dehi koṇḍañña       ahiṃsā sabbabhūtabhabyānaṃ    
puttaparivuto tuvaṃ            raṭṭhaṃ janapadañca pālehi .

[795] mā no deva avadhi          dāse no dehi khaṇḍahālassa    
api nigaḷabandhakāpi           hatthī asse ca pālema .

Mā no deva avadhi          dāse no dehi khaṇḍahālassa    
api nigaḷabandhakāpi           hatthichakaṇāni ujjhema .

Mā no deva avadhi          dāse no dehi khaṇḍahālassa    
api nigaḷabandhakāpi           assachakaṇāni ujjhema .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 277 -->
<a id="page-277"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 277]*

Mā no deva avadhi          dāse no dehi khaṇḍahālassa    
yassa honti tava kāmā       api raṭṭhā pabbājitā    
bhikkhācariyaṃ carissāma .

[796] dukkhaṃ kho me janayittha [^277-1]     vilapantā jīvitassa kāmā hi    
muñcathadāni kumāre          alaṃpi me hotu puttayaññena .

[797] pubbeva khosi me vutto      dukkaraṃ durabhisambhavaṃ cetaṃ    
atha no upakkhatassa yaññassa    kasmā karosi vikkhepaṃ .

Sabbe vajanti sugatiṃ          ye yajanti yepi ceva yajāpenti    
ye cāpi anumodanti         yajantānaṃ edisaṃ mahāyaññaṃ .

[798] atha kissa ca no [^277-2] pubbe    sotthānaṃ brāhmaṇe avācesi    
atha no akāraṇasmā         yaññatthāya deva ghātesi .

Pubbeva no daharakāle       na hanesi na ghātesi    
daharamhā yobbanappattā      adūsakā tāta haññāma  .

Hatthigate assagate          sannaddhe passa no mahārāja    
yuddhe vā yujjhamāne vā     nahi mādisā [3] honti yaññatthāya .

Paccante vā kuppite        aṭavīsu vā mādise niyojenti    
atha no akāraṇasmā         abhūmiyaṃ tāta haññāma .

Yākāci sakuṇikāyo [^277-4]       vasanti tiṇagharāni katvāna    
tāsaṃpi piyā puttā          atha no tvaṃ deva ghātesi .

Mā khvassa [^277-5] saddahesi      na maṃ khaṇḍahālo ghāteyya  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 278 -->
<a id="page-278"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 278]*

Mamañhi so ghātetvāna       anantarā tampi deva ghāteyya .

Gāmavaraṃ nigamavaraṃ dadanti       bhogaṃpissa mahārāja    
athaggapiṇḍikāpi             kule kule hete bhuñjanti .

Tesaṃpi tādisānaṃ            icchanti dubbhituṃ mahārāja    
yebhuyyena hi ete         akataññuno brāhmaṇā deva .

Mā no deva avadhi          dāse no dehi khaṇḍahālassa    
api nigaḷabandhakāpi           hatthī asse ca pālema .

Mā no deva avadhi          dāse no dehi khaṇḍahālassa    
api nigaḷabandhakāpi           hatthichakaṇāni ujjhema .

Mā no deva avadhi          dāse no dehi khaṇḍahālassa    
api nigaḷabandhakāpi           assachakaṇāni ujjhema .

Mā no deva avadhi          dāse no dehi khaṇḍahālassa    
yassa honti tava kāmā       api raṭṭhā pabbājitā    
bhikkhācariyaṃ carissāma .

[799] dukkhaṃ kho me janayittha [^278-1]     vilapantā jīvitassa kāmā hi    
muñcathadāni [^278-2] kumāre       alaṃpi me hoti puttayaññena .

[800] pubbeva khosi me vutto      dukkaraṃ durabhisambhavaṃ cetaṃ    
atha no upakkhatassa yaññassa    kasmā karosi vikkhepaṃ .

Sabbe vajanti sugatiṃ          ye yajanti yepi ceva yajāpenti [^278-3]    
ye cāpi anumodanti         yajantānaṃ edisaṃ mahāyaññaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 279 -->
<a id="page-279"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 279]*

[801] yadi kira yajitvā puttehi       devalokaṃ ito cutā yanti    
brāhmaṇo tāva yajatu         pacchāpi yajissasi rāja [^279-1] .

Yadi kira yajitvā puttehi       devalokaṃ ito cutā yanti    
eseva khaṇḍahālo           yajatu sakehi puttehi .

Evaṃ jānaṃ ce [^279-2] khaṇḍahālo   kiṃ puttake na ghātesi    
sabbañca ñātijanaṃ             attānañca na ghātesi .

Sabbe vajanti nirayaṃ           ye yajanti yepi ceva yajāpenti    
ye cāpi anumodanti          yajantānaṃ edisaṃ mahāyaññaṃ .

[3]    
[802] kathañca kira puttakāmāyo    
gahapatayo gharaṇiyo ca    
nagaramhi na upavadanti rājānaṃ    
mā ghātayi orasaṃ puttaṃ .

Kathañca kira puttakāmāyo    
gahapatayo gharaṇiyo ca    
nagaramhi na upavadanti rājānaṃ    
mā ghātayi atrajaṃ puttaṃ .

Rañño camhi atthakāmo    
hito ca sabbajanapadassa [^279-4]    
na koci assa paṭighaṃ mayā    
#[^279-1] sī. Pacchāpi yakhissate rājā . Ma. Pacchāpi yajasi tuvaṃ rājā .  [^279-2] ma. Jānanto .

#[^279-3] ma. Sace hi so sjjhati yo hanāti   hatopi so saggamupeti ṭhānaṃ    
#         bhovādi bhovādina mārayeyyuṃ      ye cāpi tesaṃ abhisaddaheyyuṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 280 -->
<a id="page-280"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 280]*

Janapado  na  pavedeti .

[803] gacchatha vo gharaṇiyo          tātañca vadetha khaṇḍahālañca    
mā ghātetha kumāre         adūsake sīhasaṅkāse    
gacchatha vo gharaṇiyo          tātañca vadetha khaṇḍahālañca    
mā ghātetha kumāre         apekkhite sabbalokassa .

[804] yannūnāhaṃ jāyeyyaṃ rathakārakule [^280-1] vā    
pukkusakule [^280-2] vā vessesu vā jāyeyyaṃ    
na hajja maṃ rājā yaññe [^280-3] ghāteyya .

[805] sabbāpi mantiniyo [^280-4]        gacchatha ayyassa khaṇḍahālassa    
pādesu nipatatha             aparādhāhaṃ na passāmi .

Sabbāpi gharaṇiyo [^280-5]         gacchatha ayyassa khaṇḍahālassa    
pādesu nipatatha             kinte bhante mayaṃ adūsema .

[806] kapaṇā [^280-6] vilapati selā      disvāna bhātaro upanītatte    
yañño kira me ukkhipito      tātena saggakāmena .

[807] āvatti ca parivatti ca        vasulo sammukhā rañño    
mā no deva pitaraṃ avadhi      daharamhā ayobbanappattā .

[808] eso te vasula pitā        samehi pitarā saha    
dukkhaṃ kho me janayasi         vilapanto antepurasmiṃ    
muñcathadāni kumāre          alaṃpi me hotu puttayaññena .

[809] pubbeva khosi me vutto      dukkaraṃ durabhisambhavaṃ cetaṃ    
#[^280-1] ma. Rathakārakulesu vā .   [^280-2] ma. Pukkusakulesu vā .   [^280-3] sī. Yu. Yaññatthāya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 281 -->

##### *[Siam Rath Page 281]*

Atha no upakkhatassa yaññassa    kasmā karosi vikkhepaṃ    
sabbe vajanti sugatiṃ          ye yajanti yepi ceva yajāpenti    
ye cāpi anumodanti         yajantānaṃ edisaṃ mahāyaññaṃ .

[810] sabbaratanassa yañño upakkhato   ekarāja tava paṭiyatto    
abhinikkhamassu deva           saggaṃ gato tvaṃ pamodissasi .

[811] daharā sattasatā etā       candakumārassa bhariyāyo    
kese vikiritvāna           rodantiyo maggamanuyāyanti [^281-1] .

Aparā pana sokena          nikkhantā nandane viya devā    
kesā vikiritvāna [^281-2]        rodantiyo maggamanuyāyanti [^281-3] .

[812] kāsikasucivatthadharā           kuṇḍalino aggalucandanavilittā

Nīyanti candasuriyā           janassa katvā hadayasokaṃ .

tyajja candasuriyā           ubhova pattikā yanti .

tyajja candasuriyā           ubhova pattikā yanti .

tedāni ayyaputtā          cattāro vadhāya ninnītā .

tedāni ayyaputtā          cattāro vadhāya ninnītā .

##### *[Siam Rath Page 285]*

Tathā hessati pupphavatiyā      yiṭṭhesu candasuriyesu .

[816] ummattikā bhavissāmi         bhūnahatā paṃsunāva [^285-1] parikiṇṇā    
sace candakumāraṃ [^285-2] hanti     pāṇā me deva bhijjanti [^285-3] .

Ummattikā bhavissāmi         bhūnahatā paṃsunāva [^285-1] parikiṇṇā    
sace suriyakumāraṃ hanti        pāṇā me deva bhijjanti [^285-4] .

[817] kinnumā na ramāpeyyuṃ        aññamaññaṃ piyaṃvadā    
ghaṭṭikā uparikkhī ca          pokkharaṇī ca gāyikā    
candasuriyesu naccantiyo       samā tāsaṃ na vijjati .

[818] imaṃ mayhaṃ hadayasokaṃ          paṭimuñcatu khaṇḍahāla tava mātā    
yo mayhaṃ hadayasoko         candasmiṃ vadhāya ninnīte .

Imaṃ mayhaṃ hayadasokaṃ          paṭimuñcatu khaṇḍahāla tava mātā    
yo mayhaṃ hadayasoko         suriyasmiṃ vadhāya ninnīte .

Imaṃ mayhaṃ hadayasokaṃ          paṭimuñcatu khaṇḍahāla tava jāyā    
yo mayhaṃ hadayasoko         candasmiṃ vadhāya ninnīte .

Imaṃ mayhaṃ hadayasokaṃ          paṭimuñcatu khaṇḍahāla tava jāyā    
yo mayhaṃ hadayasoko         suriyasmiṃ vadhāya ninnīte .

Mā ca putte mā ca patiṃ      addakkhi khaṇḍahāla tava mātā    
yo ghātesi kumāre         adūsake sīhasaṅkāse .

Mā ca putte mā ca patiṃ      addakkhi khaṇḍahāla tava mātā    
yo ghātesi kumāre         apekkhite sabbalokassa .

##### *[Siam Rath Page 286]*

Mā ca putte mā ca patiṃ      addakkhi khaṇḍahāla tava jāyā    
yo ghātesi kumāre         adūsake sīhasaṅkāse .

Mā ca putte mā ca patiṃ      addakkhi khaṇḍahāla tava jāyā    
yo ghātesi kumāre         apekkhite sabbalokassa .

[819] mā no deva avadhi          dāse no dehi khaṇḍahālassa    
api nigaḷabandhakāpi           hatthī asse ca pālema .

Mā no deva avadhi          dāse no dehi khaṇḍahālassa    
api nigaḷabandhakāpi           hatthichakaṇāni ujjhema .

Mā no deva avadhi          dāse no dehi khaṇḍahālassa    
api nigaḷabandhakāpi           assachakaṇāni ujjhema .

Mā no deva avadhi          dāse no dehi khaṇḍahālassa    
yassa honti tava kāmā       api raṭṭhā pabbājitā    
bhikkhācariyaṃ carissāma .

Dibyaṃ deva upayācanti        puttatthikā daliddāpi    
paṭibhāṇānipi hitvā          putte na labhanti ekaccā    
āsāsikāni [^286-1] karonti      puttā no jāyantu tato paputtā    
atha no akāraṇasmā         yaññatthāya deva ghātesi .

Upayācitakena puttaṃ labhanti     mā tāta no aghātesi    
mā kicchaladdhakehi puttehi     yajittho imaṃ yaññaṃ .

Upayācitakena puttaṃ labhanti     mā tāta no aghātesi  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 287 -->
<a id="page-287"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 287]*

Mā kapaṇaladdhakehi puttehi     ammāya no vippavāsesi .

[820] bahudukkhaṃ [^287-1] posiyā candaṃ     amma tuvaṃ jīyase puttaṃ    
vandāmi kho te pāde       labhataṃ tāto paralokaṃ    
handa ca maṃ upagūha [^287-2]        pāde te amma vandituṃ dehi    
gacchāmidāni pavāsaṃ          yaññatthāya ekarājassa .

Handa ca maṃ upagūha [^287-3]        pāde te amma vandituṃ dehi    
gacchāmidāni pavāsaṃ          mātu katvā hadayasokaṃ .

Handa ca maṃ upagūha [^287-4]        pāde te amma vandituṃ dehi    
gacchāmidāni pavāsaṃ          janassa katvā hadayasokaṃ .

[821] handa ca padumapattānaṃ         moliṃ bandhassu gotamiputta    
campakadalamissāyo           esā te porāṇikā pakati .

Handa ca vilepanante         pacchimakaṃ candanaṃ vilimpassu    
yehi ca suvilitto           sobhasi rājaparisāyaṃ .

Handa ca mudukāni vatthāni      pacchimaṃ kāsikaṃ nivāsehi    
yehi ca sunivattho           sobhasi rājaparisāyaṃ .

Muttāmaṇikanakavibhūsitāni        gaṇhassu hatthābharaṇāni    
yehi ca hatthābharaṇehi        sobhasi rājaparisāyaṃ .

[822] na hi nūnāyaṃ raṭṭhapālo       bhūmipati janapadassa dāyādo    
lokissaro mahanto          putte sinehaṃ janayati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 288 -->
<a id="page-288"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 288]*

[823] mayhaṃ piyā [^288-1] puttā athopi   piyā [^288-2] tumhe ca bhariyāyo    
saggañca patthayamāno [^288-3]      tenāhaṃ ghātayissāmi .

[824] maṃ paṭhamaṃ ghātehi            mā me hadayaṃ dukkhaṃ aphālesi    
alaṅkato sundarako putto     deva tava sukhumālo .

Handayya maṃ hanassu           sasokā [^288-4] candakena [^288-5] hessāmi    
puññaṃ karassu vipulaṃ           vicarāma ubho paraloke .

[825] mā tvaṃ cande rucci (maraṇaṃ)   bahukā tava devarā visālakkhi    
te taṃ ramayissanti           yiṭṭhasmiṃ gotamiputte .

[826] evaṃ vutte candā attānaṃ    hanti hatthatalakehi    
alamettha [^288-6] jīvitena        pissāmi visaṃ marissāmi .

Na hi nūnimassa rañño         ñātimittā [^288-7] ca vijjare    
suhadā yena vadanti rājānaṃ    mā ghātayi orase putte    
na hi nūnimassa rañño         ñātimittā [^288-7] ca vijjare

Vilasatampi maṃ katvā          yajassu sattadhā mahārāja    
mā jeṭṭhaputtaṃ avadhi         adūsakaṃ sīhasaṅkāsaṃ .

<!-- Siam Rath Page 290 -->
<a id="page-290"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 290]*

Niyyātha candasuriyā          janassa katvā hadayasokaṃ .

[829] sabbasmiṃ upakkhatasmiṃ nisīdite    candasuriyasmiṃ [^290-1] yaññatthāya    
pañcālarājadhītā pañjalikā     sabbaparisantaramanupariyāsi [^290-2] .

Yena saccena khaṇḍahālo      pāpakammaṃ karoti dummedho    
etena saccavajjena         samaṅginī sāmikena homi .

Ye idhatthi amanussā         yāni ca yakkhabhūtabhabyāni    
karontu me veyyāvaṭikaṃ      samaṅginī sāmikena homi .

Yā devatā idhāgatā        yāni ca sabbabhūtabhabyāni [^290-3]    
saraṇesiniṃ anāthaṃ tāyatha maṃ     yācāmi vo patimāhaṃ ajeyyaṃ .

[830] taṃ sutvā amanusso          ayokuṭaṃ paribbhamitvāna    
bhayamassa janayanto           rājānaṃ idamavoca .

Bujjhassu kho rājakali         mā tehaṃ matthakaṃ nitāḷesiṃ    
mā jeṭṭhaputtaṃ avadhi         adūsakaṃ sīhasaṅkāsaṃ .

Ko te diṭṭho rājakali       puttabhariyāyo haññamānāyo    
seṭṭhī ca gahapatayo          adūsakā saggakāmā hi .

[831] taṃ sutvā khaṇḍahālo         rājā ca abbhūtamidaṃ disvāna    
sabbesaṃ bandhanāni mocesuṃ     yathā taṃ anupaghātaṃ [^290-4] .

Sabbesu vippamuttesu         ye tattha samāgatā tadā āsuṃ    
sabbe ekekaleḍḍumadaṃsu      esa vadho khaṇḍahālassa .

#[^290-1] ma. Candasmiṃ .   [^290-2] ma. Sabbaparisāya samanapariyāyi .  [^290-3] ma. Yakkhabhūtabhabyāni .

<!-- Siam Rath Page 292 -->
<a id="page-292"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 292]*

Vādiṃsu nandippavesanagaraṃ [^292-1]    bandhanā mokkho aghositthāti .

Candakumārajātakaṃ sattamaṃ .

[^292-8] mahānāradakassapajātakaṃ    
[834] ahu rājā videhānaṃ         aṅgati nāma khattiyo    
pahutayoggo dhanimā          anantabalaporiso .

So ca paṇṇarase [^292-2] rattiṃ     purime yāme anāgate    
cātumāsā komudiyā         amacce sannipātayi .

Paṇḍite sutasampanne         mitapubbe vicakkhaṇe    
vijayañca sunāmañca           senāpatiṃ alātakaṃ .

Tamanupucchi vedeho          paccekaṃ brūtha saṃ ruciṃ    
cātumāsā komudajja         juṇhaṃ byapahataṃ tamaṃ    
kāyajja ratiyā rattiṃ         viharemu imaṃ utuṃ .

[835] tato senāpati rañño        alāto etadabravi    
haṭṭhaṃ yoggaṃ balaṃ sabbaṃ        senaṃ sannāhayāmase .

Niyyāma deva yuddhāya        anantabalaporisā    
ye te vasaṃ na āyanti       vasaṃ upanīyāmase [^292-3] .

Esā mayhaṃ sakā diṭṭhi       ajitaṃ ojiyāmase [^292-4]    
ramassu deva yuddhāya         etaṃ cittamataṃ mama    
#[^292-1] ma. Nandiṃ pavesi nagaraṃ .    [^292-2] ma. Pannarasiṃ .     [^292-3] ma. Upanayāmase .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 293 -->
<a id="page-293"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 293]*

[836] alātassa vaco sutvā        sunāmo etadabravi    
sabbe tuyhaṃ mahārāja        amittā vasamāgatā .

Nikkhittasatthā paccattā [^293-1]    nivātamanuvattare    
uttamo ussavo ajja        na yuddhaṃ mama ruccati .

Annapānañca khajjañca         khippaṃ abhiharantu te    
ramassu deva kāmehi         naccagītesu vādite [^293-2] .

[837] sunāmassa vaco sutvā        vijayo etadabravi    
sabbe kāmā mahārāja       niccaṃ tava upaṭṭhitā .

Na hete dullabhā deva       tava kāmehi modituṃ    
sadāpi kāmā labbhanti [^293-3]     netaṃ cittamataṃ mama .

Samaṇaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vāpi         upāsemu bahussutaṃ    
yo najja vinaye kaṅkhaṃ        atthadhammavidū ise .

[838] vijayassa vaco sutvā         rājā aṅgatimabravi    
yathā vijayo bhaṇati           mayhaṃ petaṃva ruccati .

Samaṇaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vāpi         upāsemu bahussutaṃ    
yo najja vinaye kaṅkhaṃ        atthadhammavidū ise .

Sabbeva santā karotha matiṃ     kaṃ upāsemu paṇḍitaṃ    
yo [^293-4] najja vinaye kaṅkhaṃ     atthadhammavidū ise .

[839] vedehassa vaco sutvā       alāto etadabravi  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 294 -->
<a id="page-294"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 294]*

Atthāyaṃ migadāyasmiṃ          acelo dhīrasammato .

Guṇo kassapagottāyaṃ         suto citrakathī gaṇī    
taṃ deva payirupāsemu         so no kaṅkhaṃ vinessati .

[840] alātassa vaco sutvā        rājā codesi sārathiṃ    
migadāyaṃ gamissāma           yuttaṃ yānaṃ idhānaya .

[841] tassa yānaṃ ayojesuṃ         dantaṃ rūpiyapakkharaṃ .

Sukkamaṭṭhaparivāraṃ            paṇḍaraṃ dosinā mukhaṃ .

Tatrāsuṃ kumudāyuttā         cattāro sindhavā hayā    
anilūpamasamuppātā           sudantā suvaṇṇamālino .

Setacchattaṃ setaratho         setassā setavījanī    
vedeho sahamaccehi         nīyaṃ candova sobhati .

Tamanuyāyiṃsu bahavo           indikhaggadharā [^294-1] balī    
assapiṭṭhigatā vīrā          narā naravarādhipaṃ .

So muhuttaṃva yāyitvā        yānā oruyha khattiyo    
vedeho sahamaccehi         patti guṇamupāgami .

Yepi tattha tadā āsuṃ        brāhmaṇibbhā samāgatā    
na te apanayi rājā         akataṃ bhūmimāgate .

[842] tato so mudukā bhisiyā       muducittakasanthate    
mudupaccatthate rājā         ekamantaṃ upāvisi .

Nisajja rājā sammodi        kathaṃ sārāṇiyaṃ tato  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 295 -->
<a id="page-295"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 295]*

Kacci yāpanīyaṃ bhante         vātānamaviyattatā .

Kacci akasirā vutti          labhasi piṇḍiyāpanaṃ [^295-1]    
appābādho [^295-2] casi kacci     cakkhu na parihāyati .

[843] taṃ guṇo paṭisammodi          vedehaṃ vinaye rataṃ    
yāpanīyaṃ mahārāja           sabbametaṃ tadūbhayaṃ .

Kacci tuyhaṃpi vedeha         paccantā na balīyare    
kacci arogaṃ yoggante       kacci vahati vāhanaṃ    
kacci te byādhiyo natthi      sarīrassupatāpiyā [^295-3] .

[844] paṭisammodito rājā         tato pucchi anantarā    
atthaṃ dhammañca ñāyañca        dhammakāmo rathesabho .

Kathaṃ dhammañcare macco        mātāpitūsu kassapa    
kathañcare ācariye          puttadāre kathañcare .

Kathañcareyya vuḍḍhesu         kathaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇe    
kathañca balakāyasmiṃ           kathaṃ janapade care .

Kathaṃ dhammañcaritvāna          pecca [^295-4] gacchanti suggatiṃ    
kathañceke adhammaṭṭhā        patanti nirayaṃ adho .

[845] vedehassa vaco sutvā       kassapo etadabravi    
suṇohi me mahārāja         saccaṃ avitathaṃ padaṃ .

Natthi dhammacaritassa           phalaṃ kalyāṇapāpakaṃ    
#[^295-1] ma. Piṇḍayāpanaṃ .     [^295-2] ma. Apābādho .    [^295-3] sī. Yu. Sarīrassupatāpikā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 296 -->
<a id="page-296"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 296]*

Natthi deva paraloko [^296-1]      ko tato hi idhāgato .

Natthi deva pitaro vā        kuto mātā kuto pitā    
natthi ācariyo nāma         adantaṃ ko damessati .

Samatulyāni bhūtāni           natthi jeṭṭhāpacāyikā    
natthi balaṃ viriyaṃ vā          kuto uṭṭhānaporiso [^296-2] .

Niyatāni hi bhūtāni           yathā goṭaviso tathā    
laddheyyaṃ labhate macco       tattha dānaphalaṃ kuto .

Natthi dānaphalaṃ deva          avaso devavīriyo    
bālehi dānaṃ paññattaṃ        paṇḍitehi paṭicchitaṃ    
avasā denti dhīrānaṃ         bālā paṇḍitamānino .

[846] sattime sassatā kāyā       acchejjā avikopino    
tejo paṭhavī āpo ca        vāyo sukhadukkhañcime .

Jīve ca sattime kāyā       yesaṃ chettā na vijjati    
natthi hantā va chettā vā    haññare vāpi kocinaṃ [^296-3] .

Antareyeva [^296-4] kāyānaṃ      satthāni vītivattare    
yo cāyaṃ [^296-5] siramādāya      paresaṃ nisitāsinā    
na so chindati te kāye      tattha pāpaphalaṃ kuto .๊    
cullāsītimahākappe          sabbe sujjhanti saṃsaraṃ    
anāgate tamhi kāle        saññatopi na sujjhati .

Caritvāpi bahuṃ bhadraṃ          neva sujjhanti nāgate    
#[^296-1] ma. Paro loko .    [^296-2] ma. -porisaṃ .      [^296-3] ma. Haññe yevāpi koci naṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 297 -->
<a id="page-297"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 297]*

Pāpañcepi bahuṃ katvā        taṃ khaṇaṃ nātivattare .

Anupubbena no suddhi         kappānaṃ cullāsītiyā    
niyatiṃ nātivattāma           velantamiva sāgaro .

[847] kassapassa vaco sutvā        alāto etadabravi    
yathā bhadanto bhaṇati          mayhametaṃva ruccati .

Ahaṃpi purimaṃ jātiṃ            sare saṃsaritattano    
piṅgalo nāmahaṃ āsiṃ         luddo goghātako pure .

Bārāṇasiyaṃ phītāyaṃ           bahuṃ pāpaṃ kataṃ mayā    
bahū mayā hatā pāṇā        mahiṃsā sūkarā ajā .

Tato cutāhaṃ idha jāto       iddhe senāpatikule    
natthi nūna phalaṃ pāpe [^297-1]      yohaṃ [^297-2] na nirayaṃ gato .

[848] athettha vījako nāma         dāso āsi paṭajjarī [^297-3] .

Uposathaṃ upavasanto          guṇasantikupāgami .

Kassapassa vaco sutvā        alātassa ca bhāsitaṃ    
passasanto muhuṃ uṇhaṃ         rudaṃ assūni vattayi .

[849] tamanupucchi vedeho          kimatthaṃ samma rodasi    
kinte sutaṃ vā diṭṭhaṃ vā      kiṃ maṃ vedesi vedanaṃ .

[850] vedehassa vaco sutvā       vījako etadabravi    
natthi me vedanā dukkhā      mahārāja suṇohi me .

Ahaṃpi purimaṃ jātiṃ            sarāmi sukhamattano  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 298 -->
<a id="page-298"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 298]*

Sāketāhaṃ pure āsiṃ         bhāvaseṭṭhī guṇe rato .

Sammato brāhmaṇibbhānaṃ       saṃvibhāgarato suci    
na cāpi pāpakaṃ kammaṃ         sarāmi katamattano .

Tato cutāhaṃ vedeha         idha jāto duritthiyā    
gabbhamhi kumbhadāsiyā         yato jāto suduggato .

Evampi duggato santo       samacariyamadhiṭṭhito    
upaḍḍhabhāgaṃ bhattassa          dadāmi yo me icchati .

Cātuddasiṃ pañcadasiṃ           sadā upavasāmahaṃ    
na cāpi pāṇe hiṃsāmi        theyyañcāpi vivajjayiṃ .

Sabbameva hi nūnetaṃ          suciṇṇaṃ bhavati nipphalaṃ    
niratthaṃ maññidaṃ sīlaṃ           alāto bhāsatī yathā .

Kalimeva nūna gaṇhāmi         asippo dhuttako yathā    
kaṭaṃ alāto gaṇhāti         kitavo sikkhito yathā .

Dvāraṃ nappaṭipassāmi         yena gacchāmi suggatiṃ    
tasmā rāja parodāmi        sutvā kassapabhāsitaṃ .

[851] vījakassa vaco sutvā         rājā aṅgatimabravi    
natthi dvāraṃ sugatiyā         niyataṃ [^298-1] kaṅkha vījaka .

Sukhaṃ vā yadi vā dukkhaṃ        niyataṃ [^298-2] kira labbhati    
saṃsārasuddhi sabbesaṃ          mā turito [^298-3] anāgate .

Ahampi pubbe kalyāṇo       brāhmaṇibbhesu pāvaṭo 4  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 299 -->
<a id="page-299"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 299]*

Vohāramanusāsanto          ratihīno tadantarā .

[852] punapi bhante dakkhemu         saṅgati ce bhavissati .

Idaṃ vatvāna vedeho        paccagā sannivesanaṃ .

[853] tato ratyā vivasane         upaṭṭhānampi aṅgati    
amacce sannipātetvā       idaṃ vacanamabravi .

Candake me vimānasmiṃ        sadā kāme vidhentu me    
mā mupagacchacha [^299-1] atthesu      guyhappakāsiyesu ca .

Vijayo ca sunāmo ca         senāpati alātako    
ete atthe nisīdantu        vohārakusalā tayo .

Idaṃ vatvāna vedeho        kāmeva bahu maññatha    
na cāpi brāhmaṇibbhesu       atthe kismiñci pāvaṭo [^299-2] .

[854] tato dvesattarattassa        vedehassatrajā piyā    
rājakaññā rucā [^299-3] nāma     dhātimātaramabravi .

Alaṅkarotha maṃ amma          sakhiyo ca alaṅkarontu me    
suve paṇṇaraso dibyo        gacchaṃ pitussa santike [^299-4] .

Tassā mālyaṃ abhihariṃsu        candanañca mahārahaṃ    
maṇisaṅkhamuttāratanaṃ           nānāratte ca ambare .

Tañca sovaṇṇamaye pīṭhe       nisinnaṃ bahukitthiyo    
parikīriya asobhiṃsu [^299-5]         rucaṃ ruciravaṇṇiniṃ .

[855] sā ca sakhīmajjhagatā          sabbābharaṇabhūsitā    
#[^299-1] ma. Mā upagaccha .    [^299-2] ma. Byāvaṭo .   [^299-3] sī. Yu. Rujā . Ito paraṃ īdisameva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 300 -->
<a id="page-300"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 300]*

Sateritā [^300-1] abbhamiva        candakaṃ pāvisī rucā .

Upasaṅkamitvā vedehaṃ        vanditvā vinaye rataṃ    
suvaṇṇakhacite [^300-2] pīṭhe        ekamantaṃ upāvisi .

[856] tañca disvāna vedeho       accharānaṃva saṅgamaṃ    
rucaṃ sakhimajjhagataṃ             idaṃ vacanamabravi .

Kacci ramasi pāsāde         anto pokkharaṇiṃ pati    
kacci bahuvidhaṃ khajjaṃ           sadā abhiharanti te .

Kacci bahuvidhaṃ mālyaṃ          ocinitvā kumāriyo    
gharake karotha paccekaṃ        khiḍḍā ratiratā ahu [^300-3] .

Kena vā vikalaṃ tuyhaṃ         kiṃ khippaṃ āharantu te    
mano karassu kuṭṭamukhī [^300-4]      api candasamaṃ [^300-5] hi te .

[857] vedehassa vaco sutvā       rucā pitaramabravi    
sabbametaṃ mahārāja          labbhatissarasantike .

Suve paṇṇaraso dibyo        sahassaṃ āharantu me    
yathā dinnañca dassāmi        dānaṃ sabbavaṇīsvahaṃ .

[858] rucāya vacanaṃ sutvā          rājā aṅgatimabravi    
bahuṃ vināsitaṃ vittaṃ           niratthaṃ aphalaṃ tayā .

Uposathe vasaṃ niccaṃ          annapānaṃ na bhuñjasi    
niyatetaṃ abhuttabbaṃ           natthi puññaṃ abhuñjato    
vijakopi hi sutvāna          tadā kassapabhāsitaṃ    
#[^300-1] ma. Sateratā .    [^300-2] sī. Yu. Suvaṇṇavikate .    [^300-3] ma. Muhuṃ .    [^300-4] ma. Kuḍḍamukhī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 301 -->
<a id="page-301"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 301]*

Passasanto muhuṃ uṇhaṃ         rudaṃ assūni vattayi .

Yāva ruce jīvamānā [^301-1]      mā bhattamapanāmayi    
natthi bhadde paro loko      kiṃ niratthaṃ vihaññasi .

[859] vedehassa vaco sutvā       rucā ruciravaṇṇinī    
jānaṃ pubbāparaṃ dhammaṃ         pitaraṃ etadabravi .

Sutameva pure āsi          paccakkhaṃ [^301-2] diṭṭhamidaṃ mayā    
bālūpasevī yo hoti         bālova samapajjatha .

Muḷho hi muḷhamāgamma        bhiyyo mohaṃ nigacchati    
paṭirūpaṃ alātena            vījakena ca muyhituṃ .

[860] tvañca devāsi sappañño      dhīro atthassa kovido    
kathaṃ bālehi sadiso          hīnadiṭṭhimupāgami .

Sace hi saṃsārapathena sujjhati    
niratthiyā pabbajjā guṇassa    
kīṭova aggiṃ jalitaṃ apāpataṃ    
āpajjati mohamuḷho [^301-3] naggabhāvaṃ .

Saṃsārasuddhīti pure niviṭṭhā    
kammaṃ vidūsenti bahū ajānaṃ    
pubbe kali duggahitova attho [^301-4]    
dummocayo balisā ambujova .

[861] upamante karissāmi          mahārāja tavatthiyā  
#[^301-1] sī. Yu. Jīvasino .   [^301-2] ma. Sakkhi .    [^301-3] sī. Yu. Momuho .   [^301-4] kalī duggahitā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 302 -->
<a id="page-302"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 302]*

upamāyapidhekacce           atthaṃ jānanti paṇḍitā .

Vāṇijānaṃ yathā nāvā        appamāṇabhārā garu    
atibhāraṃ samādāya           aṇṇave avasīdati .

Evameva naro pāpaṃ         thokaṃ thokampi ācinaṃ    
atibhāraṃ samādāya           niraye avasīdati .

Na tāva bhāro paripūro       alātassa mahīpati    
ācināti ca taṃ pāpaṃ         yena gacchati duggatiṃ .

Pubbevassa kataṃ puññaṃ         alātassa mahīpati    
tasseva deva nissando       yañceso labhate sukhaṃ .

Khīyatevassa taṃ puññaṃ          tathāhi aguṇe rato    
ujumaggaṃ avahāya            kummaggaṃ anudhāvati .

Tulā yathā paggahitā         ohite tulamaṇḍale    
unnameti tulāsīsaṃ           bhāre oropite sati .

Evameva naro puññaṃ         thokaṃ thokampi ācinaṃ    
saggātimāno dāsova        vījako sātave rato .

Yamajja vījako dāso         dukkhaṃ passati attani    
pubbevassa kataṃ pāpaṃ         tameso paṭisevati .

Khīyatevassa taṃ pāpaṃ          tathāhi vinaye rato    
kassapañca samāpajja          māhevuppathamāgamā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 303 -->
<a id="page-303"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 303]*

[862] yaṃ yaṃ hi rāja bhajati          santaṃ vā yadivā asaṃ    
sīlavantaṃ visīlaṃ vā           vasaṃ tasseva gacchati .

Yādisaṃ kurute mittaṃ          yādisañcūpasevati    
sopi tādisako hoti         sahavāso hi tādiso .

Sevamāno sevamānaṃ         saṃphuṭṭho saṃphusaṃ paraṃ    
saro duṭṭho kalāpaṃva         alittamupalimpati    
upalepabhayā dhīro           neva pāpasakhā siyā .

Pūtimacchaṃ kusaggena           yo naro upanayhati    
kusāpi pūtī vāyanti          evaṃ bālūpasevanā .

Tagarañca palāsena           yo naro upanayhati    
pattāpi surabhī vāyanti        evaṃ dhīrūpasevanā .

Tasmā pattapuṭasseva         ñatvā sampākamattano    
asante nūpaseveyya         sante seveyya paṇḍito    
asanto nirayaṃ nenti         santo pāpenti sugatiṃ .

[863] ahampi jātiyo satta         sare saṃsaritattano    
anāgatepi satteva          yā gamissaṃ ito cutā .

Yā me sā sattamī jāti      ahu pubbe janādhipa  
kammāraputto magadhesu        ahu rājagahe pure .Ā  
pāpaṃ sahāyamāgamma          bahu pāpaṃ kataṃ mayā  
paradārassa heṭhentā        carimhā amarā viya .H  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 304 -->
<a id="page-304"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 304]*

Taṃ kammaṃ nihitaṃ aṭṭhā         bhasmācchannova pāvako    
atha aññehi kammehi         ajāyiṃ vaṃsabhūmiyaṃ .

Kosambiyaṃ seṭṭhikule         iddhe phīte mahaddhane    
ekaputto mahārāja         niccaṃ sakkatapūjito .

Tattha mittaṃ asevissaṃ         sahāyaṃ sātave rataṃ    
paṇḍitaṃ sutasampannaṃ           so maṃ atthe nivesayi .

Cātuddasiṃ pañcadasiṃ           bahuṃ rattimupāvisiṃ    
taṃ kammaṃ nihitaṃ aṭṭhā         nidhīva udakantike .

Atha pāpānaṃ kammānaṃ         yametaṃ magadhe kataṃ    
phalaṃ pariyāgataṃ [^304-1] pacchā      bhutvā duṭṭhavisaṃ yathā .

Tato cutāhaṃ vedeha         roruve niraye ciraṃ    
sakammunā apacissaṃ           taṃ saraṃ na sukhaṃ labhe .

Bahuvassagaṇe tattha           khepayitvā bahuṃ dukkhaṃ    
bhinnāgate [^304-2] ahu rāja      chakalo [^304-3] uddhatapphalo .

[864] sātaputtā mayā vuḷhā       piṭṭhiyā ca rathena ca    
tassa kammassa nissando       paradāragamanassa me .

Tato cutāhaṃ vedeha         kapi āsiṃ brahāvane    
niluñcitaphaloyeva            yūthapena pagabbhinā    
tassa kammassa nissando       paradāragamanassa me .

Tato cutāhaṃ vedeha         dasannesu pasu ahuṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 305 -->
<a id="page-305"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 305]*
