Niluñcito javo bhadro        yoggaṃ vuḷhaṃ ciraṃ mayā    
tassa kammassa nissando       paradāragamanassa me .

Tato cutāhaṃ vedeha         vajjīsu kulamāgamā    
nevitthī na pumā āsiṃ        manussatte sudullabhe    
tassa kammassa nissando       paradāragamanassa me .

Tato cutāhaṃ vedeha         ajāyiṃ nandane vane    
bhavane tāvatiṃsāhaṃ           accharā kāmavaṇṇinī .

Vicitravatthābharaṇā           āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā    
kusalā naccagītassa           sakkassa paricārikā .

Tattha ṭhitāhaṃ vedeha         sarāmi jātiyo imā    
anāgatepi satteva          yā gamissaṃ ito cutā .

Pariyāgatantaṃ kusalaṃ           yaṃ me kosambiyaṃ kataṃ    
deve ceva manusse ca       sandhāvissaṃ ito cutā .

Satta jaccā [^305-1] mahārāja     niccaṃ sakkatapūjitā    
itthībhāvā [^305-2] na muccissaṃ     chaṭṭhāva gatiyo [^305-3] imā .

Sattamī ca gati deva          devaputto mahiddhiko    
pumā devo bhavissāmi        devakāyasmimuttamo .

Ajjāpi santānamayaṃ          mālaṃ ganthenti nandane    
devaputto javo nāma        yo me mālaṃ paṭicchati .

Muhutto viya so dibyo       idha vassāni soḷasa  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 306 -->
<a id="page-306"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 306]*

Rattindivo ca so dibyo      mānusiṃ saradosataṃ .

Iti kammāni anventi        asaṅkheyyāpi jātiyo    
kalyāṇaṃ yadi vā pāpaṃ        na hi kammaṃ vinassati [^306-1] .

[865] yo icche puriso hotuṃ       jātiṃ jātiṃ punappunaṃ    
paradāraṃ vivajjeyya          dhotapādova kaddamaṃ .

Yā icche puriso hotuṃ       jātiṃ jātiṃ punappunaṃ    
sāmikaṃ apacāyeyya          indaṃva paricārikā .

Yo icche dibbabhogañca       dibbamāyuṃ yasaṃ sukhaṃ    
pāpāni parivajjetvā        tividhaṃ dhammamācare .

Kāyena vācā manasā        appamatto vicakkhaṇo    
attano hoti atthāya        itthī vā yadivā pumā .

Yekecime manujā jīvaloke    
yasassino sabbasamantabhogā    
asaṃsayaṃ tehi pure suciṇṇaṃ    
kammassakāse puthu sabbasattā .

Iṅghānucintesi sayampi deva    
kutonidānā te imā janinda    
yā te imā accharasannikāsā    
alaṅkatā kāñcanajālachannā .

Iccevaṃ pitaraṃ kaññā         rucā tosesi aṅgatiṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 307 -->
<a id="page-307"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 307]*

Muḷhassa maggamācikkhi         dhammamakkhāsi subbatā .

[866] athāgamā brahmalokā        nārado mānusiṃ pathaṃ    
jambūdīpaṃ avekkhanto         addasa [^307-1] rājānamaṅgatiṃ .

Tato patiṭṭha pāsāde        vedehassa purakkhato [^307-2]    
tañca disvā anuppattaṃ        rucā isimavandatha .

[867] athāsanamhā oruyha         rājā byamhitamānaso [^307-3]    
nāradaṃ paripucchanto          idaṃ vacanamabravi .

Kuto nu āgacchasi devavaṇṇī    
obhāsayaṃ sabbadisā [^307-4] candimā va    
akkhāhi me pucchito nāmagottaṃh    
kathannu jānanti manussaloke .

[868] ahañhi devato idāni emi    
obhāsayaṃ sabbadisā [^307-4] candimā va    
akkhāmi te pucchito nāmagottaṃ    
jānanti maṃ nārado kassapo cāti .

[869] acchariyarūpaṃ [^307-5] tava yādisañca    
vehāyasaṃ gacchasi tiṭṭhasi ca    
pucchāmi taṃ nārada etamatthaṃ    
atha kena vaṇṇena tavāyamiddhi .

#[^307-1] ma. Addā .    [^307-2] ma. Puratthato .   [^307-3] ma. Byathitamānaso .  [^307-4] sī. Yu. Saṃvariṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 308 -->
<a id="page-308"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 308]*

[870] saccañca dhammo ca damo ca cāgo    
guṇā mamete pakatā purāṇā    
teheva dhammehi susevitehi    
manojavo yenakāmaṃ gatosmi .

[871] acchariyamācikkhasi [^308-1] puññasiddhiṃ    
sace hi [^308-2] evaṃ yathā tvaṃ vadesi    
pucchāmi taṃ nārada etamatthaṃ    
puṭṭho ca me sādhu viyākarohi .

[872] pucchassu maṃ rāja tavesa attho    
yaṃ saṃsayaṃ kurute bhūmipāla    
ahantaṃ nissaṃsayataṃ gamemi    
nayehi ñāyehi ca hetubhi ca .

[873] pucchāmi taṃ nārada etamatthaṃ    
puṭṭho ca me nārada mā musā bhaṇi    
atthi nu devā pitaro nu atthi    
loko paro atthi jano yamāha .

[874] attheva devā pitaro ca atthi    
loko paro atthi jano yamāha    
kāmesu giddhā ca narā pamuḷhā    
lokaṃ paraṃ na vidū mohayuttā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 309 -->
<a id="page-309"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 309]*

[875] atthīti ce nārada saddahāsi    
nivesanaṃ paraloke matānaṃ    
idheva me pañcasatāni dehi    
dassāmi te paraloke sahassaṃ .

[876] dajjemu kho pañcasatāni bhoto    
jaññāmu ce sīlavantaṃ vadaññaña    
luddantaṃ bhontaṃ niraye vasantaṃ    
ko codaye paraloke sahassaṃ .

Idheva yo hoti adhammasīlo [^309-1]    
pāpācāro alaso luddakammo    
na paṇḍitā tasmiṃ iṇaṃ dadanti    
na hi āgamo hoti tathāvidhamhā .

Dakkhañca posaṃ manujā viditvā    
uṭṭhānakaṃ sīlavantaṃ vadaññaña    
sayameva bhogehi nimantayanti    
kammaṃ karitvā punamāharesi .

[877] ito cuto [^309-2] dakkhasi tattha rāja    
kākolasaṅghehi vikassamānaṃ [^309-3]    
taṃ khajjamānaṃ niraye vasantaṃ    
kākehi gijjhehi ca soṇakehi [^309-4]    
#[^309-1] yu. Akammasīlo .   [^309-2] sī. Yu. Gato .   [^309-3] sī. Yu. Kākoḷasaṅghehipi kaḍḍhamānaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 310 -->
<a id="page-310"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 310]*

Sañchinnagattaṃ ruhiraṃ savantaṃ    
ko codaye paraloke sahassaṃ .

[878] andhatamaṃ tattha na candasuriyā    
nirayo sadā tumulo ghorarūpo    
sā neva ratti na divā paññāyati    
tathāvidhe ko vicare dhanatthiko .

[879] savalo ca sāmo ca duve suvāṇā    
pavaḍḍhakāyā balino mahantā    
khādanti dantehi ayomayehi    
ito panuṇṇaṃ paraloke patantaṃ [^310-1] .

Taṃ khajjamānaṃ niraye vasantaṃ    
luddehi vāḷehi aghammigehi    
sañchinnagattaṃ ruhiraṃ savantaṃ    
ko codaye paraloke sahassaṃ .

[880] usūhi sattīhi [2] sunissitāhi    
hananti vijjhanti ca paccāmittā    
kālūpakālā nirayamhi ghore    
pubbe naraṃ dukkaṭakammakāriṃ .

Taṃ haññamānaṃ niraye vajantaṃ  
kucchismiṃ passasmiṃ vipphālitūdaraṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 311 -->
<a id="page-311"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 311]*

Sañchinnagattaṃ ruhiraṃ savantaṃ    
ko codaye paraloke sahassaṃ .

[881] sattī usū tomarabhiṇḍivālā    
vividhāvudhaṃ vassati tattha devo    
patanti aṅgāramivaccimanto    
sīlāsanī vassati luddakamme .

Uṇho ca vāto nirayamhi dussaho    
na tamhi sukhaṃ labhati [^311-1] ittaraṃpi    
tantaṃ vidhāvantamalenamāturaṃ    
ko codaye paraloke sahassaṃ .

[882] sandhāvamānaṃpi [^311-2] rathesu yuttaṃ    
sañjotibhūtaṃ paṭhaviṃ kamantaṃ    
patodalaṭṭhīhi sucodayantaṃ [^311-3]    
ko codaye paraloke sahassaṃ .

[883] tamāruhantaṃ khurasañcitaṃ giriṃ    
vibhiṃsanaṃ pajjalitaṃ bhayānakaṃ    
sañchinnagattaṃ ruhiraṃ savantaṃ    
ko codaye paraloke sahassaṃ .

[884] tamāruhantaṃ pabbatasannikāsaṃ  
aṅgārarāsiṃ jalitaṃ bhayānakaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 312 -->
<a id="page-312"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 312]*

Sandaḍḍhagattaṃ [^312-1] kapaṇaṃ rudantaṃ    
ko codaye paraloke sahassaṃ .

[885] abbhakūṭasamā uccā          kaṇṭakāhi citā [^312-2] dumā    
ayomayehi tikkhehi          naralohitapāyibhi .

Tamāruhanti nāriyo          narā ca paradāragū    
coditā sattihatthehi         yamaniddesakāribhi .

Tamāruhantaṃ niraye           simbaliṃ ruhiramakkhitaṃ    
vidaddhakāyaṃ [^312-3] vitacaṃ         āturaṃ gāḷhavedanaṃ .

Passasantaṃ muhuṃ uṇhaṃ          pubbakammāparādhikaṃ    
dummagge vitacaṃ gattaṃ         ko taṃ yāceyya taṃ dhanaṃ .

[886] abbhakūṭasamā uccā          asipattacitā dumā    
ayomayehi tikkhehi          naralohitapāyibhi .

Tamāruhantaṃ asipattapādapaṃ    
asīhi tikkhehi ca chijjamānaṃ    
sañchinnagattaṃ ruhiraṃ savantaṃ    
ko codaye paraloke sahassaṃ .

[887] tato nikkhantamattantaṃ         asipattanirayā [^312-4] dumā [^312-5]    
sampatitaṃ vetaraṇiṃ            ko taṃ yāceyya taṃ dhanaṃ .

[888] kharā khārodakā tattā       duggā vetaraṇī nadī    
ayopokkharasañchannā         tikkhapattehi sandati .

#[^312-1] ma. Sudaḍḍhagattaṃ .  [^312-2] ma. Kaṇṭakanicitā .  [^312-3] ma. Vidaḍḍhakāyaṃ .  [^312-4] ma. Asipattācitā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 313 -->
<a id="page-313"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 313]*

Tattha sañchinnagattantaṃ         vuyhantaṃ ruhiramakkhitaṃ    
vetaraññe anālambe        ko taṃ yāceyya taṃ dhanaṃ .

[889] vedhāmi rukkho viya chijjamāno0    
disaṃ na jānāmi pamuḷhasañño    
bhayānutappāmi mahāva [^313-1] me bhayā    
sutvāna gāthā [^313-2] tava bhāsitā ise .

Āditte vārimajjhaṃ va        dīpaṃvogherivaṇṇave [^313-3]    
andhakāreva pajjoto        tvannosi saraṇaṃ ise .

Atthañca dhammañca anusāsa maṃ ise    
atītamaddhā aparādhitaṃ mayā    
ācikkha me nārada suddhimaggaṃ    
yathā ahaṃ no nirayaṃ pateyyaṃ .

[890] yathā ahu dhataraṭṭho          vessāmitto ca aṭṭhako    
yamadatti [^313-4] usinnaro(sivirājā 5) paricārikā [^313-6] samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ    
ete caññe ca rājāno     ye saggavisayaṃ [^313-7] gatā .

Adhammaṃ parivajjetvā         dhammañcara mahīpati    
annahatthā ca te byamhe     ghosayantu pure tava .

Ko chāto ko ca tasito      ko mālaṃ ko vilepanaṃ    
nānārattānaṃ vatthānaṃ        ko naggo paridahissati .

#[^313-1] ma. Mahā ca .    [^313-2] ma. Kathā .   [^313-3] ma. Dīpaṃvoghe mahaṇṇave .   [^313-4] sī. Yamataggi .

#ma. Yāmataggi .    [^313-5] ma. Usindaro cāpi sivī ca rājā .   [^313-6] ma. Paricārakā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 314 -->
<a id="page-314"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 314]*

Ko panthe chattamādeti [^314-1]    pādukā ca mudū subhā    
iti sāyañca pāto ca        ghosayantu pure tava .

Jiṇṇaṃ posaṃ gavassañca         māssu yuñja yathā pure    
parihārañca dajjāsi          adhikārakato balī .

[891] kāyo te rathasaññāto       manosārathiko lahu    
avihiṃsāsāritakkho           saṃvibhāgapaṭicchado .

Pādasaññamanemiyo           hatthasaññamapakkharo    
kucchisaññamanabbhanto          vācāsaññamakūjano .

Saccavākyasamattaṅgo         apesuññasusaññato    
girāsakhilanelaṅgo           mitabhāṇisilesito .

Saddhālobhasusaṅkhāro         nivātañjalikubbaro    
athaddhatānatīsāko           sīlasaṃvaranaddhano .

Akkodhanamanugghāṭī           dhammapaṇḍarachattako    
bāhusaccamupālambo [^314-2]       ṭhiticittamupādhiyo .

Kālaññutācittasāro         vesārajjatidaṇḍako    
nivātavuttiyottaṅgo [^314-3]      anatimānayugo lahu .

Alīnacittasanthāro           vuḍḍhisevirajohato [^314-4]    
sati patodo dhīrassa          dhiti yogo ca rasmiyo .

Mano dantaṃ pathaṃ neti         samadantehi vājibhi [^314-5]  
#[^314-1] ma. Chattamāneti .   [^314-2] ma. ...Mapālambo .   [^314-3] ma. ...Yottako .   [^314-4] vuḍḍhisevī  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 315 -->
<a id="page-315"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 315]*

icchā lobho ca kummaggo     ujumaggo ca saññamo .

Rūpe sadde rase gandhe      vāhanassa padhāvato    
paññā ākoṭanī rāja        tattha attāva sārathi .

Sace etena yānena        samacariyā daḷhā dhiti    
sabbakāmaduho rāja          na jātu nirayaṃ vajeti .

[892] alāto devadattosi         sunāmo āsi bhaddaji    
vijayo sārīputtosi          moggallānosi vījako .

Sunakkhatto licchaviputto       guṇo āsi acelako    
ānando sā rucā āsi      yā rājānaṃ pasādayi .

Uruvelakassapo rājā        pāpadiṭṭhi tadā ahu    
mahābrahmā bodhisatto       evaṃ dhāretha jātakanti .

Mahānāradakassapajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ .

[^315-9] vidhurajātakaṃ    
[893] paṇḍu kīsiyāsi dubbalā    
vaṇṇarūpaṃ na tavedisaṃ pure    
vimale akkhāhi pucchitā    
kīdisī tuyhaṃ sarīravedanā .

[894] dhammo manujesu mātīnaṃ  
dohaḷo nāma janinda vaccati  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 316 -->
<a id="page-316"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 316]*

Dhammāhataṃ nāgakuñjara    
vidhurassa hadayābhipatthaye .

[895] candaṃ kho tvaṃ vimale dohaḷāyasi    
suriyaṃ vā athavāpi mālutaṃ    
dullabhaṃ [^316-1] hi vidhurassa dassanaṃ [^316-2]    
ko vidhuraṃ idhamānayissati .

[896] kinnu tāta tuvaṃ pajjhāyasi    
padumaṃ hatthagataṃva te mukhaṃ    
kinnu dummanarūposi issara    
mā tvaṃ soci amittatāpana .

[897] mātā hi tava irandati [^316-3]    
vidhurassa hadayaṃ dhaniyyati    
dullabhaṃ hi vidhurassa dassanaṃ    
ko vidhuraṃ idhamānayissati .

[898] taṃ bhattāraṃ [^316-4] pariyesanaṃ cara    
yo vidhuraṃ idhamānayissati .

Pituno ca sā sutvāna vākyaṃ    
rattiṃ nikkhamma avassutī cari .

[899] ke gandhabbe ke rakkhase ca nāge    
ke kiṃpurise athavāpi mānuse    
#1-[^316-2] sī. Yu. Dullabhe ... Dassane .   [^316-3] ma. Irandhati . Ito paraṃ īdisameva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 317 -->
<a id="page-317"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 317]*

ke paṇḍite sabbakāmadade    
dīgharattaṃ bhattā me bhavissati .

[900] assāsa hessāmi te pati    
bhattā te hessāmi anindalocane    
paññāhi mama tathāvidhāhi    
assāsa hessasi bhariyā mama .

[901] avacāsi puṇṇakaṃ irandatī    
pubbapathānugatena cetasā    
ehi gacchāma pitu mamantike    
eseva te etamatthaṃ pavakkhati .

[902] alaṅkatā suvasanā           mālinī candanussadā    
yakkhaṃ hatthe gahetvāna       pitu santikamupāgami .

[903] nāgavara vaco suṇāhi me    
paṭirūpaṃ paṭipajja suṅkiyaṃ    
patthemi ahaṃ irandatiṃ    
tāya samaṅgiṃ karohi maṃ tuvaṃ .

Sataṃ hatthī sataṃ assā         sataṃ assatarī rathā    
sataṃ valabhiyo puṇṇā          nānāratnassa kevalā    
te nāga paṭipajjassu         dhītaraṃ dehirandatiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 318 -->
<a id="page-318"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 318]*

[904] yāva āmantaye ñātī        mitte ca suhadajjane    
anāmantakatakammaṃ            taṃ pacchā anutappati .

[905] tato so varuṇo nāgo       pavisitvā nivesanaṃ    
bhariyaṃ āmantayitvāna         idaṃ vacanamabravi .

Ayaṃ so puṇṇako yakkho       yācati maṃ irandatiṃ    
bahunā vittalābhena          tassa dema piyaṃ mama .

[906] na dhanena na vittena         labbhā amhaṃ irandatī    
sace ca kho hadayaṃ paṇḍitassa    
dhammena laddhā idha māhareyya    
etena vittena kumāri labbhā    
nāññaṃ dhanaṃ uttariṃ patthayāma .

[907] tato so varuṇo nāgo       nikkhamitvā nivesanā    
puṇṇakāmantayitvāna          idaṃ vacanamabravi .

Na dhanena na vittena         labbhā amhaṃ irandatī    
sace tuvaṃ dahayaṃ paṇḍitassa    
dhammena laddhā idhamāharesi    
etena vittena kumāri labbhā    
nāññaṃ dhanaṃ uttariṃ patthayāma .

[908] yaṃ paṇḍitotyeke vadanti loke  
tameva bāloti punāhu aññe  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 319 -->
<a id="page-319"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 319]*

Akkhāhi me vippavadanti ettha    
kaṃ paṇḍitaṃ nāga tuvaṃ vadesi .

[909] korabyarājassa dhanañjayassa [^319-1]    
yadi te suto vidhuro nāma kattā    
ānehi taṃ paṇḍitaṃ dhammaladdhā    
irandatī paṭṭhacarāva [^319-2] te hotu .

[910] idañca sutvā varuṇassa vākyaṃ    
uṭṭhāya yakkho paramappatīto    
tattheva santo purisaṃ asaṃsi    
ānehi ājaññamidheva yuttaṃ .

Jātarūpamayā kaṇṇā          kācamhicamayā khurā    
jambonadassa pākassa         suvaṇṇassa uracchado .

[911] devavāhavahaṃ yānaṃ           assamāruyha puṇṇako    
alaṅkato kappitakesamassu    
pakkāmi vehāyasamantalikkhe .

So puṇṇako kāmarāgena [^319-3] giddho    
irandatiṃ nāgakaññaṃ jigiṃsaṃ    
gantvāna taṃ bhūtapatiṃ yasassiṃ    
iccabravi vessavaṇaṃ kuveraṃ .

Bhogavatī nāma mandire  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 320 -->
<a id="page-320"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 320]*

Vāsā hiraññavatīti vuccati    
nagare nimmite kāñcanamaye    
maṇḍalassa uragassa niṭṭhitaṃ .

Aṭṭālakā oṭṭhagīviyo    
lohitaṅgassa masāragallinoba    
pāsādettha silāmayā[^320-5]    
sovaṇṇaratanena chāditā .

Ambā tilakā ca jambuyo    
sattapaṇṇā mucalindaketakā    
piyaṅgukā [^320-1] uddālakā sahā    
pāribhaddakā sinduvāritā [^320-2] .

Campeyyakā nāgamallikā    
bhaginīmālā athettha koliyā    
ete dumā pariṇāmitā    
sobhayanti uragassa mandire [^320-3] .

Khajjurettha silāmayā sovaṇṇadhuvapupphitā bahū    
yattha vasatopapātiko    
nāgarājā varuṇo mahiddhiko .

Tassa komārikā bhariyā  
vimalā kāñcanavelliviggahā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 321 -->
<a id="page-321"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 321]*

Kāḷā taruṇāva uggatā    
pucimandatthanī [^321-1] cārudassanā .

Lākhārasarattasucchavī    
kaṇikārāva nivātapupphitā    
tidivokkacarāva accharā    
vijjuvabbhaghanāva nissaṭā .

Sā dohaḷinī suvimhitā    
vidhurassa hadayaṃ dhaniyyati    
taṃ nesaṃ dadāmi issara    
tena te denti irandatiṃ mamaṃ .

[912] so puṇṇako bhūtapatiṃ yasassiṃ    
āmantiya vessavaṇaṃ kuveraṃ    
tattheva santo [^321-2] purisaṃ asaṃsi    
ānehi ājaññamidheva yuttaṃ .

Jātarūpamayā kaṇṇā          kācamhicamayā khurā    
jambonadassa pākassa         suvaṇṇassa uracchado .

Devavāhavahaṃ yānaṃ           assamāruyha puṇṇako    
alaṅkato kappitakesamassu    
pakkāmi vehāyasamantalikkhe .

[913] so agamā rājagahaṃ surammaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 322 -->
<a id="page-322"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 322]*

Aṅgassa rañño nagaraṃ durāsadaṃ [^322-1]    
bahūtabhakkhaṃ bahuannapānaṃ    
masakkasāraṃ viya vāsavassa .

Mayūrakoñcagaṇasaṅghuṭṭhaṃ    
dijābhighuṭṭhaṃ dijasaṅghasevitaṃ    
nānāsakuṇābhirudaṃ suvaṅgaṇaṃ [^322-2]    
pupphābhikiṇṇaṃ himavaṃva pabbataṃ .

So puṇṇako vepulamābhiruyha [^322-3]    
siluccayaṃ kiṃpurisānuciṇṇaṃ    
anvesamāno maṇiratanaṃ uḷāraṃ    
tamaddasa pabbatakūṭamajjhe .

[914] disvā maṇiṃ pabhassaraṃ jātivantaṃ [^322-4]    
dhanāharaṃ [^322-5] maṇiratanaṃ uḷāraṃ    
daddallamānaṃ yasasā yasassinaṃ    
obhāsatī vijjurivantalikkhe .

Tamaggahī veḷuriyaṃ mahagghaṃ    
manoharaṃ [^322-5] nāma mahānubhāvaṃ    
ājaññamāruyha anomavaṇṇo    
pakkāmi vehāyasamantalikkhe .

#[^322-1] sĪ.Ma. Durāyutaṃ .   [^322-2] sĪ.Yu. Subhaṅgaṇaṃ .   [^322-3] ma. -bhiruhi .   [^322-4] ma. Jātimantaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 323 -->
<a id="page-323"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 323]*

[915] so agamā [^323-1] nagaraṃ indapattaṃ    
oruyhupāgañchi sabhaṃ kurūnaṃ    
samāgate ekasataṃ samagge    
avhettha yakkho avikampamāno .

Konīdha raññaṃ varamābhijeti    
kamābhijeyyāma varaddhanena    
kamanuttaraṃ ratanavaraṃ jināma    
ko vāpi no jeti varaddhanena .

[916] kuhiṃ nu raṭṭhe tava jātibhūmi    
na korabyasseva vaco tavedaṃ    
abhītosi [^323-2] no vaṇṇanibhāya sabbe    
akkhāhi me nāmañca bandhave ca .

[917] kaccāyano māṇavakosmi rāja    
anūnanāmo iti mavhayanti    
aṅgesu me ñātayo bandhavā ca    
akkhena devasmi idhānupatto .

[918] kiṃ māṇavassa ratanāni atthi    
ye taṃ jinanto hare akkhadhutto    
bahūni rañño ratanāni atthi    
te tvaṃ daliddo kathamavhayesi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 324 -->
<a id="page-324"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 324]*

[919] dhanāharo [^324-1] nāma maṇi mamāyaṃ    
dhanāharaṃ [^324-2] maṇiratanaṃ uḷāraṃ    
imañca ājaññaṃ amittatāpanaṃ    
etaṃ me jinitvā hare akkhadhutto .

[920] eko maṇi māṇava kiṃ karissati    
ājāniyeko pana kiṃ karissati    
bahūni rañño ratanāni atthi    
ājāniyā vātajavā anappakā .

Dohaḷakaṇḍaṃ nāma .

[921] idañca me maṇiratanaṃ          passa tvaṃ dvipaduttama    
itthīnaṃ viggahā cettha        purisānañca viggahā .

Migānaṃ viggahā cettha        sakuṇānañca viggahā    
nāgarāje supaṇṇe ca [^324-3]     maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

[922] hatthānīkaṃ rathānīkaṃ           asse pattī ca vammine [^324-4]    
caturaṅginimaṃ senaṃ            maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Hatthārohe anīkaṭṭhe        rathike pattikārake    
balaggāni viyūhāni [^324-5]        maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

[923] puraṃ aṭṭālasampannaṃ [^324-6]       bahupākāratoraṇaṃ    
siṅghāṭake subhūmiyo          maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

#[^324-1] ma. Manoharo .   [^324-2] ma. Manoharaṃ .   [^324-3] ma. Nāgarājā supaṇṇā ca .  [^324-4] sĪ.Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 325 -->
<a id="page-325"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 325]*

Esikā parikhāyo ca         palīghaṃ aggaḷāni ca    
aṭṭālake ca dvāre ca      maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

[924] passa toraṇamaggesu          nānādijagaṇā bahū    
haṃsā koñcā mayūrā ca       cakkavākā ca kukkuhā .

Kuṇālakā bahū citrā         sikhaṇḍī jīvajīvakā    
nānādijagaṇākiṇṇaṃ           maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

[925] passa nagaraṃ supākāraṃ         abbhūtaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ    
samussitaddhajaṃ rammaṃ           suvaṇṇavālukasanthataṃ .

Passettha paṇṇasālāyo       vibhattā bhāgaso mitā    
nivesane nivese ca         sandhibyūhe pathatthiyo [^325-1] .

[926] pānāgāre ca soṇḍe ca     suddā [^325-2] odaniyā gharā    
vesī ca gaṇikāyo ca         maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Mālākāre ca rajake        gandhike atha dussike    
suvaṇṇakāre maṇikāre        maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Āḷārike ca sūde ca        naṭanaṭṭakagāyine [^325-3]    
pāṇissare kumbhathūnike        maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

[927] passa bherīmudiṅgā ca         saṅkhā paṇavadendimā    
sabbañca tālāvacaraṃ          maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Sammatālañca vīṇañca          naccagītaṃ suvāditaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 326 -->
<a id="page-326"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 326]*

Turiyatālitasaṅghuṭṭhaṃ           maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Laṅghikā muṭṭhikā cettha       māyākārā ca sobhiyā    
vetālike ca jalle ca       maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

[928] samajjā cettha vattanti       ākiṇṇā naranāribhi    
mañcātimañce bhūmiyo         maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Passa malle samajjasmiṃ        pothente [^326-1] diguṇaṃ bhujaṃ    
nihate nihatamāne ca         maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

[929] passa pabbatapādesu          nānāmigagaṇā bahū    
sīhā byagghā varāhā ca      acchakokataracchayo .

Palasatā gavajā ca           mahiṃsā rohitā rurū    
eṇeyyā sarabhā ceva       gaṇino nikkasūkarā .

Kadalimigā bahū citrā         viḷārā sasakaṇṇakā    
nānāmigagaṇākiṇṇaṃ           maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

[930] najjāyo supatitthāyo        sovaṇṇavālukasanthatā    
acchā savanti ambūni         macchagumbanivesitā .

Kumbhilā makarā cettha        sasasumārā ca kacchapā    
pāṭhīnā bahusā [^326-2] macchā     balajā muñjarohitā .

Nānādijagaṇākiṇṇā          nānādumagaṇāyutā    
veḷuriyaphalakarodāyo         maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 327 -->
<a id="page-327"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 327]*

[931] passettha pokkharaṇiyo        suvibhattā catuddisā    
nānādijagaṇākiṇṇā          puthulomacchasevitā [^327-1]    
samantodakasampannaṃ           mahiṃ sāgarakuṇḍalaṃ    
upetaṃ vanarājībhi            maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

[932] purato videhe passa         goyāniye ca pacchato    
kuruyo jambūdīpañca           maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Passa candañca suriyañca        obhāsente catuddisā    
sineruṃ anupariyāyante        maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Sineruṃ himavantañca           sāgarañca mahiddhiyaṃ [^327-2]    
cattāro ca mahārāje       maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Ārāme vanagumbe ca        pāṭiye ca siluccaye    
ramme kiṃpurisākiṇṇe         maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Pārusakaṃ cittalataṃ            missakaṃ nandanaṃ vanaṃ    
vejayantañca pāsādaṃ         maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Sudhammaṃ tāvatiṃsañca           pārichattañca pupphitaṃ    
erāvaṇaṃ nāgarājaṃ          maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Passettha devakaññāyo       nabhā vijjurivuggatā    
nandane vicarantiyo          maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Passettha devakaññāyo       devaputtapalobhinī  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 328 -->
<a id="page-328"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 328]*

Devaputte ramamāne [^328-1]      maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

[933] parosahassaṃ pāsāde         veḷuriyaphalakasanthate    
pajjalantena [^328-2] vaṇṇena      maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Tāvatiṃse ca yāme ca        tusite cāpi nimmite    
paranimmitavasavattino [^328-3]       maṇimhi passa nimmitaṃ .

Passettha pokkharaṇiyo        vippasannodakā sucī    
maṇḍālakehi sañchannā        padumuppalakehi ca .

[934] dasettha rājiyo setā       dassanīyā [^328-4] manoramā    
cha piṅgalā paṇṇarasā         haliddā ca catuddasa .

Vīsati tattha sovaṇṇā         vīsati rajatāmayā    
indagopakavaṇṇābhā          tāva dissanti tiṃsati .

Dasettha kāḷiyo cha ca [^328-5]     mañjeṭṭhā pañcavīsati    
missā bandhukapupphehi         nīluppalavicittakā .

Evaṃ sabbaṅgasampannaṃ         accimantaṃ pabhassaraṃ    
odhisuṅkaṃ mahārāja          passa tvaṃ dvipaduttama .

Maṇikaṇḍaṃ nāma .

[935] upāgataṃ rāja upehi lakkhaṃ    
netādisaṃ maṇiratanaṃ tavatthi    
dhammena jiyyāma [^328-6] asāhasena    
jito ca no khippamavākarohi .

#[^328-1] sī. Yu. Caramāne .   [^328-2] ma. Pajjalante ca .    [^328-3] sī. Yu. Paranimmitābhiratino .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 329 -->
<a id="page-329"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 329]*

[936] pañcāla paccuggata sūrasena    
macchā ca maddā saha kekakebhi    
passantu no te asathena yuddhaṃ    
na no sabhāyaṃ na karonti kiñci .

[937] te pāvisuṃ akkhamadena mattā    
rājā kurūnaṃ puṇṇako cāpi yakkho    
rājā kaliṃ viccinamaggahesi    
kaṭamaggahi puṇṇako nāma yakkho    
te tattha jūte ubhaye [^329-1] samāgate    
raññaṃ sakāse sakhinañca majjhe    
ajesi yakkho naravīraseṭṭhaṃ    
tatthappanādo tumulo babhūva .

[938] jayo mahārāja parājayo ca    
āyūhataṃ aññatarassa hoti    
janinda jinnosi [^329-2] varandhanena    
jito ca me khippamavākarohi .

[939] hatthī gavassā maṇikuṇḍalā ca  
yañcāpi mayhaṃ ratanaṃ paṭhabyā  
gaṇhāhi kaccāna varaṃ dhanānaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 330 -->
<a id="page-330"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 330]*

Ādāya yenicchasi tena gaccha .

[940] hatthī gavassā maṇikuṇḍalā ca    
yañcāpi tuyhaṃ ratanaṃ paṭhabyā    
tesaṃ varo vidhuro nāma kattā    
so me jito taṃ me avākarohi .

[941] attā ca me so saraṇaṃ gatī ca    
dīpo ca leṇo ca parāyano ca    
asantuleyyo mama so dhanena    
pāṇena me sadiso esa kattā .

[942] ciraṃ vivādo mama tuyhamassa [^330-1]    
kāmañca pucchāma tameva gantvā    
eseva no vivaratu etamatthaṃ    
yaṃ vakkhati hotu tathā [^330-2] ubhinnaṃ .

[943] addhā hi saccaṃ bhaṇasi         na ca māṇava sāhasaṃ    
tameva gantvā pucchāma       tena tussāmubho janā .

[944] saccaṃ nu devā vidahū kurūnaṃ    
dhamme ṭhitaṃ vidhurannāmamaccaṃ    
dāsosi rañño uda vāsi ñāti    
vidhuroti saṅkhyā [^330-3] katamosi loke .

[945] āmāyadāsāpi bhavanti heke  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 331 -->
<a id="page-331"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 331]*

Dhanena kītāpi bhavanti dāsā    
sayaṃpi dāsā upayanti heke [^331-1]    
bhayā panuṇṇāpi [^331-2] bhavanti dāsā    
ete narānaṃ caturova dāsā .

Addhā hi yonito ahampi jāto    
bhavo ca rañño abhavo ca rañño    
dāsohaṃ devassa paraṃpi gantvā    
dhammena maṃ māṇava tuyha dajjā .

[946] ayaṃpi [^331-3] dutiyo vijayo mamajja    
puṭṭho hi kattā vivarittha [^331-4] pañhaṃ    
adhammarūpo vata rājaseṭṭho    
subhāsitaṃ nānujānāsi mayhaṃ .

[947] evañce no so vivarittha [^331-5] pañhaṃ    
dāsohamasmi na ca khosmi ñāti    
gaṇhāhi kaccāna varaṃ dhanānaṃ    
ādāya yenicchasi tena gaccha .

Akkhakaṇḍaṃ nāma .

[948] vidhura vasamānassa            gahaṭṭhassa sakaṃ gharaṃ    
khemā vutti kathaṃ assa        kathannu assa saṅgaho .

Abyāpajjhaṃ kathaṃ assa         saccavādī ca māṇavo  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 332 -->
<a id="page-332"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 332]*

Asmā lokā paraṃ lokaṃ       kathaṃ pecca na socati .

[949] taṃ tattha gatimā dhitimā        matimā atthadassimā้    
saṅkhātā sabbadhammānaṃ        vidhuro etadabravi .

Na sādhāraṇadārassa          na bhuñje sādhumekako [^332-1]    
na seve lokāyatikaṃ         netaṃ paññāya vaḍḍhanaṃ .

Sīlavā vattasampanno         appamatto vicakkhaṇo    
nivātavutti atthaddho         surato sakhilo mudu .

Saṅgahetā ca mittānaṃ        saṃvibhāgī vidhānavā    
tappeyya annapānena        sadā samaṇabrāhmaṇe .

Dhammakāmo sutādhāro        bhaveyya paripucchako    
sakkaccaṃ payirupāseyya        sīlavante bahussute .

Gharamāvasamānassa            gahaṭṭhassa sakaṃ gharaṃ    
khemā vutti siyā evaṃ       evaṃ nu assa saṅgaho .

Abyāpajjho siyā evaṃ       saccavādī ca māṇavo    
asmā lokā paraṃ lokaṃ       evaṃ pecca na socati .

Gharāvāsapañhā nāma .

[950] ehi dāni gamissāma          dinno no issarena me    
mamevatthaṃ paṭipajja            esa dhammo sanantano .

[951] jānāmi māṇava tayāhamasmi  
dinnohamasmi tava issarena  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 333 -->
<a id="page-333"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 333]*

Tīhañca taṃ vāsayemu agāre    
yenaddhunā anusāsemu putte .

[952] tamme tathā hotu vasemu tīhaṃ    
kurutaṃ bhavaṃ ajja gharesu kiccaṃ    
anusāsataṃ puttadāre bhavajja    
yathā tayī pecca [^333-1] sukhī bhaveyya .

[953] sādhūti vatvāna pahūtakāmo    
pakkāmi yakkho vidhurena saddhiṃ    
taṃ kuñjarājaññahayānuciṇṇaṃ    
pāvekkhi antepuramariyaseṭṭho .

[954] koñcaṃ mayūrañca piyañca ketaṃ    
upāgami tattha surammarūpaṃ    
pahūtabhakkhaṃ bahuannapānaṃ    
masakkasāraṃ viya vāsavassa .

[955] tattha naccanti gāyanti        avhayanti varāvaraṃ    
accharā viya devesu         nāriyo samalaṅkatā .

Samaṅgīkatvā pamadāhi yakkhaṃ    
annena pānena ca dhammapālo    
attatthamevānuvicintayanto [^333-2]    
pāvekkhi bhariyāya tadā sakāse .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 334 -->
<a id="page-334"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 334]*

Taṃ candanagandharasānulittaṃ    
suvaṇṇajambonadanikkhasādisaṃ    
bhariyaṃ avaca ehi suṇohi bhoti    
puttāni āmantaya tambanette .

Sutvāna vākyaṃ patino anojā [^334-1]    
suṇisaṃ avaca tambanakhiṃ sunettaṃ    
āmantaya cammadharāni [^334-2] cete    
puttāni indīvarapupphasāme .

[956] te āgate muddhani dhammapālo    
cumbitvā putte avikampamāno    
āmantayitvāna avoca vākyaṃ    
dinnohaṃ raññā idha māṇavassa .

Tassajjahaṃ attasukhī vidheyyo    
ādāya yenicchati tena gacchati    
ahañca vo anusāsituṃ āgatosmi    
kathaṃ ahaṃ aparittāya gacche .

Sace vo rājā kururaṭṭhavāsī [^334-3]    
janasandho puccheyya pahūtakāmo    
kimābhijānātha pure purāṇaṃ    
kiṃ vo pitā anusāse puratthā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 335 -->
<a id="page-335"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 335]*

Samānāsanā [^335-1] hotha mayā ca [^335-2] sabbe    
ko nīdha rañño abbhatiko manusso    
tamañjaliṃ kariya vadetha evaṃ    
mā heva deva na hi esa dhammo    
viyaggharājassa nihīnajacco    
samānāsano [^335-3] deva kathaṃ bhaveyya .

Lakkhakaṇḍaṃ [^335-4]    
[957] so ca putte amacce ca      ñātayo suhadajjane    
alīnamanasaṅkappo            vidhuro etadabravi .

Ethayyā [^335-5] rājavasatiṃ       nisīditvā suṇātha me    
yathā rājakulaṃ patto         yasaṃ poso nigacchati .

[958] na hi rājakulaṃ patto         aññāto labhate yasaṃ    
nātisūro nātidummedho [^335-6]    nappamatto kudācanaṃ .

Yadāssa sīlaṃ paññañca         soceyyañcādhigacchati    
atha vissāsate tamhi         guyhañcassa na rakkhati .

[959] tulā yathā paggahitā         samadaṇḍā sudhāritā    
ajjhiṭṭho na vikampeyya       sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Tulā yathā paggahitā         samadaṇḍā sudhāritā    
sabbāni abhisambhonto        sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

#[^335-1] ma. Samāsanā .   [^335-2] ma. Va .     [^335-3] ma. Samāsano .    [^335-4] sī. Pekkhaṇakaṇḍaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 336 -->
<a id="page-336"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 336]*

[960] divā vā yadi vā rattiṃ       rājakiccesu paṇḍito    
ajjhiṭṭho na vikampeyya       sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Divā vā yadi vā rattiṃ       rājakiccesu paṇḍito    
sabbāni abhisambhonto        sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Yo cassa sukato maggo       rañño supaṭiyādito    
na tena vutto gaccheyya      sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

[961] na rañño samakaṃ bhuñje        kāmabhoge kudācanaṃ    
sabbattha pacchato gacche       sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Na rañño sadisaṃ vatthaṃ         na mālaṃ na vilepanaṃ    
ākappaṃ sarakuttiṃ vā         na rañño sadisamācare    
aññaṃ kareyya ākappaṃ        sarājavasatiṃ vase .[^336-9]    
[962] kīḷe rājā amaccehi        bhariyāhi parivārito    
nāmacco rājabhariyāsu        bhāvaṃ kubbetha paṇḍito .

Anuddhato acapalo           nipako saṃvutindriyo    
manopaṇidhisampanno           sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

[963] nāssa bhariyāhi kīḷeyya       na manteyya rahogato    
nāssa kosā dhanaṃ gaṇhe      sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Na niddaṃ bahu maññeyya        na madāya suraṃ pive    
nāssa dāye mige haññe     sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Nāssa pīṭhaṃ na pallaṅkaṃ        na kocchaṃ na nāvaṃ rathaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 337 -->
<a id="page-337"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 337]*

Sammatomhīti ārūyhe        sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Nātidūre bhaje [^337-1] rañño     nāccāsanne vicakkhaṇo    
samekkhañcassa [^337-2] tiṭṭheyya    sandissanto sabhattuno .

Na me [^337-3] rājā sakhā hoti   na rājā hoti methuno    
khippaṃ kujjhanti rājāno       sukenakkhīva ghaṭṭitaṃ .

Na pūjito maññamāno         medhāvī paṇḍito naro    
pharusaṃ paṭimanteyya           rājānaṃ parisaṃ gataṃ .

[964] laddhadvāro labhe dvāraṃ [^337-4]   neva rājūsu vissase    
aggiva saṃyato tiṭṭhe [^337-5]      sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Puttaṃ vā bhātaraṃ saṃ vā       sampaggaṇhāti khattiyo    
gāmehi nigamehi vā         raṭṭhehi janapadehi vā    
tuṇhībhūto upekkheyya        na bhaṇe chekapāpakaṃ .

[965] hatthārohā anīkaṭṭhā        rathikā pattikārakā [^337-6]    
tesaṃ kammāpadānena [^337-7]      rājā vaḍḍheti vettanaṃ    
na tesaṃ antarā gacche       sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Cāpova oname [^337-8] dhīro     vaṃso vāpi pakampaye    
paṭilomaṃ na vatteyya         sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Cāpovūnūdaro assa          macchovassa ajivhatā [^337-9]    
appāsī nipako sūro         sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

#[^337-1] sī. Yu. Bhave .    [^337-2] ma. Sammukhañcassa .    [^337-3] ma. Ve .   [^337-4] yu. Laddhavāro labhe  
#vāraṃ .    [^337-5] sī. Yu. Aggīva yato tiṭṭheyya .   [^337-6] ma. Hatkārohe anīkaṭṭhe rathike  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 338 -->
<a id="page-338"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 338]*

[966] na bāḷhaṃ itthiṃ gaccheyya      samphassaṃ tejasaṅkhyaṃ    
kāsaṃ sāsaṃ daraṃ bālyaṃ [^338-1]     khīṇamedho nigacchati .

Nātivelaṃ pabhāseyya         na tuṇhī sabbadā siyā    
avikiṇṇaṃ mitaṃ vācaṃ           patte kāle udīriye .

Akkodhano asaṅghaṭṭo        sacco saṇho apesuṇo    
samphaṃ giraṃ na bhāseyya        sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

[967] mātāpetibharo [^338-2] assa      kule jeṭṭhāpacāyiko    
saṇho sakhilasambhāso [^338-3]      sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Vinīto sippavā danto        katatto niyato mudu    
appamatto suci dakkho        sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Nivātavutti vuḍḍhesu          sappatisso sagāravo    
surato sukhasaṃvāso           sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Ārakā parivajjeyya         sañhituṃ [^338-4] pahitaṃ janaṃ    
bhattāraññevudikkheyya        na ca aññassa rājino .

[968] samaṇe brāhmaṇe cāpi       sīlavante bahussute    
sakkaccaṃ payirupāseyya        sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Samaṇe brāhmaṇe cāpi       sīlavante bahussute    
sakkaccaṃ anuvāseyya         sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Samaṇe brāhmaṇe cāpi       sīlavante bahussute  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 339 -->
<a id="page-339"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 339]*

Tappeyya annapānena        sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Samaṇe brāhmaṇe cāpi       sīlavante bahussute    
āsajja paññe sevetha       ākaṅkhaṃ vuḍḍhimattano .

[969] dinnapubbaṃ na hāpeyya        dānaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇe    
na ca kiñci nivāreyya        dānakāle vanibbake .

Paññavā buddhisampanno        vidhānavidhikovido    
kālaññū samayaññū ca          sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Uṭṭhātā kammadheyyesu       appamatto vicakkhaṇo    
susaṃvihitakammanto            sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

[970] khalaṃ sālaṃ pasuṃ khettaṃ         gantvā cassa abhikkhaṇaṃ    
mitaṃ dhaññaṃ nidhāpeyya         mitaṃ ca [^339-1] pācaye ghare .

Puttaṃ vā bhātaraṃ saṃ vā       sīlesu asamāhitaṃ    
anaṅgāva [^339-2] hi te bālā    yathā petā tatheva te .

Coḷañca nesaṃ piṇḍañca        āsīnānaṃ padāpaye    
dāse kammakare pose [^339-3]    sīlesu susamāhite    
dakkhe uṭṭhānasampanne       ādhipaccasmi ṭhāpaye .

[971] sīlavā ca alobho [^339-4] ca      anuvatto [^339-5] ca rājino    
āvī raho hito tassa        sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

Chandaññū rājino cassa        cittaṭṭho cassa [^339-6] rājino    
asaṅkusakavuttissa            sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

#[^339-1] ma. Mitaṃ va .   [^339-2] ma. Anaṅgavā .    [^339-3] ma. Pesse .   [^339-4] ma. Alolo ca .  [^339-5] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 340 -->
<a id="page-340"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 340]*

Acchādane ca nahāpane [^340-1]    dhove pāde adhosiraṃ    
āhatopi na kuppeyya        sa rājavasatiṃ vase .

[972] kumbhiñhi pañjaliṃ kayirā [^340-2]     cātaṃ [^340-3] cāpi padakkhiṇaṃ    
kimeva sabbakāmānaṃ          dātāraṃ dhīramuttamaṃ .

Yo deti sayanaṃ vatthaṃ         yānaṃ āvasathaṃ gharaṃ    
pajjunnoriva bhūtānaṃ [^340-4]       bhogehi abhivassati .

Eseyyā rājavasatī         vattamāno yathā naro    
ārādhayati rājānaṃ          pūjaṃ labhati bhattusu .

Rājavasatī nāma .

[5]    
[973] evaṃ samanusāsitvā          ñātisaṅghaṃ vicakkhaṇo    
parikiṇṇo suhajjehi [^340-6]       rājānamupasaṅkami .

Vanditvā sirasā pāde       katvā ca naṃ padakkhiṇaṃ    
vidhuro avaca rājānaṃ         paggahetvāna añjaliṃ .

Ayaṃ maṃ māṇavo neti         kattukāmo yathāmatiṃ    
ñātīnatthaṃ pavakkhāmi          taṃ suṇohi arindama .

Putte ca me udikkhesi       yañcamaññaṃ ghare dhanaṃ    
yathā pacchā [^340-7] na hāyetha    ñātisaṅgho mayī gate    
yatheva khalati bhūmyaṃ           bhūmyāyeva patiṭṭhati    
evetaṃ khalitaṃ mayhaṃ          etaṃ passāmi accayaṃ .

#[^340-1] ma. Ucchādaye ca nhāpaye .   [^340-2] ma. Kumbhampañjaliṃ kariyā .   [^340-3] sī. Yu. Vāyasaṃ vā .

#cātoti ekā sakuṇajāti .   [^340-4] ma. Bhūtāni .   [^340-5] ma. Antarapeyyāla .  [^340-6] ma. Suhadehi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 341 -->
<a id="page-341"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 341]*

[974] sakkā na gantuṃ iti mayhaṃ hoti    
chetvā [^341-1] vadhitvā idha kātiyānaṃ    
idheva hohi iti mayhaṃ ruccati    
mā tvaṃ agā uttamabhūripañña .

[975] mā hevadhammesu [^341-2] manaṃ paṇīdahi    
atthe ca dhamme ca yutto bhavassu    
dhiratthu kammaṃ akusalaṃ anariyaṃ    
yaṃ katvā pacchā nirayaṃ vajeyya .

Nevesa dhammo na puneti kiccaṃ    
ayiro hi dāsassa janinda issaro    
ghātetu jhāpetu athopi hantuṃ    
na ca mayhaṃ kodhatthi vajāmi cāhaṃ .

[976] jeṭṭhaputtaṃ upaguyha          vineyya hadaye daraṃ    
assupuṇṇehi nettehi        pāvisi so mahāgharaṃ .

[977] sālāva sampamadditā [^341-3]      mālutena pamadditā    
senti puttā ca dārā ca     vidhurassa nivesane .

Itthīsahassaṃ bhariyānaṃ          dāsīsattasatāni ca    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       vidhurassa nivesane .

Orodhā ca kumārā ca       vesiyānā ca brāhmaṇā    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       vidhurassa nivesane .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 342 -->
<a id="page-342"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 342]*

Hatthārohā anīkaṭṭhā        rathikā pattikārakā    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       vidhurassa nivesane .

Samāgatā jānapadā          negamā ca samāgatā    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       vidhurassa nivesane .

Itthīsahassaṃ bhariyānaṃ          dāsīsattasatāni ca    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       kasmā no vijahissasi .

Orodhā ca kumārā ca       vesiyānā ca brāhmaṇā [^342-1]    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       kasmā no vijahissasi .

Hatthārohā anīkaṭṭhā        rathikā pattikārakā [^342-2]    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       kasmā no vijahissasi .

Samāgatā jānapadā          negamā ca samāgatā [^342-3]    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       kasmā no vijahissasi .

[978] katvā gharesu kiccāni        anusāsitvā sakaṃ janaṃ    
mittāmacce ca bhacce [^342-4] ca   puttadāre ca bandhave .

Kammantaṃ saṃvidhetvāna         ācikkhitvā ghare dhanaṃ    
nidhiñca iṇadānañca           puṇṇakaṃ etadabravi .

Avasī tuvaṃ mayha tīhaṃ agāre    
katāni kiccāni gharesu mayhaṃ    
anusāsitā puttadārā mayā ca    
karomi kiccāni [^342-5] yathāmatinte .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 343 -->
<a id="page-343"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 343]*

[979] sace hi katte anusāsitā te    
puttā ca dārā anujīvino ca    
handehidāni taramānarūpo    
dīgho hi addhāpi ayaṃ puratthā .

Asambhītova [^343-1] gaṇhāhi       ājānīyassa [^343-2] vāladhiṃ    
idaṃ pacchimakaṃ tuyhaṃ           jīvalokassa dassanaṃ .

[980] sohaṃ kissānubhāyissaṃ [^343-3]      yassa me natthi dukkaṭaṃ    
kāyena vācā manasā        yena gaccheyya duggatiṃ .

[981] so assarājā vidhuraṃ vahanto    
pakkāmi vehāyasamantalikkhe    
sākhāsu selesu asajjamāno    
kāḷāgiriṃ khippamupāgamāsi .

[982] itthīsahassaṃ bhariyānaṃ          dāsīsattasatāni ca    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ    
yakkho brāhmaṇavaṇṇena       vidhuraṃ ādāya gacchati .

Orodhā ca kumārā ca       vesiyānā ca brāhmaṇā    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ    
yakkho brāhmaṇavaṇṇena       vidhuraṃ ādāya gacchati .

Hatthārohā anīkaṭṭhā        rathikā pattikārakā  
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 344 -->
<a id="page-344"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 344]*

Yakkho brāhmaṇavaṇṇena       vidhuraṃ ādāya gacchati .

Samāgatā jānapadā          negamā ca samāgatā    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ    
yakkho brāhmaṇavaṇṇena       vidhuraṃ ādāya gacchati .

Itthīsahassaṃ bhariyānaṃ          dāsīsattasatāni ca    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       paṇḍito so kuhiṃ gato .

Orodhā ca kumārā ca       vesiyānā ca brāhmaṇā    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       paṇḍito so kuhiṃ gato .

Hatthārohā anīkaṭṭhā        rathikā pattikārakā    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       paṇḍito so kuhiṃ gato .

Samāgatā jānapadā          negamā ca samāgatā    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       paṇḍito so kuhiṃ gato .

[983] sace so sattarattena        paṇḍito nāgamissati    
sabbe aggiṃ pavissāma [^344-1]     natthattho jīvitena no .

[984] paṇḍito ca viyatto ca        vibhāvī ca vicakkhaṇo    
khippaṃ mocesi attānaṃ        mā bhāyitthāgamissati [^344-2] .

Antarapeyyālaṃ nāma .

[985] so tattha gantvāna vicintayanto  
uccāvacā cetanakā bhavanti  
nayimassa jīvena mamatthi kiñci  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 345 -->
<a id="page-345"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 345]*

Hantvānimaṃ hadayaṃ ānayissaṃ [^345-1] .

[986] so tattha gantvā pabbatamantarasmiṃ [^345-2]    
anto pavisitvāna paduṭṭhacitto    
asaṃvutasmiṃ [^345-3] jagatippadese    
adhosiraṃ dhārayi kātiyāno .

[987] so lambamāno narake papāte    
mahabbhaye lomahaṃse vidugge    
asantasanto kurūnaṃ kattuseṭṭho    
iccabravi puṇṇakaṃ nāma yakkhaṃ .

Ariyāvakāsosi anarīyarūpo    
asaññato saññatasannikāso    
accāhitaṃ kammaṃ karosi luddaṃ [^345-4]    
bhāve ca te kusalaṃ natthi kiñci .

Yaṃ maṃ papātasmiṃ papātumicchasi    
ko nu tavattho maraṇena mayhaṃ    
amānusasseva tavajja vaṇṇo    
ācikkha me tvaṃ katamāsi devatā .

[988] yadi te suto puṇṇako nāma yakkho    
rañño kuverassa hi so sajibbo [^345-5]    
#[^345-1] sī. Yu. Ādiyissaṃ .    [^345-2] ma. Pabbatantarasmiṃ .    [^345-3] asamphutasmintipi pāṭho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 346 -->
<a id="page-346"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 346]*

Bhūmindharo varuṇo nāma nāgo    
brahā sucī vaṇṇabalūpapanno .

Tassānujaṃ dhītaraṃ kāmayāmi    
irandatī [^346-1] nāma sā nāgakaññā    
tassā sumajjhāya piyāya hetu    
patārayiṃ tuyhaṃ vadhāya dhīra .

[989] māheva te [^346-2] yakkha ahosi moho    
naṭṭhā bahū duggahitena lokā [^346-3]    
kinte sumajjhāya piyāya kiccaṃ    
maraṇena me iṅgha suṇomi [^346-4] sabbaṃ .

[990] mahānubhāvassa mahoragassa    
dhītukāmo ñātibhatohamasmi [^346-5]    
taṃ yācamānaṃ sasuro avoca    
yathāmamaññiṃsu sukāmanītaṃ .

Dajjemu kho te sutanana sunettaṃ    
suvimhitaṃ [^346-6] candanalittagattaṃ    
sace tuvaṃ dahayaṃ paṇḍitassa    
dhammena laddhā idhamāharesi .

Etena vittena kumāri labbhā    
nāññaṃ dhanaṃ uttari patthayāma    
#[^346-1] ma. Irandhatī .    [^346-2] ma. Tvaṃ .   [^346-3] ma. Loke .   [^346-4] sī. Yu. Suṇoma .  [^346-5] yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 347 -->
<a id="page-347"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 347]*

Evaṃ na muḷhosmi suṇohi katte .

Na cāpi me duggahitatthi kiñci    
hadayena te dhammaladdhena nāgā    
irandatiṃ [^347-1] nāgakaññaṃ dadanti .

Tasmā ahaṃ tuyhaṃ vadhāya yutto    
evaṃ mamattho maraṇena tuyhaṃ    
idheva taṃ narake pātayitvā    
hantvāna taṃ hadayaṃ ānayissaṃ .

[991] khippaṃ mamaṃ uddhara kātiyāna    
hadayena me yadi te atthi kiccaṃ    
yekeci me sādhu narassa dhammā    
sabbeva te pātukaromi ajja .

[992] so puṇṇako kurūnaṃ kattuseṭṭhaṃ    
nagamuddhani khippaṃ patiṭṭhapetvā    
assatthamāsīnaṃ samekkhiyāna    
paripucchi kattāramanomapaññaṃ .

Samuddhato mesi tuvaṃ papātā    
hadayena te ajja mamatthi kiccaṃ    
yekeci te sādhu narassa dhammā    
sabbeva me pātukarohi ajja .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 348 -->
<a id="page-348"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 348]*

[993] samuddhato tyasmi ahaṃ papātā    
hadayena me yadi te atthi kiccaṃ    
yekeci me sādhu narassa dhammā    
sabbeva te pātukaromi ajja .

[994] yātānuyāyī ca bhavāhi māṇava    
allañca pāṇiṃ parivajjayassu    
mā cassu mittesu kadāci dubbhī    
mā ca vasaṃ asatīnaṃ nigacche .

[995] kathaṃ nu yātaṃ anuyāyī hoti    
allañca pāṇiṃ dahate kathaṃ so    
asatī ca kā ko pana mittadubbho    
akkhāhi me pucchito etamatthaṃ .

[996] assatthataṃ [^348-1] nopica diṭṭhapubbaṃ    
yo āsanenāpi nimantayeyya    
tasseva atthaṃ puriso kareyya    
yātānuyāyīti tamāhu paṇḍitā .

Yassekarattimpi ghare vaseyya  
yatthannapānaṃ puriso labhetha [^348-2]  
na tassa pāpaṃ manasāpi cintaye  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 349 -->
<a id="page-349"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 349]*

Allañca [^349-1] pāṇiṃ dahate mittadubbho .

Yassa rukkhassa chāyāya        nisīdeyya sayeyya vā    
na tassa sākhaṃ bhañjeyya       mittadubbho hi pāpako .

Puṇṇampi cemaṃ paṭhaviṃ dhanena    
dajjitthiyā puriso sammatāya    
laddhā khaṇaṃ atimaññeyya tampi่    
tāsaṃ vasaṃ asatīnaṃ na gacche .

Evaṃ kho yātaṃ anuyāyī hoti    
allañca pāṇiṃ dahate punevaṃ    
asatī ca sā so pana mittadubbho    
so dhammiko hohi jahassu adhammaṃ .

Sādhunaradhammakaṇḍaṃ nāma .

[997] avasiṃ ahaṃ tuyhaṃ tīhaṃ agāre    
annena pānena upaṭṭhitosmi    
mitto mamāsi visajjāmahaṃ taṃ    
kāmaṃ gharaṃ uttamapañña gaccha .

Api hāyatu nāgakulassa [^349-3] attho    
alampi me nāgakaññāya hetu [^349-4]    
so tvaṃ sakeneva subhāsitena    
muttosi me ajja vadhāya pañña .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 350 -->
<a id="page-350"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 350]*

[998] handa tuvaṃ yakkha mamampi nehi    
sassuraṃ nu te [^350-1] atthaṃ mayī carassu    
ahampi nāgādhipatīvimānaṃ [^350-2]    
dakkhemi [^350-3] nāgassa adiṭṭhapubbaṃ .

[999] yaṃ ve narassa ahitāya assa    
na taṃ pañño arahati dassanāya    
atha kena vaṇṇena amittagāmaṃ    
tuvamicchasi uttamapañña gantuṃ .

[1000] addhā pajānāmi ahampi etaṃ    
na taṃ pañño arahati dassanāya    
pāpañca me natthi kataṃ kuhiñci    
tasmā na saṅke maraṇāgamāya .

[1001] handa ca ṭhānaṃ atulānubhāvaṃ    
mayā saha dakkhasi ehi katte    
yathacchati [^350-4] naccagītehi nāgo    
rājā yathā vessavaṇo niḷiññaṃ [^350-5] .

Taṃ nāgakaññā caritaṃ gaṇena    
nikīḷitaṃ niccamaho ca rattiṃ    
pahutamālyaṃ bahupupphachannaṃ    
#[^350-1] sasurantiketipi pāṭho .   Ma. Sasuraṃ te .   [^350-2] ma. Mayaṃ ca nāgādhipatiṃ vimānaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 351 -->
<a id="page-351"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 351]*

Obhāsati vijjurivantalikkhe .[^351-4]    
annena pānena upetarūpaṃ    
naccehi gītehi ca vāditehi    
paripūraṃ kaññāhi alaṅkatāhi    
upasobhati vatthapilandhanena .

[1002] so puṇṇako kurūnaṃ kattuseṭṭhaṃ    
nisīdayi pacchato āsanasmiṃ    
ādāya kattāramanomapaññaṃ    
upānayi bhavanaṃ nāgarañño .

Patvāna ṭhānaṃ atulānubhāvaṃ    
aṭṭhāsi kattā pacchato puṇṇakassa    
sāmaggipekkhī pana [^351-1] nāgarājā    
pubbeva jāmātaramajjhabhāsatha .

[1003] yaṃ nu tvaṃ agamā manussalokaṃ    
anvesamāno hadayaṃ paṇḍitassa    
kacci samiddhena idhānupatto    
ādāya kattāramanomapaññaṃ .

[1004] ayaṃ hi so āgato yaṃ tvamicchasi  
dhammena laddho mama dhammapālo  
taṃ passatha sammukhā bhāsamānaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 352 -->
<a id="page-352"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 352]*

Sukho have [^352-1] sappurisehi saṅgamo .

Kālāgirikaṇḍaṃ nāma .

[1005] adiṭṭhapubbaṃ disvāna         macco maccubhayaṭṭito [^352-2]    
byamhito nābhivādesi       na idaṃ paññavatāmiva .

[1006] na camhi byamhito nāga      na ca maccubhayaṭṭito [^352-3]    
na vajjho abhivādeyya       vajjhaṃ vā nābhivādaye .

Kathaṃ nu [^352-4] abhivādeyya      abhivādāpayetha ve    
yaṃ naro hantumiccheyya       taṃ kammaṃ nupapajjati .

[1007] evametaṃ yathā brūsi        saccaṃ bhāsasi paṇḍita    
na vajjho abhivādeyya       vajjhaṃ vā nābhivādaye    
kathaṃ nu [^352-5] abhivādeyya      abhivādāpayetha ve    
yaṃ naro hantumiccheyya       taṃ kammaṃ nupapajjati .

[1008] asassataṃ sassataṃ nu tavayidaṃ vimānaṃ [^352-6]    
iddhī jutī balaviriyūpapatti    
pucchāmi taṃ nāgarājetamatthaṃ    
kathaṃ nu te laddhamidaṃ vimānaṃ .

Adhiccaladdhaṃ pariṇāmajante    
sayaṃ kataṃ udāhu devehi dinnaṃ    
akkhāhi me nāgarājetamatthaṃ    
yatheva te laddhamidaṃ vimānaṃ .

#[^352-1] yu. Bhave .  2-[^352-3] sī. Yu. Bhayaddito . Ito paraṃ īdisameva .  4-[^352-5] ma. No .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 353 -->
<a id="page-353"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 353]*

[1009] nādhiccaladdhaṃ na pariṇāmajamme    
na sayaṃ kataṃ nāpi devehi dinnaṃ    
sakehi kammehi apāpakehi    
puññehi me laddhamidaṃ vimānaṃ .

[1010] kinte vataṃ kimpana brahmacariyaṃ    
kissa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipāko    
iddhī jutī balaviriyūpapatti    
idañca te nāga mahāvimānaṃ .

[1011] ahañca bhariyā ca manussaloke    
saddhā ubho dānapatī ahumhā    
opānabhūtaṃ me gharaṃ tadāsi    
santappitā samaṇabrāhmaṇā ca .

Mālañca gandhañca vilepanañca    
padīpiyaṃ seyyamupassayañca    
acchādanaṃ sāyanamannapānaṃ    
sakkacca dānāni adamha tattha .

Taṃ me vataṃ tampana brahmacariyaṃ    
tassa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipāko    
iddhī jutī balaviriyūpapatti    
idañca me dhīra mahāvimānaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 354 -->
<a id="page-354"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 354]*

[1012] evañca te laddhamidaṃ vimānaṃ    
jānāsi puññāna phalūpapattiṃ    
tasmā hi dhammañcara appamatto    
yathā vimānaṃ puna māvasesi .

[1013] nayīdha santi samaṇabrāhmaṇā ca    
yesannapānāni dademu katte    
akkhāhi me pucchito etamatthaṃ    
yathā vimānaṃ puna māvasema .

[1014] bhogī hi te santi idhūpapannā    
puttā ca dārā anujīvino ca    
telu tuvaṃ vacasā kammunā ca    
asampaduṭṭho ca bhavāhi niccaṃ .

Evaṃ tuvaṃ nāga asampadosaṃ    
anupālaya vacasā kammunā ca    
ṭhatvā idha yāvatāyukaṃ vimāne    
uddhaṃ ito gacchasi devalokaṃ .

[1015] addhā hi so socati rājaseṭṭho    
tayā vinā yassa tuvaṃ sajibbo    
dukkhūpanītopi tayā samecca    
vindeyya poso sukhamāturopi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 355 -->
<a id="page-355"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 355]*

[1016] addhā sataṃ bhāsasi nāga dhammaṃ    
anuttaraṃ atthapadaṃ suciṇṇaṃ    
etādisiyāsu hi āpadāsu    
paññāyate mādisānaṃ viseso .

[1017] akkhāhi no tāyaṃ mudhā nu laddho    
akkhehi no tāyaṃ ajesi jūte    
dhammena laddho itimāyamāha    
kathaṃ [2] tuvaṃ hatthamimassamāgato .

[1018] yo missaro tattha ahosi rājā    
tamāyamakkhehi ajesi jūte    
so maṃ jito rājā imassadāsi    
dhammena laddhosmi asāhasena .

[1019] mahorago attamano udaggo    
sutvāna dhīrassa subhāsitāni    
hatthe gahetvāna anomapaññaṃ    
pāvekkhi bhariyāya tadā sakāse .

Yena tvaṃ vimale paṇḍu        yena bhattaṃ na ruccati    
na ca me tādiso vaṇṇo      ayameso tamonudo .

Yassa te hadayenattho        āgatoyaṃ pabhaṅkaro    
tassa vākyaṃ nisāmehi        dullabhaṃ dassanaṃ puna .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 356 -->
<a id="page-356"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 356]*

[1020] disvāna taṃ vimalā bhūripaññaṃ    
dasaṅguliṃ añjaliṃ paggahetvā    
haṭṭhena bhāvena patītarūpā    
iccabravi kurūnaṃ kattuseṭṭhaṃ .

Adiṭṭhapubbaṃ disvāna          macco maccubhayaṭṭito    
byamhito nābhivādesi        na idaṃ paññavatāmiva .

[1021] na camhi byamhito nāgi       na ca maccubhayaṭṭito    
na vajjho abhivādeyya        vajjhaṃ vā nābhivādaye .

Kathaṃ nu [^356-1] abhivādeyya       abhivādāpayetha ve    
yaṃ naro hantumiccheyya        taṃ kammaṃ nupapajjati .

[1022] evametaṃ yathā brūsi         saccaṃ bhāsasi paṇḍita    
na vajjho abhivādeyya        vajjhaṃ vā nābhivādaye .

Kathaṃ nu [^356-2] abhivādeyya       abhivādāpayetha ve    
yaṃ naro hantumiccheyya        taṃ kammaṃ nupapajjati .

[1023] asassataṃ sassataṃ nu tavayidaṃ vimānaṃ    
iddhī jutī balaviriyūpapatti    
pucchāmi taṃ nāgakaññetamatthaṃ    
kathaṃ nu te laddhamidaṃ vimānaṃ .

Adhiccaladdhaṃ pariṇāmajante  
sayaṃ kataṃ udāhu devehi dinnaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 357 -->
<a id="page-357"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 357]*

akkhāhi me nāgakaññetamatthaṃ    
yatheva te laddhamidaṃ vimānaṃ .

[1024] nādhiccaladdhaṃ na pariṇāmajamme    
na sayaṃ kataṃ nāpi devehi dinnaṃ    
sakehi kammehi apāpakehi    
puññehi me laddhamidaṃ vimānaṃ .

[1025] kinte vataṃ kimpana brahmacariyaṃ    
kissa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipāko    
iddhī jutī balaviriyūpapatti    
idañca te nāgi mahāvimānaṃ .

[1026] ahañca kho sāmiko cāpi mayhaṃ    
saddhā ubho dānapatī ahumhā    
opānabhūtaṃ me gharaṃ tadāsi    
santappitā samaṇabrāhmaṇā ca .

Mālañca gandhañca vilepanañca    
padīpiyaṃ seyyamupassayañca    
acchādanaṃ sāyanamannapānaṃ    
sakkacca dānāni adamha tattha .

Taṃ me vataṃ tampana brahmacariyaṃ  
tassa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipāko  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 358 -->
<a id="page-358"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 358]*

Iddhī jutī balaviriyūpapatti    
idañca me dhīra mahāvimānaṃ .

[1027] evañca te laddhamidaṃ vimānaṃ    
jānāsi puññāna phalūpapattiṃ    
tasmāhi dhammañcara appamattā    
yathā vimānaṃ puna māvasesi .

[1028] nayīdha santi samaṇabrāhmaṇā ca    
yesannapānāni dademu katte    
akkhāhi me pucchito etamatthaṃ    
yathā vimānaṃ puna māvasema .

[1029] bhogī hi te santi idhūpapannā    
puttā ca sāmī [^358-1] anujīvino ca    
tesu tuvaṃ vacasā kammunā ca    
asampaduṭṭhā ca bhavāhi niccaṃ .

Evaṃ tuvaṃ nāgi asampadosaṃ    
anupālaya vacasā kammunā ca    
ṭhatvā idha yāvatāyukaṃ vimāne    
uddhaṃ ito gacchasi devalokaṃ .

[1030] addhā hi so socati rājaseṭṭho  
tayā vinā yassa tuvaṃ sajibbo  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 359 -->
<a id="page-359"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 359]*

Dukkhūpanītopi tayā samecca    
vindeyya poso sukhamāturopi .

[1031] addhā sataṃ bhāsasi nāgi dhammaṃ    
anuttaraṃ atthapadaṃ suciṇṇaṃ    
etādisiyāsu hi āpadāsu    
paññāyate mādisānaṃ viseso .

[1032] akkhāhi no tāyaṃ mudhā nu laddho    
akkhehi no tāyaṃ ajesi jūte    
dhammena laddho itimāyamāha [^359-1]    
kathaṃ [2] tuvaṃ hatthamimassamāgato .

[1033] yo missaro tattha ahosi rājā    
tamāyamakkhehi ajesi jūte    
so maṃ jito rājā imassadāsi    
dhammena laddhosmi asāhasena .

[1034] yatheva varuṇo nāgo       pañhaṃ pucchittha paṇḍitaṃ    
tatheva nāgakaññāpi        pañhaṃ pucchittha paṇḍitaṃ .

Yatheva varuṇaṃ nāgaṃ         dhīro tosesi pucchito    
tatheva nāgakaññampi        dhīro tosesi pucchito .

Ubhopi te attamane viditvā  
mahoragaṃ nāgakaññañca dhīro  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 360 -->
<a id="page-360"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 360]*

Acchambhī abbhīto alomahaṭṭho    
iccabravi varuṇaṃ nāgarājaṃ .

Mā rodhayi [^360-1] nāga āyāhamasmi    
yena tava attho idaṃ sarīraṃ    
hadayena maṃsena karohi kiccaṃ    
sayaṃ karissāmi yathāmatinte .

[1035] paññā have hadayaṃ paṇḍitānaṃ    
te tyamha paññāya mayaṃ sutuṭṭhā    
anūnanāmo labhatajja dāraṃ    
ajjeva taṃ kuruyo pāpayātu .

[1036] sa puṇṇako attamano udaggo    
irandatiṃ [^360-2] nāgakaññaṃ labhitvā    
haṭṭhena bhāvena patītarūpo    
iccabravi kurūnaṃ kattuseṭṭhaṃ .

Bhariyāya maṃ tvaṃ akarī samaṅgiṃ    
ahañca te vidhura karomi kiccaṃ    
idañca te maṇiratanaṃ dadāmi    
ajjeva taṃ kuruyo pāpayāmi .

[1037] ajeyyamesā tava hotu mittī 3  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 361 -->
<a id="page-361"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 361]*

Bhariyāya kaccāna piyāya saddhiṃ    
ānandacitto [^361-1] sumano patīto    
datvā maṇiṃ mañca nayindapattaṃ .

[1038] sa puṇṇako kurūnaṃ kattuseṭṭhaṃ    
nisīdayi purato āsanasmiṃ    
ājaññamāruyha anomavaṇṇaṃ    
pakkāmi vehāyasamantalikkhe [^361-2] .

Mano manussassa yathāpi gacche    
tatopissa khippataraṃ ahosi    
sa puṇṇako kurūnaṃ kattuseṭṭhaṃ    
upānayi nagaraṃ indapattaṃ .

[1039] etindapattaṃ nagaraṃ padissatiza    
rammāni ca ambavanāni bhāgaso    
ahañca bhariyāya samaṅgibhūto    
tuvañca pattosi sakaṃ niketaṃ .

[1040] sa puṇṇako kurūnaṃ kattuseṭṭhaṃ  
oropayitvā dhammasabhāya majjhe  
ājaññamāruyha anomavaṇṇo  
#[^361-1] ma. Anandi vitto .   [^361-2] idaṃ pādadvayaṃ sī. Ma. Potthakesu na dissati . Ādāya  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 362 -->
<a id="page-362"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 362]*

Pakkāmi vehāyasamantalikkhe .

Taṃ disvā rājā paramappatīto    
uṭṭhāya bāhāhi palissajitvā    
avikampayaṃ dhammasabhāya majjhe    
nisīdayi pamukhaṃ āsanasmiṃ .

[1041] tvaṃ no vinetāsi rathaṃva naṭṭhaṃ [^362-1]    
nandanti taṃ kuruyo dassanena    
akkhāhi me pucchito etamatthaṃ    
kathaṃ pamokkho ahu māṇavassa .

[1042] yaṃ māṇavotyābhivadi janinda    
na so manusso naravīraseṭṭha    
yadi te suto puṇṇako nāma yakkho    
rañño kuverassa hi so sajibbo .

Bhūmindharo varuṇo nāma nāgo    
brahā sucī vaṇṇabalūpapanno    
tassānujaṃ dhītaraṃ kāmayāno    
irandatī nāma sā nāgakaññā .

Tassā sumajjhāya piyāya hetu  
patārayittha maraṇāya mayhaṃ  
so ceva bhariyāya samaṅgibhūto  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 363 -->
<a id="page-363"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 363]*

Ahañcānuññāto maṇi ca laddho .

[1043] rukkho hi mayhaṃ gharadvāre jāto [^363-1]    
paññākkhandho sīlamayassa sākhā    
atthe ca dhamme ca ṭhito nipāko    
gavapphalo hatthigavassachanno .

Naccagītaturiyābhinādite    
ucchijja senaṃ [^363-2] puriso ahosi    
so no ayaṃ āgato sanniketaṃ    
rukkhassimassāpacitiṃ karotha .

Yekeci vittā mama paccayena    
sabbeva te pātukarontu ajja    
tibbāni katvāna upāyanāni    
rukkhassimassāpacitiṃ karotha .

Yekeci bandhā [^363-3] mama atthi raṭṭhe    
sabbeva te bandhanā mocayantu    
yathevayaṃ bandhanasmā pamutto    
evamete muñcare bandhanasmā .

Unnaṅgalā māsamimaṃ karontu  
maṃsodanaṃ brāhmaṇā bhakkhayantu  
amajjapā majjarahā pivantu  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 364 -->
<a id="page-364"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 364]*

puṇṇāhi thālāhi palissutāhi .

Mahāpathaṃ nicca samavhayantu    
tibbañca rakkhaṃ vidahantu raṭṭhe    
yathāññamaññaṃ na viheṭhayeyyuṃ    
rukkhassimassāpacitiṃ karotha .Ba    
[1044] orodhā ca kumārā ca       vesiyānā ca brāhmaṇā    
bahuṃ annañca pānañca         paṇḍitassābhihārayuṃ .

Hatthārohā anīkaṭṭhā        rathikā pattikārakā    
bahuṃ annañca pānañca         paṇḍitassābhihārayuṃ .

Samāgatā jānapadā          negamā ca samāgatā    
bahuṃ annañca pānañca         paṇḍitassābhihārayuṃ .

Bahujjano pasannosi          disvā paṇḍitamāgate    
paṇḍitamhi anuppatte         celukkhepo pavattathāti .

Vidhurajātakaṃ navamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 365 -->
<a id="page-365"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 365]*

10 vessantarajātakaṃ    
[1045] phussatī [^365-1] varavaṇṇābhe       varassu dasadhā vare    
paṭhabyā cārupubbaṅgī         yaṃ tuyhaṃ manaso piyaṃ .

[1046] devarāja namo tyatthu        kiṃ pāpaṃ pakataṃ mayā    
rammā cāvesi maṃ ṭhānā      vātova dharaṇīruhaṃ .

[1047] na ceva te kataṃ pāpaṃ        na ca me tvamasi appiyā    
puññañca te parikkhīṇaṃ         yena tevaṃ vadāmihaṃ [^365-2] .

Santike maraṇaṃ tuyhaṃ          vinābhāvo bhavissati    
paṭiggaṇhāhi me ete       vare dasa pavecchato .

[1048] varañce me ado sakka       sabbabhūtānamissara    
sivirājassa bhaddante         tattha assaṃ nivesane .

Nīlanettā nīlabhamū           nīlakkhīva [^365-3] yathā migī    
phussatī nāma nāmena         tattha assaṃ [^365-4] purinduṃ .

Puttaṃ labhetha varadaṃ           yācayogaṃ amacchariṃ    
pūjitaṃ paṭirājūbhi             kittimantaṃ yasassinaṃ .

Gabbhaṃ me dhārayantiyā        majjhimaṅgaṃ anuṇṇataṃ    
kucchi anuṇṇato assa         cāpaṃva likhitaṃ samaṃ .

Thanā me nappavatteyyuṃ       palitā nassantu [^365-5] vāsava    
kāye rajo na limpetha       vajjhañcāpi pamocaye .

Mayūrakoñcābhirude           nārīvaragaṇāyute    
#[^365-1] sī. Yu. Phsati . Ito paraṃ īdisameva .   [^365-2] ma. Vadāmahaṃ .    [^365-3] ma. Nilakkhī ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 366 -->
<a id="page-366"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 366]*

Khujjacelāvakākiṇṇe [^366-1]      sūdamāgadhavaṇṇite    
citraggaḷerughusite           surāmaṃsappabodhane    
sivirājassa bhaddante         tatthassaṃ mahesī piyā .

[1049] ye te dasa varā dinnā      mayā sabbaṅgasobhane    
sivirājassa vijite           sabbe te lacchasī vare .

[1050] idaṃ vatvāna maghavā          devarājā sujampati    
phussatiyā varaṃ datvā         anumodittha vāsavo .

Dasavaragāthā nāma .

[1051] tato [^366-2] cutā sā phussatī     khattiye upapajjatha    
jetuttaramhi nagare          sañjayena samāgami .

Dasa māse dhārayitvā        karontī pura padakkhiṇaṃ    
vessānaṃ vīthiyā majjhe       janesi phussatī mamaṃ .

Na mayhaṃ mattikaṃ nāmaṃ         napi petikasambhavaṃ    
jātomhi vessavīthiyā        tasmā vessantaro ahuṃ .

Yadāhaṃ dārako homi         jātiyā aṭṭhavassiko    
tadā nisajja pāsāde        dānaṃ dātuṃ vicintayiṃ    
hadayaṃ dadeyyaṃ cakkhuṃ          maṃsampi rudhirampica    
dadeyyaṃ kāyaṃ sāvetvā      yadi koci yācako mamaṃ .

#[^366-1] ma. Khujjacelāpakākiṇṇe .   [^366-2] imā cha gāthāyo sīhaḷapotthakeyeva dissanti  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 367 -->
<a id="page-367"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 367]*

Sabhāvaṃ cintayantassa          akampitamasaṇṭhitaṃ    
akampi tattha paṭhavī           sineruvanavaṭaṃsakā .

[1052] paruḷhakacchanakhalomā          paṅkadantā rajassirā    
paggayha dakkhiṇaṃ bāhuṃ         kiṃ maṃ yācanti brāhmaṇā .

[1053] ratanaṃ deva yācāma          sivīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhanaṃ    
dadāhi pavaraṃ nāgaṃ           īsādantaṃ uruḷhavaṃ .

[1054] dadāmi na vikampāmi          yaṃ maṃ yācanti brāhmaṇā    
pabhinnaṃ kuñjaraṃ dantiṃ          opaguyhaṃ [^367-1] gajuttamaṃ .

[1055] hatthikkhandhato oruyha        rājā cāgādhimānaso    
brāhmaṇānaṃ adā dānaṃ       sivīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhano .

[1056] tadāsi yaṃ bhiṃsanakaṃ            tadāsi lomahaṃsanaṃ    
hatthināge padinnamhi         medanī samakampatha .

Tadāsi yaṃ bhiṃsanakaṃ            tadāsi lomahaṃsanaṃ    
hatthināge padinnamhi         khubbhittha nagaraṃ tadā .

Samākulaṃ puraṃ āsi           ghoso ca vipulo mahā    
hatthināge padinnamhi         sivīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhane .

[1057] athettha [^367-2] vattati saddo     tumulo bheravo mahā    
hatthināge padinnamhi         khubbhittha nagaraṃ tadā .

Athettha vattati saddo        tumulo bheravo mahā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 368 -->
<a id="page-368"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 368]*

Hatthināge padinnamhi         sivīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhane .

[1058] uggā ca rājaputtā ca       vesiyānā ca brāhmaṇā    
hatthārohā anīkaṭṭhā        rathikā pattikārakā    
kevalo cāpi nigamo         siviyo cāpi [^368-1] samāgatā    
disvā nāgaṃ niyyamānaṃ        te rañño paṭivedayuṃ .

Vidhamaṃ deva te raṭṭhaṃ         putto vessantaro tava    
kathaṃ no hatthinaṃ dajjā        nāgaṃ raṭṭhassa pūjitaṃ .

Kathaṃ no kuñjaraṃ dajjā        īsādantaṃ uruḷhavaṃ    
khettaññaña sabbayuddhānaṃ        sabbasetaṃ gajuttamaṃ .

Paṇḍukambalasañchannaṃ           pabhinnaṃ sattumaddanaṃ    
dantiṃ savālavījaniṃ            setaṃ kelāsasādisaṃ .

Sasetacchattaṃ supattheyyaṃ [^368-2]    sāthabbanaṃ sahatthipaṃ    
aggayānaṃ rājavāhiṃ          brāhmaṇānaṃ adā dānaṃ [^368-3] .

[1059] annaṃ pānañca so [^368-4] dajjā   vatthasenāsanāni ca    
etaṃ kho dānapaṭirūpaṃ         etaṃ kho brāhmaṇārahaṃ .

Ayaṃ te vaṃsarājā no        sivīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhano [^368-5]    
kathaṃ vessantaro putto       gajaṃ bhājeti sañjaya .

Sace tvaṃ na karissasi         sivīnaṃ vacanaṃ idaṃ    
maññe taṃ saha puttena        sivihatthe [^368-6] karissare .

#[^368-1] ma. Sivayo ca .     [^368-2] ma. Saüpādheyyaṃ .     [^368-3] ma. Gajaṃ . Sī. Yu. Dhanaṃ .   [^368-4] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 369 -->
<a id="page-369"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 369]*

[1060] kāmaṃ janapado māsi          raṭṭhañcāpi vinassatu    
nāhaṃ sivīnaṃ vacanā           rājaputtaṃ adūsakaṃ    
pabbājeyyaṃ sakā raṭṭhā      putto hi mama oraso .

Kāmaṃ janapado māsi          raṭṭhañcāpi vinassatu    
nāhaṃ sivīnaṃ vacanā           rājaputtaṃ adūsakaṃ    
pabbājeyyaṃ sakā raṭṭhā      putto hi mama atrajo .

Na cāhaṃ tasmiṃ dubbheyyaṃ       ariyasīlavato hi so    
asilokopi me assa         pāpañca pasave bahuṃ    
kathaṃ vessantaraṃ puttaṃ         satthena ghātayāmase .

[1061] mā naṃ daṇḍena satthena       na hi so bandhanāraho    
pabbājehi ca naṃ raṭṭhā       vaṅke vasatu pabbate .

[1062] eso ce sivīnaṃ chando       chandaṃ na panudāmase    
imaṃ so vasatu rattiṃ          kāme ca paribhuñjatu .

Tato ratyā vivasane         suriyassuggamanaṃ pati [^369-1]    
samaggā siviyo [^369-2] hutvā     raṭṭhā pabbājayantu naṃ .

[1063] uṭṭhehi katte taramāno      gantvā vessantaraṃ vada    
siviyo deva te kuddhā       negamā ca samāgatā    
uggā ca rājaputtā ca       vesiyānā ca brāhmaṇā .

Hatthārohā anīkaṭṭhā        rathikā pattikārakā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 370 -->
<a id="page-370"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 370]*

Kevalo cāpi nigamo         siviyo cāpi samāgatā .

Asmā ratyā vivasane        suriyassuggamanaṃ pati    
samaggā siviyo hutvā        raṭṭhā pabbājayanti taṃ .

[1064] sa kattā taramānova         sivirājena pesito    
āmuttahatthābharaṇo          suvattho candanapphosito [^370-1]    
sīsaṃ nhāto udake so       āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalo    
upāgami puraṃ rammaṃ           vessantaranivesanaṃ .

Tatthaddasa kumāraṃ so         ramamānaṃ sake pure    
parikiṇṇaṃ amaccehi           tidasānaṃ va vāsavaṃ .

[1065] so tattha gantvā taramāno    kattā vessantaraṃ bravi    
dukkhante vedayissāmi        mā me kujjha rathesabha .

Vanditvā rodamāno so      kattā rājānamabravi    
bhattā mesi mahārāja        sabbakāmarasāharo    
dukkhante vedayissāmi        tattha assāsayantu maṃ .

Siviyo deva te kuddhā       negamā ca samāgatā    
uggā ca rājaputtā ca       vesiyānā ca brāhmaṇā .

Hatthārohā anīkaṭṭhā        rathikā pattikārakā    
kevalo cāpi nigamo         siviyo cāpi samāgatā .

Asmā ratyā vivasane        suriyassuggamanaṃ pati    
samaggā siviyo hutvā        raṭṭhā pabbājayanti taṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 371 -->
<a id="page-371"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 371]*

[1066] kismiṃ me siviyo kuddhā       yo na passāmi dukkaṭaṃ    
taṃ me katte viyācikkha       kasmā pabbājayanti maṃ .

[1067] uggā ca rājaputtā ca       vesiyānā ca brāhmaṇā    
hatthārohā anīkaṭṭhā        rathikā pattikārakā    
nāgadānena khīyanti          tasmā pabbājayanti taṃ .

[1068] hadayaṃ cakkhumpahaṃ dajjaṃ         kiṃ me bāhirakaṃ dhanaṃ    
hiraññaṃ vā suvaṇṇaṃ vā        muttā veḷuriyā maṇi    
dakkhiṇaṃ vāmahaṃ [^371-1] bāhuṃ       disvā yācakamāgate    
dadeyyaṃ na vikampeyyaṃ        dāne me ramatī [^371-2] mano .

Kāmaṃ maṃ siviyo sabbe        pabbājentu hanantu vā    
neva dānā viramissaṃ         kāmaṃ chindantu sattadhā .

[1069] evantaṃ siviyo āhu         negamā ca samāgatā    
kontimārāya tīrena         girimārañjaraṃ pati    
yena pabbājitā yanti        tena gacchatu subbato .

[1070] sohaṃ tena gamissāmi         yena gacchanti dūsakā    
rattindivaṃ me khamatha          yāva dānaṃ dadāmihaṃ .

[1071] āmantayittha rājā naṃ        maddiṃ sabbaṅgasobhaniṃ    
yaṃ te kiñci mayā dinnaṃ       dhanaṃ dhaññañca vijjati    
hiraññaṃ vā suvaṇṇaṃ vā        muttā veḷuriyā bahū    
sabbantaṃ nidaheyyāsi         yañca te pettikaṃ dhanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 372 -->
<a id="page-372"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 372]*

[1072] tamabravi rājaputtī           maddī sabbaṅgasobhanā    
kuhiṃ deva nidahāmi           taṃ me akkhāhi pucchito .

[1073] sīlavantesu dajjesi          dānaṃ maddi yathārahaṃ    
na hi dānā paraṃ atthi        patiṭṭhā sabbapāṇinaṃ .

[1074] puttesu maddi dayesi         sassuyā sassuramhi ca    
yo ca taṃ bhattā maññeyya     sakkaccantaṃ upaṭṭhahe .

No ce taṃ bhattā maññeyya    mayā vippavasena te    
aññaṃ bhattāraṃ pariyesa        mā kilittha [^372-1] mayā vinā .

[1075] ahaṃ hi vanaṃ gacchāmi          ghoraṃ vāḷamigāyutaṃ    
saṃsayo jīvitaṃ mayhaṃ           ekakassa brahāvane .

[1076] tamabravi rājaputtī           maddī sabbaṅgasobhanā    
abhumme kathaṃ nu bhaṇasi         pāpakaṃ vata bhāsasi .

Nesa dhammo mahārāja        yaṃ tvaṃ gaccheyya ekako    
ahaṃpi tena gacchāmi          yena gacchasi khattiya .

Maraṇaṃ vā tayā saddhiṃ         jīvitaṃ vā tayā vinā    
tadeva maraṇaṃ seyyo         yañce jīve tayā vinā .

Aggiṃ ujjālayitvāna         ekajālasamāhitaṃ    
tattheva maraṇaṃ [^372-2] seyyo     yañce jīve tayā vinā .

Yathā āraññakaṃ nāgaṃ         dantiṃ anveti hatthinī    
jessantaṃ giriduggesu         samesu visamesu ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 373 -->
<a id="page-373"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 373]*

Evantaṃ anugacchāmi          putte ādāya pacchato    
subharā te bhavissāmi         na te hessāmi dubbharā .

[1077] ime kumāre passanto       mañjuke piyabhāṇine    
āsīne vanagumbasmiṃ          na rajjassa sarissasi .

Ime kumāre passanto       mañjuke piyabhāṇine    
kīḷante vanagumbasmiṃ          na rajjassa sarissasi .

Ime kumāre passanto       mañjuke piyabhāṇine    
assame ramaṇīyamhi           na rajjassa sarissasi .

Ime kumāre passanto       mañjuke piyabhāṇine    
kīḷante assame ramme       na rajjassa sarissasi .

Ime kumāre passanto       māladhārī alaṅkate    
assame ramaṇīyamhi           na rajjassa sarissasi .

Ime kumāre passanto       māladhārī alaṅkate    
kīḷante assame ramme       na rajjassa sarissasi .

Yadā dakkhasi naccante        kumāre māladhārine    
assame ramaṇīyamhi           na rajjassa sarissasi .

Yadā dakkhasi naccante        kumāre māladhārine    
kīḷante assame ramme       na rajjassa sarissasi .

Yadā dakkhasi mātaṅgaṃ         kuñjaraṃ saṭṭhihāyanaṃ    
ekaṃ araññe vicarantaṃ [^373-1]     na rajjassa sarissasi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 374 -->
<a id="page-374"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 374]*

Yadā dakkhasi mātaṅgaṃ         kuñjaraṃ saṭṭhihāyanaṃ    
sāyaṃ pāto vicarantaṃ         na rajjassa sarissasi .

Yadā kareṇusaṅghassa          yūthassa purato vajaṃ    
koñcaṃ kāhati mātaṅgo       kuñjaro saṭṭhihāyano    
tassa taṃ nadato sutvā        na rajjassa sarissasi .

Ubhato vanavikāse           yadā dakkhasi kāmadaṃ    
vane vāḷamigākiṇṇe         na rajjassa sarissasi .

Migaṃ disvāna sāyaṇhaṃ         pañcamālinimāgataṃ [^374-1]    
kiṃpurise ca naccante         na rajjassa sarissasi .

Yadā sussasi [^374-2] nigghosaṃ      sandamānāya sindhuyā    
gītaṃ kiṃpurisānañca            na rajjassa sarissasi .

Yadā sussasi [^374-2] nigghosaṃ      girigabbharacārino    
vassamānassulūkassa           na rajjassa sarissasi .

Yadā sīhassa byagghassa        khaggassa gavayassa ca    
vane sussasi [^374-2] vāḷānaṃ      na rajjassa sarissasi .

Yadā morīhi parikiṇṇaṃ         varahinaṃ [^374-3] matthakāsinaṃ    
moraṃ dakkhasi [^374-4] naccantaṃ      na rajjassa sarissasi .

Yadā morīhi parikiṇṇaṃ         aṇḍajaṃ citrapekkhanaṃ [^374-5]    
moraṃ dakkhasi naccantaṃ         na rajjassa sarissasi .

#[^374-1] ma. Pañcamālinamāgataṃ .   [^374-2] ma. Sossasi .   [^374-3] ma. Barihīnaṃ .   [^374-4] ma. Dakkhisi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 375 -->
<a id="page-375"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 375]*

Yadā morīhi parikiṇṇaṃ         nīlagīvaṃ sikhaṇḍinaṃ    
moraṃ dakkhasi naccantaṃ         na rajjassa sarissasi .

Yadā dakkhasi hemante        pupphite dharaṇīruhe    
surabhisampavāyante [^375-1]        na rajjassa sarissasi .

Yadā hemantike māse       haritaṃ dakkhasi medaniṃ    
indagopakasañchannaṃ           na rajjassa sarissasi .

Yadā dakkhasi hemante        pupphite dharaṇīruhe    
kuṭajaṃ bimbajālañca           pupphitaṃ loddapadmakaṃ    
surabhisampavāyante [^375-2]        na rajjassa sarissasi .

Yadā hemantike māse       vanaṃ dakkhasi pupphitaṃ    
opupphāni ca padmāni        na rajjassa sarissasi .

Himavantaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ .

[1078] tesaṃ lālapitaṃ [^375-3] sutvā      puttassa suṇisāya ca    
kalūnaṃ paridevesi            rājaputtī yasassinī .

Seyyo visaṃ me khāyitaṃ       papātā papateyyahaṃ    
rajjuyā bajjhamiyāhaṃ    
kasmā vessantaraṃ puttaṃ       pabbājenti adūsakaṃ .

Ajjhāyakaṃ dānapatiṃ           yācayogaṃ amacchariṃ    
kasmā [^375-4] vessantaraṃ puttaṃ    pabbājenti adūsakaṃ [^375-5] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 376 -->
<a id="page-376"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 376]*

Pūjitaṃ paṭirājūhi             kittimantaṃ yasassinaṃ    
kasmā vessantaraṃ puttaṃ       pabbājenti adūsakaṃ .

Mātāpetibharaṃ jantuṃ          kule jeṭṭhāpacāyinaṃ    
kasmā vessantaraṃ puttaṃ       pabbājenti adūsakaṃ .

Rañño hitaṃ devahitaṃ [^376-1]       ñātīnaṃ sakhinaṃ hitaṃ    
hitaṃ sabbassa raṭṭhassa    
kasmā vessantaraṃ puttaṃ       pabbājenti adūsakaṃ .

[1079] madhūnīva palitāni [^376-2]          ambāva patitā chamā    
evaṃ hessati te raṭṭhaṃ       pabbājenti adūsakaṃ .

Haṃso nikkhīṇapattova          pallalasmiṃ anūdake    
apaviṭṭho amaccehi          eko rājā vihiyyasi .

Tantaṃ brūmi mahārāja         attho te mā upaccagā    
mā naṃ sivīnaṃ vacanā          pabbājesi adūsakaṃ .

[1080] dhammassāpacitiṃ kummiṃ [^376-3]       sivīnaṃ vinayaṃ dhajaṃ    
pabbājemi sakaṃ puttaṃ         pāṇā piyataro hi me .

[1081] yassa pubbe dhajaggāni        kaṇikārāva pupphitā    
yāyantamanuyāyanti           svajjekova gamissati .

Yassa pubbe dhajaggāni        kaṇikāravanāniva    
yāyantamanuyāyanti           svajjekova gamissati .

Yassa pubbe anīkāni         kaṇikārāva pupphitā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 377 -->
<a id="page-377"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 377]*

Yāyantamanuyāyanti           svajjekova gamissati .

Yassa pubbe anīkāni         kaṇikāravanāni va    
yāyantamanuyāyanti           svajjekova gamissati .

Indagopakavaṇṇābhā          gandhārā paṇḍukambalā    
yāyantamanuyāyanti           svajjekova gamissati .

Yo pubbe hatthinā yāti      sivikāya rathena ca    
svajja vessantaro rājā     kathaṃ gacchati pattiko .

Kathaṃ candanalittaṅgo          naccagītappabodhano    
khurājinaṃ pharasuñca            khārikājañca hāriti [^377-1] .

Kasmā nābhiharīyanti          kāsāvā ajināni ca    
pavisantaṃ brahāraññaṃ          kasmā cīraṃ na bajjhare .

Kathaṃ nu cīraṃ dhārenti         rājapabbajitā janā    
kathaṃ kusamayaṃ cīraṃ             maddī paridahessati [^377-2] .

Kāsiyāni ca dhāretvā       khomakodumbarāni ca    
kusacīrāni dhārentī          kathaṃ maddī karissati .

Vayhāhi pariyāyitvā         sivikāya rathena ca    
sā kathajja anuccaṅgī         pathaṃ gacchati pattikā .

Yassā mudutalā hatthā        calanā [^377-3] ca sukhe ṭhitā    
sā kathajja anuccaṅgī         pathaṃ gacchati pattikā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 378 -->
<a id="page-378"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 378]*

Yassā mudutalā pādā        calanā [^378-1] ca sukhe ṭhitā    
pādukāhi suvaṇṇāhi          pīḷamānāva gacchati    
sā kathajja anuccaṅgī         pathaṃ gacchati pattikā .

Yassu itthīsahassassa [^378-2]       purato gacchati mālinī    
sā kathajja anuccaṅgī         vanaṃ gacchati ekikā .

Yā sā [^378-3] sivāya sutvāna    muhuṃ uttasate pure    
sā kathajja anuccaṅgī         vanaṃ gacchati bhīrukā .

Yā sā [^378-3] indasagottassa    ulūkassa pavassato    
sutvāna nadato bhītā         vāruṇīva pavedhati    
sā kathajja anuccaṅgī         vanaṃ gacchati bhīrukā .

Sakuṇī hataputtāva            suññaṃ disvā kulāvakaṃ    
ciraṃ dukkhena jhāyissaṃ         suññaṃ āgammimaṃ puraṃ .

Sakuṇī hataputtāva            suññaṃ disvā kulāvakaṃ    
kīsā paṇḍu bhavissāmi         piye putte apassatī .

Sakuṇī hataputtāva            suññaṃ disvā kulāvakaṃ    
tena tena padhāvissaṃ         piye putte apassatī .

Kururī [^378-4] hatacchāpāva        suññaṃ disvā kulāvakaṃ    
ciraṃ dukkhena jhāyissaṃ         suññaṃ āgammimaṃ puraṃ .

Kururī hatacchāpāva           suññaṃ disvā kulāvakaṃ    
kīsā paṇḍu bhavissāmi         piye putte apassatī .

#[^378-1] sī. Ma. Caraṇā .   [^378-2] ma. Yāssu itthisahassānaṃ .   [^378-3] ma. Yāssu .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 379 -->
<a id="page-379"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 379]*

Kururī hatacchāpāva           suññaṃ disvā kulāvakaṃ    
tena tena padhāvissaṃ         piye putte apassatī .

Sā nūna cakkavakkīva [^379-1]       pallalasmiṃ anūdake    
ciraṃ dukkhena jhāyissaṃ         suññaṃ āgammimaṃ puraṃ .

Sā nūna cakkavakkīva          pallalasmiṃ anūdake    
kīsā paṇḍu bhavissāmi         piye putte apassatī .

Sā nūna cakkavakkīva          pallalasmiṃ anūdake    
tena tena padhāvissaṃ         piye putte apassatī .

Evaṃ me vilapantiyā         rājaputtaṃ adūsakaṃ    
pabbājesi ca [^379-2] naṃ raṭṭhā    maññe hessāmi jīvitaṃ .

[1082] tassā lālapitaṃ sutvā        sabbā antepure ahu [^379-3]    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       sivikaññā samāgatā .

Sālāva sampamadditā [^379-4]      mālutena pamadditā    
senti puttā ca dārā ca     vessantaranivesane .

Orodhā ca kumārā ca       vesiyānā ca brāhmaṇā    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       vessantaranivesane .

Hatthārohā anīkaṭṭhā        rathikā pattikārakā    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       vessantaranivesane .

Tato ratyā vivasane         suriyassuggamanaṃ pati    
atha vessantaro rājā       dānaṃ dātuṃ upāgami .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 380 -->
<a id="page-380"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 380]*

Vatthāni vatthakāmānaṃ         soṇḍānaṃ detha vāruṇiṃ    
bhojanaṃ bhojanatthīnaṃ           sammadeva pavecchatha .

Mā ca kiñci vanibbake        heṭhayittha idhāgate    
tappetha annapānena         gacchantu paṭipūjitā .

(athettha vattati saddo       tumulo bheravo mahā    
dānena taṃ nīharanti          puna dānaṃ adā tuvaṃ [^380-1] .)    
[1083] te su mattā kilantāva       sampatanti vanibbakā    
nikkhamante mahārāje        sivīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhane .

Acchecchacha vata bho rukkhaṃ       nānāphaladadaṃ dumaṃ    
yathā vessantaraṃ raṭṭhā       pabbājenti adūsakaṃ .

Acchecchacha vata bho rukkhaṃ       nānāphaladharaṃ dumaṃ    
yathā vessantaraṃ raṭṭhā       pabbājenti adūsakaṃ .

Acchecchacha vata bho rukkhaṃ       sabbakāmadadaṃ dumaṃ    
yathā vessantaraṃ raṭṭhā       pabbājenti adūsakaṃ .

Acchecchacha vata bho rukkhaṃ       sabbakāmarasāharaṃ    
yathā vessantaraṃ raṭṭhā       pabbājenti adūsakaṃ .

Ye vuḍḍhā ye ca daharā      ye ca majjhimaporisā    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ    
nikkhamante mahārāje        sivīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhane .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 381 -->
<a id="page-381"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 381]*

Atiyakkhā vessavarā [^381-1]      itthāgārā ca rājino    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ    
nikkhamante mahārāje        sivīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhane .

Thiyopi tattha pakkanduṃ         yā tamhi nagare ahu    
nikkhamante mahārāje        sivīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhane .

Ye brāhmaṇā ye ca samaṇā   aññe vāpi vanibbakā    
bāhā paggayha pakkanduṃ       adhammo kira bho iti .

Yathā vessantaro rājā      yajamāno sake pure    
sivīnaṃ vacanatthena            samhā raṭṭhā nirajjati .

Satta hatthisate datvā        sabbālaṅkārabhūsite    
suvaṇṇakacche mātaṅge        hemakappanivāsase [^381-2]    
āruḷhe gāmaṇīyebhi         tomaraṅkusapāṇibhi    
esa vessantaro rājā      samhā raṭṭhā nirajjati .

Satta assasate datvā        sabbālaṅkārabhūsite    
ājāniyeva jātiyā         sindhave sīghavāhane    
āruḷhe gāmaṇīyebhi         indiyācāpadhāribhi [^381-3]    
esa vessantaro rājā      samhā raṭṭhā nirajjati .

Satta rathasate datvā         sannaddhe ussitaddhaje  
dīpe athopi veyyagghe       sabbālaṅkārabhūsite  
āruḷhe gāmaṇīyebhi         cāpahatthehi cammibhi 4  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 382 -->
<a id="page-382"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 382]*

Esa vessantaro rājā      samhā raṭṭhā nirajjati .

Satta itthīsate datvā        ekamekā rathe ṭhitā    
sannaddhā nikkharajjūhi         suvaṇṇehi alaṅkatā    
pītālaṅkārā pītavasanā       pītābharaṇabhūsitā [^382-1]    
āḷārappamukhā [^382-2] hasulā     susaññā tanumajjhimā    
esa vessantaro rājā      samhā raṭṭhā nirajjati .

Satta dhenusate datvā        sabbā kaṃsupadhārino [^382-3]    
esa vessantaro rājā      samhā raṭṭhā nirajjati .

Satta dāsīsate datvā        satta dāsasatāni ca    
esa vessantaro rājā      samhā raṭṭhā nirajjati .

Hatthī asse rathe datvā      nāriyo ca alaṅkatā    
esa vessantaro rājā      samhā raṭṭhā nirajjati .

Tadāsi yaṃ bhiṃsanakaṃ            tadāsi lomahaṃsanaṃ    
mahādāne padinnamhi         medanī samakampatha .

Tadāsi yaṃ bhiṃsanakaṃ            tadāsi lomahaṃsanaṃ    
yaṃ pañjalīkato rājā         samhā raṭṭhā nirajjati .

[1084] athettha vattati saddo        tumulo bheravo mahā    
dānena taṃ nīharanti          puna dānaṃ dadāti so [^382-4] .

#[^382-1] ma. -vibhūsitā .  [^382-2] ma. Āḷārapamhā . Pamhanti akkhigaṇḍo .   [^382-3] ma. Kaṃspadhāraṇā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 383 -->
<a id="page-383"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 383]*

[1085] te su mattā kilantāva       sampatanti vanibbakā    
nikkhamante mahārāje        sivīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhane .

[1086] āmantayittha rājānaṃ         sañjayaṃ dhammikaṃ [^383-1] varaṃ    
avaruddhasi maṃ deva           vaṅkaṃ gacchāmi pabbataṃ .

Ye hi keci mahārāja        bhūtā ye ca bhavissare    
atittāyeva kāmehi         gacchanti yamasādhanaṃ .

Sohaṃ sake abhisasiṃ           yajamāno sake pure    
sivīnaṃ vacanatthena            samhā raṭṭhā nirajjahaṃ    
aghantaṃ paṭisevissaṃ    
vane vāḷamigākiṇṇe         khaggadīpinisevite    
ahaṃ puññāni karomi          tumhe paṅkamhi sīdatha .

[1087] anujānāhi maṃ amma          pabbajjā mama ruccati    
sohaṃ sake abhisasiṃ           yajamāno sake pure    
sivīnaṃ vacanatthena            samhā raṭṭhā nirajjahaṃ    
aghantaṃ paṭisevissaṃ    
vane vāḷamigākiṇṇe         khaggadīpinisevite    
ahaṃ puññāni karomi          vaṅkaṃ gacchāmi pabbataṃ [^383-2] .

[1088] anujānāmi taṃ putta          pabbajjā te samijjhatu    
ayañca maddī kalyāṇī         susaññā tanumajjhimā    
acchataṃ saha puttehi          kiṃ araññe karissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 384 -->
<a id="page-384"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 384]*

[1089] nāhaṃ akāmā dāsiṃpi         araññaṃ netumussahe
